
# ========================================================
# CMA-ES Variant: Fixed baseline CMA-ES | Cycle 0
# ========================================================

## Metric:
Values represent error to the BBOB optimum. Lower is better.

## Algorithm Code:
```python

import numpy as np
import ioh


class CMAES:
    '''
    Baseline CMA-ES.
    '''
    def __init__(self,
                 pop_size: int = None,
                 num_iterations: int = 1000,
                 sigma: float = 0.5):

        self.pop_size = pop_size
        self.num_iterations = num_iterations
        self.sigma = sigma

    def __call__(self, problem: ioh.problem.RealSingleObjective):

        dim = problem.meta_data.n_variables
        low_b, high_b = problem.bounds.lb[0], problem.bounds.ub[0]

        if self.pop_size is None:
            lambda_ = 4 + int(3 * np.log(dim))
        else:
            lambda_ = self.pop_size

        mu = lambda_ // 2

        weights = np.log(mu + 0.5) - np.log(np.arange(1, mu + 1))
        weights = weights / np.sum(weights)

        mu_eff = 1.0 / np.sum(weights ** 2)

        c_sigma = (mu_eff + 2) / (dim + mu_eff + 5)

        d_sigma = (
            1
            + 2 * max(0, np.sqrt((mu_eff - 1) / (dim + 1)) - 1)
            + c_sigma
        )

        c_c = (4 + mu_eff / dim) / (dim + 4 + 2 * mu_eff / dim)

        c1 = 2 / ((dim + 1.3) ** 2 + mu_eff)

        c_mu = min(
            1 - c1,
            2 * (mu_eff - 2 + 1 / mu_eff) / ((dim + 2) ** 2 + mu_eff)
        )

        expected_norm = np.sqrt(dim) * (
            1 - 1 / (4 * dim) + 1 / (21 * dim ** 2)
        )

        mean = np.random.uniform(low_b, high_b, dim)
        sigma = self.sigma * (high_b - low_b)

        C = np.eye(dim)

        p_sigma = np.zeros(dim)
        p_c = np.zeros(dim)

        best_solution = None
        best_fitness = np.inf

        history = {
            "best_fitness": [],
            "sigma": [],
            "mean": [],
            "stagnation_counter": 0,
            "restart_count": 0
        }

        for iteration in range(self.num_iterations):

            # Numerical safeguard for covariance matrix
            C = (C + C.T) / 2

            if not np.all(np.isfinite(C)):
                C = np.eye(dim)

            C = C + 1e-12 * np.eye(dim)

            eigenvalues, B = np.linalg.eigh(C)
            eigenvalues = np.maximum(eigenvalues, 1e-20)

            D = np.sqrt(eigenvalues)
            BD = B @ np.diag(D)

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 1: Sampling
            # ========================================================

            z = np.random.randn(lambda_, dim)

            population = mean + sigma * (z @ BD.T)

            # Numerical safeguard for candidate solutions
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(population)):
                population = np.nan_to_num(
                    population,
                    nan=0.0,
                    posinf=high_b,
                    neginf=low_b
                )

            population = np.clip(population, low_b, high_b)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 1
            # ========================================================

            fitness = np.array([problem(ind) for ind in population])

            sorted_indices = np.argsort(fitness)

            population = population[sorted_indices]
            z = z[sorted_indices]
            fitness = fitness[sorted_indices]

            previous_best_fitness = best_fitness

            if fitness[0] < best_fitness:
                best_fitness = fitness[0]
                best_solution = population[0].copy()
                history["stagnation_counter"] = 0
            else:
                history["stagnation_counter"] += 1

            selected_population = population[:mu]
            selected_z = z[:mu]

            old_mean = mean.copy()

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 2: Mean update
            # ========================================================

            mean = np.sum(
                weights[:, np.newaxis] * selected_population,
                axis=0
            )

            mean = np.clip(mean, low_b, high_b)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 2
            # ========================================================

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 3: Adaptation
            # ========================================================

            C_inv_sqrt = B @ np.diag(1 / D) @ B.T

            p_sigma = (
                (1 - c_sigma) * p_sigma
                + np.sqrt(c_sigma * (2 - c_sigma) * mu_eff)
                * (C_inv_sqrt @ ((mean - old_mean) / sigma))
            )

            sigma_exponent = (
                (c_sigma / d_sigma)
                * (np.linalg.norm(p_sigma) / expected_norm - 1)
            )

            # Prevent overflow in the exponential step-size update
            sigma_exponent = np.clip(
                sigma_exponent,
                -50.0,
                50.0
            )

            sigma = sigma * np.exp(sigma_exponent)

            # Recover from numerical instability if sigma becomes NaN or Inf
            if not np.isfinite(sigma):
                sigma = self.sigma * (high_b - low_b)

            sigma = np.clip(
                sigma,
                1e-12,
                1e3 * (high_b - low_b)
            )

            norm_p_sigma = np.linalg.norm(p_sigma)

            h_sigma_condition = norm_p_sigma / np.sqrt(
                1 - (1 - c_sigma) ** (2 * (iteration + 1))
            ) < (1.4 + 2 / (dim + 1)) * expected_norm

            h_sigma = 1.0 if h_sigma_condition else 0.0

            p_c = (
                (1 - c_c) * p_c
                + h_sigma
                * np.sqrt(c_c * (2 - c_c) * mu_eff)
                * ((mean - old_mean) / sigma)
            )

            y = (selected_population - old_mean) / sigma

            rank_mu_update = np.zeros((dim, dim))

            for i in range(mu):
                rank_mu_update += weights[i] * np.outer(y[i], y[i])

            C = (
                (1 - c1 - c_mu) * C
                + c1 * np.outer(p_c, p_c)
                + c_mu * rank_mu_update
            )

            C = (C + C.T) / 2

            # Final covariance safeguard
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(C)):
                C = np.eye(dim)

            C = C + 1e-12 * np.eye(dim)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 3
            # ========================================================

            history["best_fitness"].append(best_fitness)
            history["sigma"].append(sigma)
            history["mean"].append(mean.copy())

        return best_solution, best_fitness

```

# ========================================================
# CMA-ES Variant: LLM-generated CMA-ES variant cycle 1 | Cycle 1
# ========================================================

## Metric:
Values represent error to the BBOB optimum. Lower is better.

## Algorithm Code:
```python
import numpy as np
import ioh


class LLM_CMAES:
    '''
    CMA-ES variant that injects a small fraction of heavy-tailed Student-t samples
    into the Gaussian offspring each generation.
    '''
    def __init__(
            self,
            pop_size=20,
            num_iterations=100,
            sigma=0.5
        ):

        self.pop_size = pop_size
        self.num_iterations = num_iterations
        self.sigma = sigma

        # Hardcoded mixture parameters per EXPLAINABILITY_REPORT
        self._alpha = 0.10  # fraction of offspring to replace with Student-t
        self._nu = 3        # degrees of freedom for Student-t

        self.variant_description = {
            "sampling": (
                "Modified: Each generation, a small fixed fraction alpha="
                "0.10 of offspring are replaced by independent multivariate "
                "Student-t(nu=3) draws in the current covariance basis. "
                "These heavy-tailed z_t are transformed by BD and scaled by "
                "sigma: x = mean + sigma * (z_t @ BD.T). The remaining "
                "offspring are sampled as standard Gaussians. Injected "
                "offspring are clipped to bounds and evaluated alongside "
                "the rest; selection and all adaptation rules remain unchanged."
            ),
            "mean_update": "Unchanged from parent algorithm.",
            "adaptation": "Unchanged from parent algorithm."
        }

    def __call__(self, problem: ioh.problem.RealSingleObjective):

        dim = problem.meta_data.n_variables
        low_b, high_b = problem.bounds.lb[0], problem.bounds.ub[0]

        if self.pop_size is None:
            lambda_ = 4 + int(3 * np.log(dim))
        else:
            lambda_ = self.pop_size

        mu = lambda_ // 2

        weights = np.log(mu + 0.5) - np.log(np.arange(1, mu + 1))
        weights = weights / np.sum(weights)

        mu_eff = 1.0 / np.sum(weights ** 2)

        c_sigma = (mu_eff + 2) / (dim + mu_eff + 5)

        d_sigma = (
            1
            + 2 * max(0, np.sqrt((mu_eff - 1) / (dim + 1)) - 1)
            + c_sigma
        )

        c_c = (4 + mu_eff / dim) / (dim + 4 + 2 * mu_eff / dim)

        c1 = 2 / ((dim + 1.3) ** 2 + mu_eff)

        c_mu = min(
            1 - c1,
            2 * (mu_eff - 2 + 1 / mu_eff) / ((dim + 2) ** 2 + mu_eff)
        )

        expected_norm = np.sqrt(dim) * (
            1 - 1 / (4 * dim) + 1 / (21 * dim ** 2)
        )

        mean = np.random.uniform(low_b, high_b, dim)
        sigma = self.sigma * (high_b - low_b)

        C = np.eye(dim)

        p_sigma = np.zeros(dim)
        p_c = np.zeros(dim)

        best_solution = None
        best_fitness = np.inf

        history = {
            "best_fitness": [],
            "sigma": [],
            "mean": [],
            "stagnation_counter": 0,
            "restart_count": 0
        }

        for iteration in range(self.num_iterations):

            # Numerical safeguard for covariance matrix
            C = (C + C.T) / 2

            if not np.all(np.isfinite(C)):
                C = np.eye(dim)

            C = C + 1e-12 * np.eye(dim)

            eigenvalues, B = np.linalg.eigh(C)
            eigenvalues = np.maximum(eigenvalues, 1e-20)

            D = np.sqrt(eigenvalues)
            BD = B @ np.diag(D)

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 1: Sampling (modified)
            # ========================================================

            # Standard Gaussian draws in isotropic coordinates
            z = np.random.randn(lambda_, dim)

            # Inject a small fraction alpha of Student-t heavy-tailed draws
            if self._alpha > 0.0:
                n_heavy = int(np.ceil(self._alpha * lambda_))
                n_heavy = min(max(n_heavy, 0), lambda_)
                if n_heavy > 0:
                    heavy_idx = np.random.choice(lambda_, size=n_heavy, replace=False)
                    # Student-t draws with nu degrees of freedom, independent per coordinate
                    z_t = np.random.standard_t(self._nu, size=(n_heavy, dim))
                    # Replace selected rows in z with Student-t samples
                    z[heavy_idx] = z_t

            # Transform into solution space using current covariance eigenbasis and scale
            population = mean + sigma * (z @ BD.T)

            # Numerical safeguard for candidate solutions
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(population)):
                population = np.nan_to_num(
                    population,
                    nan=0.0,
                    posinf=high_b,
                    neginf=low_b
                )

            population = np.clip(population, low_b, high_b)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 1
            # ========================================================

            fitness = np.array([problem(ind) for ind in population])

            sorted_indices = np.argsort(fitness)

            population = population[sorted_indices]
            z = z[sorted_indices]
            fitness = fitness[sorted_indices]

            previous_best_fitness = best_fitness

            if fitness[0] < best_fitness:
                best_fitness = fitness[0]
                best_solution = population[0].copy()
                history["stagnation_counter"] = 0
            else:
                history["stagnation_counter"] += 1

            selected_population = population[:mu]
            selected_z = z[:mu]

            old_mean = mean.copy()

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 2: Mean update
            # ========================================================

            mean = np.sum(
                weights[:, np.newaxis] * selected_population,
                axis=0
            )

            mean = np.clip(mean, low_b, high_b)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 2
            # ========================================================

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 3: Adaptation
            # ========================================================

            C_inv_sqrt = B @ np.diag(1 / D) @ B.T

            p_sigma = (
                (1 - c_sigma) * p_sigma
                + np.sqrt(c_sigma * (2 - c_sigma) * mu_eff)
                * (C_inv_sqrt @ ((mean - old_mean) / sigma))
            )

            sigma_exponent = (
                (c_sigma / d_sigma)
                * (np.linalg.norm(p_sigma) / expected_norm - 1)
            )

            # Prevent overflow in the exponential step-size update
            sigma_exponent = np.clip(
                sigma_exponent,
                -50.0,
                50.0
            )

            sigma = sigma * np.exp(sigma_exponent)

            # Recover from numerical instability if sigma becomes NaN or Inf
            if not np.isfinite(sigma):
                sigma = self.sigma * (high_b - low_b)

            sigma = np.clip(
                sigma,
                1e-12,
                1e3 * (high_b - low_b)
            )

            norm_p_sigma = np.linalg.norm(p_sigma)

            h_sigma_condition = norm_p_sigma / np.sqrt(
                1 - (1 - c_sigma) ** (2 * (iteration + 1))
            ) < (1.4 + 2 / (dim + 1)) * expected_norm

            h_sigma = 1.0 if h_sigma_condition else 0.0

            p_c = (
                (1 - c_c) * p_c
                + h_sigma
                * np.sqrt(c_c * (2 - c_c) * mu_eff)
                * ((mean - old_mean) / sigma)
            )

            y = (selected_population - old_mean) / sigma

            rank_mu_update = np.zeros((dim, dim))

            for i in range(mu):
                rank_mu_update += weights[i] * np.outer(y[i], y[i])

            C = (
                (1 - c1 - c_mu) * C
                + c1 * np.outer(p_c, p_c)
                + c_mu * rank_mu_update
            )

            C = (C + C.T) / 2

            # Final covariance safeguard
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(C)):
                C = np.eye(dim)

            C = C + 1e-12 * np.eye(dim)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 3
            # ========================================================

            history["best_fitness"].append(best_fitness)
            history["sigma"].append(sigma)
            history["mean"].append(mean.copy())

        return best_solution, best_fitness
```

# CMA-ES Variant: LLM-generated CMA-ES variant cycle 2 | Cycle 2
# ========================================================

## Metric:
Values represent error to the BBOB optimum. Lower is better.

## Algorithm Code:
```python
import numpy as np
import ioh


class LLM_CMAES:
    '''
    CMA-ES variant that applies a radial heavy-tailed multiplier to a small
    fraction of Gaussian offspring directions each generation.
    '''
    def __init__(
            self,
            pop_size=20,
            num_iterations=100,
            sigma=0.5
        ):

        self.pop_size = pop_size
        self.num_iterations = num_iterations
        self.sigma = sigma

        # Hardcoded mixture parameters per EXPLAINABILITY_REPORT
        self._alpha = 0.10  # fraction of offspring to scale with heavy-tailed multiplier
        self._nu = 3        # degrees of freedom for Student-t multiplier

        self.variant_description = {
            "sampling": (
                "Modified: Each generation, a small fixed fraction alpha="
                "0.10 of offspring have their Gaussian direction z_i multiplied "
                "by a univariate heavy-tailed scalar r drawn from Student-t(nu=3). "
                "Concretely: sample z_i ~ N(0,I) for all offspring; for selected "
                "offspring indices, draw r ~ |Student-t(nu)| and set z_i <- r * z_i. "
                "These scaled directions are transformed by BD and scaled by "
                "sigma: x = mean + sigma * (z @ BD.T). The remaining offspring "
                "are standard Gaussian samples. Injected offspring are clipped to "
                "bounds and evaluated alongside the rest; selection and all "
                "adaptation rules remain unchanged."
            ),
            "mean_update": "Unchanged from parent algorithm.",
            "adaptation": "Unchanged from parent algorithm."
        }

    def __call__(self, problem: ioh.problem.RealSingleObjective):

        dim = problem.meta_data.n_variables
        low_b, high_b = problem.bounds.lb[0], problem.bounds.ub[0]

        if self.pop_size is None:
            lambda_ = 4 + int(3 * np.log(dim))
        else:
            lambda_ = self.pop_size

        mu = lambda_ // 2

        weights = np.log(mu + 0.5) - np.log(np.arange(1, mu + 1))
        weights = weights / np.sum(weights)

        mu_eff = 1.0 / np.sum(weights ** 2)

        c_sigma = (mu_eff + 2) / (dim + mu_eff + 5)

        d_sigma = (
            1
            + 2 * max(0, np.sqrt((mu_eff - 1) / (dim + 1)) - 1)
            + c_sigma
        )

        c_c = (4 + mu_eff / dim) / (dim + 4 + 2 * mu_eff / dim)

        c1 = 2 / ((dim + 1.3) ** 2 + mu_eff)

        c_mu = min(
            1 - c1,
            2 * (mu_eff - 2 + 1 / mu_eff) / ((dim + 2) ** 2 + mu_eff)
        )

        expected_norm = np.sqrt(dim) * (
            1 - 1 / (4 * dim) + 1 / (21 * dim ** 2)
        )

        mean = np.random.uniform(low_b, high_b, dim)
        sigma = self.sigma * (high_b - low_b)

        C = np.eye(dim)

        p_sigma = np.zeros(dim)
        p_c = np.zeros(dim)

        best_solution = None
        best_fitness = np.inf

        history = {
            "best_fitness": [],
            "sigma": [],
            "mean": [],
            "stagnation_counter": 0,
            "restart_count": 0
        }

        for iteration in range(self.num_iterations):

            # Numerical safeguard for covariance matrix
            C = (C + C.T) / 2

            if not np.all(np.isfinite(C)):
                C = np.eye(dim)

            C = C + 1e-12 * np.eye(dim)

            eigenvalues, B = np.linalg.eigh(C)
            eigenvalues = np.maximum(eigenvalues, 1e-20)

            D = np.sqrt(eigenvalues)
            BD = B @ np.diag(D)

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 1: Sampling (modified)
            # ========================================================

            # Standard Gaussian draws in isotropic coordinates
            z = np.random.randn(lambda_, dim)

            # Apply a radial heavy-tailed multiplier to a small fraction alpha of offspring
            if self._alpha > 0.0:
                n_heavy = int(np.ceil(self._alpha * lambda_))
                n_heavy = min(max(n_heavy, 0), lambda_)
                if n_heavy > 0:
                    heavy_idx = np.random.choice(lambda_, size=n_heavy, replace=False)
                    # Draw univariate Student-t multipliers and take absolute value
                    r = np.abs(np.random.standard_t(self._nu, size=n_heavy))
                    # Multiply selected Gaussian directions by scalar multipliers (preserve direction)
                    z[heavy_idx] = z[heavy_idx] * r[:, np.newaxis]

            # Transform into solution space using current covariance eigenbasis and scale
            population = mean + sigma * (z @ BD.T)

            # Numerical safeguard for candidate solutions
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(population)):
                population = np.nan_to_num(
                    population,
                    nan=0.0,
                    posinf=high_b,
                    neginf=low_b
                )

            population = np.clip(population, low_b, high_b)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 1
            # ========================================================

            fitness = np.array([problem(ind) for ind in population])

            sorted_indices = np.argsort(fitness)

            population = population[sorted_indices]
            z = z[sorted_indices]
            fitness = fitness[sorted_indices]

            previous_best_fitness = best_fitness

            if fitness[0] < best_fitness:
                best_fitness = fitness[0]
                best_solution = population[0].copy()
                history["stagnation_counter"] = 0
            else:
                history["stagnation_counter"] += 1

            selected_population = population[:mu]
            selected_z = z[:mu]

            old_mean = mean.copy()

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 2: Mean update
            # ========================================================

            mean = np.sum(
                weights[:, np.newaxis] * selected_population,
                axis=0
            )

            mean = np.clip(mean, low_b, high_b)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 2
            # ========================================================

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 3: Adaptation
            # ========================================================

            C_inv_sqrt = B @ np.diag(1 / D) @ B.T

            p_sigma = (
                (1 - c_sigma) * p_sigma
                + np.sqrt(c_sigma * (2 - c_sigma) * mu_eff)
                * (C_inv_sqrt @ ((mean - old_mean) / sigma))
            )

            sigma_exponent = (
                (c_sigma / d_sigma)
                * (np.linalg.norm(p_sigma) / expected_norm - 1)
            )

            # Prevent overflow in the exponential step-size update
            sigma_exponent = np.clip(
                sigma_exponent,
                -50.0,
                50.0
            )

            sigma = sigma * np.exp(sigma_exponent)

            # Recover from numerical instability if sigma becomes NaN or Inf
            if not np.isfinite(sigma):
                sigma = self.sigma * (high_b - low_b)

            sigma = np.clip(
                sigma,
                1e-12,
                1e3 * (high_b - low_b)
            )

            norm_p_sigma = np.linalg.norm(p_sigma)

            h_sigma_condition = norm_p_sigma / np.sqrt(
                1 - (1 - c_sigma) ** (2 * (iteration + 1))
            ) < (1.4 + 2 / (dim + 1)) * expected_norm

            h_sigma = 1.0 if h_sigma_condition else 0.0

            p_c = (
                (1 - c_c) * p_c
                + h_sigma
                * np.sqrt(c_c * (2 - c_c) * mu_eff)
                * ((mean - old_mean) / sigma)
            )

            y = (selected_population - old_mean) / sigma

            rank_mu_update = np.zeros((dim, dim))

            for i in range(mu):
                rank_mu_update += weights[i] * np.outer(y[i], y[i])

            C = (
                (1 - c1 - c_mu) * C
                + c1 * np.outer(p_c, p_c)
                + c_mu * rank_mu_update
            )

            C = (C + C.T) / 2

            # Final covariance safeguard
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(C)):
                C = np.eye(dim)

            C = C + 1e-12 * np.eye(dim)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 3
            # ========================================================

            history["best_fitness"].append(best_fitness)
            history["sigma"].append(sigma)
            history["mean"].append(mean.copy())

        return best_solution, best_fitness
```

# ========================================================
# CMA-ES Variant: LLM-generated CMA-ES variant cycle 3 | Cycle 3
# ========================================================

## Metric:
Values represent error to the BBOB optimum. Lower is better.

## Algorithm Code:
```python
import numpy as np
import ioh


class LLM_CMAES:
    '''
    CMA-ES variant with covariance shrinkage regularization.
    '''
    def __init__(
            self,
            pop_size=20,
            num_iterations=100,
            sigma=0.5
        ):

        self.pop_size = pop_size
        self.num_iterations = num_iterations
        self.sigma = sigma

        # Hardcoded mixture / regularization parameters per EXPLAINABILITY_REPORT
        self._alpha = 0.10  # fraction of offspring to replace with Student-t
        self._nu = 3        # degrees of freedom for Student-t
        self._beta = 0.01   # shrinkage intensity for covariance regularization

        self.variant_description = {
            "sampling": "Unchanged from parent algorithm.",
            "mean_update": "Unchanged from parent algorithm.",
            "adaptation": (
                "Modified: After the standard rank-mu and p_c updates form the "
                "new covariance matrix C each generation, apply a small shrinkage "
                "regularization: C <- (1-beta)*C + beta*(trace(C)/dim)*I with "
                f"beta={self._beta:.3f}. This regularizer nudges C toward isotropy "
                "to improve numerical stability; all other adaptation rules remain unchanged."
            )
        }

    def __call__(self, problem: ioh.problem.RealSingleObjective):

        dim = problem.meta_data.n_variables
        low_b, high_b = problem.bounds.lb[0], problem.bounds.ub[0]

        if self.pop_size is None:
            lambda_ = 4 + int(3 * np.log(dim))
        else:
            lambda_ = self.pop_size

        mu = lambda_ // 2

        weights = np.log(mu + 0.5) - np.log(np.arange(1, mu + 1))
        weights = weights / np.sum(weights)

        mu_eff = 1.0 / np.sum(weights ** 2)

        c_sigma = (mu_eff + 2) / (dim + mu_eff + 5)

        d_sigma = (
            1
            + 2 * max(0, np.sqrt((mu_eff - 1) / (dim + 1)) - 1)
            + c_sigma
        )

        c_c = (4 + mu_eff / dim) / (dim + 4 + 2 * mu_eff / dim)

        c1 = 2 / ((dim + 1.3) ** 2 + mu_eff)

        c_mu = min(
            1 - c1,
            2 * (mu_eff - 2 + 1 / mu_eff) / ((dim + 2) ** 2 + mu_eff)
        )

        expected_norm = np.sqrt(dim) * (
            1 - 1 / (4 * dim) + 1 / (21 * dim ** 2)
        )

        mean = np.random.uniform(low_b, high_b, dim)
        sigma = self.sigma * (high_b - low_b)

        C = np.eye(dim)

        p_sigma = np.zeros(dim)
        p_c = np.zeros(dim)

        best_solution = None
        best_fitness = np.inf

        history = {
            "best_fitness": [],
            "sigma": [],
            "mean": [],
            "stagnation_counter": 0,
            "restart_count": 0
        }

        for iteration in range(self.num_iterations):

            # Numerical safeguard for covariance matrix
            C = (C + C.T) / 2

            if not np.all(np.isfinite(C)):
                C = np.eye(dim)

            C = C + 1e-12 * np.eye(dim)

            # ========================================================
            # Eigen-decomposition for sampling (uses C from previous iteration,
            # where shrinkage was applied at the end of adaptation)
            # ========================================================
            eigenvalues, B = np.linalg.eigh(C)
            eigenvalues = np.maximum(eigenvalues, 1e-20)

            D = np.sqrt(eigenvalues)
            BD = B @ np.diag(D)

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 1: Sampling (unchanged)
            # ========================================================

            # Standard Gaussian draws in isotropic coordinates
            z = np.random.randn(lambda_, dim)

            # Inject a small fraction alpha of Student-t heavy-tailed draws
            if self._alpha > 0.0:
                n_heavy = int(np.ceil(self._alpha * lambda_))
                n_heavy = min(max(n_heavy, 0), lambda_)
                if n_heavy > 0:
                    heavy_idx = np.random.choice(lambda_, size=n_heavy, replace=False)
                    # Student-t draws with nu degrees of freedom, independent per coordinate
                    z_t = np.random.standard_t(self._nu, size=(n_heavy, dim))
                    # Replace selected rows in z with Student-t samples
                    z[heavy_idx] = z_t

            # Transform into solution space using current covariance eigenbasis and scale
            population = mean + sigma * (z @ BD.T)

            # Numerical safeguard for candidate solutions
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(population)):
                population = np.nan_to_num(
                    population,
                    nan=0.0,
                    posinf=high_b,
                    neginf=low_b
                )

            population = np.clip(population, low_b, high_b)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 1
            # ========================================================

            fitness = np.array([problem(ind) for ind in population])

            sorted_indices = np.argsort(fitness)

            population = population[sorted_indices]
            z = z[sorted_indices]
            fitness = fitness[sorted_indices]

            previous_best_fitness = best_fitness

            if fitness[0] < best_fitness:
                best_fitness = fitness[0]
                best_solution = population[0].copy()
                history["stagnation_counter"] = 0
            else:
                history["stagnation_counter"] += 1

            selected_population = population[:mu]
            selected_z = z[:mu]

            old_mean = mean.copy()

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 2: Mean update (unchanged)
            # ========================================================

            mean = np.sum(
                weights[:, np.newaxis] * selected_population,
                axis=0
            )

            mean = np.clip(mean, low_b, high_b)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 2
            # ========================================================

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 3: Adaptation (modified: add shrinkage regularization)
            # ========================================================

            C_inv_sqrt = B @ np.diag(1 / D) @ B.T

            p_sigma = (
                (1 - c_sigma) * p_sigma
                + np.sqrt(c_sigma * (2 - c_sigma) * mu_eff)
                * (C_inv_sqrt @ ((mean - old_mean) / sigma))
            )

            sigma_exponent = (
                (c_sigma / d_sigma)
                * (np.linalg.norm(p_sigma) / expected_norm - 1)
            )

            # Prevent overflow in the exponential step-size update
            sigma_exponent = np.clip(
                sigma_exponent,
                -50.0,
                50.0
            )

            sigma = sigma * np.exp(sigma_exponent)

            # Recover from numerical instability if sigma becomes NaN or Inf
            if not np.isfinite(sigma):
                sigma = self.sigma * (high_b - low_b)

            sigma = np.clip(
                sigma,
                1e-12,
                1e3 * (high_b - low_b)
            )

            norm_p_sigma = np.linalg.norm(p_sigma)

            h_sigma_condition = norm_p_sigma / np.sqrt(
                1 - (1 - c_sigma) ** (2 * (iteration + 1))
            ) < (1.4 + 2 / (dim + 1)) * expected_norm

            h_sigma = 1.0 if h_sigma_condition else 0.0

            p_c = (
                (1 - c_c) * p_c
                + h_sigma
                * np.sqrt(c_c * (2 - c_c) * mu_eff)
                * ((mean - old_mean) / sigma)
            )

            y = (selected_population - old_mean) / sigma

            rank_mu_update = np.zeros((dim, dim))

            for i in range(mu):
                rank_mu_update += weights[i] * np.outer(y[i], y[i])

            C = (
                (1 - c1 - c_mu) * C
                + c1 * np.outer(p_c, p_c)
                + c_mu * rank_mu_update
            )

            C = (C + C.T) / 2

            # Apply shrinkage regularization toward isotropy (Ledoit-Wolf style)
            beta = self._beta
            trace_C = np.trace(C)
            C = (1.0 - beta) * C + beta * (trace_C / dim) * np.eye(dim)

            C = (C + C.T) / 2

            # Final covariance safeguard
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(C)):
                C = np.eye(dim)

            C = C + 1e-12 * np.eye(dim)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 3
            # ========================================================

            history["best_fitness"].append(best_fitness)
            history["sigma"].append(sigma)
            history["mean"].append(mean.copy())

        return best_solution, best_fitness
```

# ========================================================
# CMA-ES Variant: LLM-generated CMA-ES variant cycle 4 | Cycle 4
# ========================================================

## Metric:
Values represent error to the BBOB optimum. Lower is better.

## Algorithm Code:
```python
import numpy as np
import ioh


class LLM_CMAES:
    '''
    CMA-ES variant that injects a small fraction of heavy-tailed Student-t samples
    into the Gaussian offspring each generation and applies a robust median-based
    sigma correction each generation to stabilize step-size adaptation.
    '''
    def __init__(
            self,
            pop_size=20,
            num_iterations=100,
            sigma=0.5
        ):

        self.pop_size = pop_size
        self.num_iterations = num_iterations
        self.sigma = sigma

        # Hardcoded mixture parameters per EXPLAINABILITY_REPORT
        self._alpha = 0.10  # fraction of offspring to replace with Student-t
        self._nu = 3        # degrees of freedom for Student-t

        self.variant_description = {
            "sampling": "Unchanged from parent algorithm.",
            "mean_update": "Unchanged from parent algorithm.",
            "adaptation": (
                "Modified: In addition to the original p_sigma-based step-size "
                "update, apply a small robust median-based sigma correction each "
                "generation. Compute the median radius m_med of the selected "
                "offspring displacements y_i = (selected_population - old_mean) / sigma "
                "(using the current sigma). Compute an approximation of the expected "
                "median of ||N(0,I)|| in dimension dim (Wilson-Hilferty-based). "
                "Apply sigma <- sigma * exp(eta * (m_med / expected_median - 1)) "
                "with eta = 0.1 * c_sigma. This is a conservative, robust correction "
                "that uses only already-selected offspring and no extra evaluations."
            )
        }

    def __call__(self, problem: ioh.problem.RealSingleObjective):

        dim = problem.meta_data.n_variables
        low_b, high_b = problem.bounds.lb[0], problem.bounds.ub[0]

        if self.pop_size is None:
            lambda_ = 4 + int(3 * np.log(dim))
        else:
            lambda_ = self.pop_size

        mu = lambda_ // 2

        weights = np.log(mu + 0.5) - np.log(np.arange(1, mu + 1))
        weights = weights / np.sum(weights)

        mu_eff = 1.0 / np.sum(weights ** 2)

        c_sigma = (mu_eff + 2) / (dim + mu_eff + 5)

        d_sigma = (
            1
            + 2 * max(0, np.sqrt((mu_eff - 1) / (dim + 1)) - 1)
            + c_sigma
        )

        c_c = (4 + mu_eff / dim) / (dim + 4 + 2 * mu_eff / dim)

        c1 = 2 / ((dim + 1.3) ** 2 + mu_eff)

        c_mu = min(
            1 - c1,
            2 * (mu_eff - 2 + 1 / mu_eff) / ((dim + 2) ** 2 + mu_eff)
        )

        expected_norm = np.sqrt(dim) * (
            1 - 1 / (4 * dim) + 1 / (21 * dim ** 2)
        )

        # Approximate expected median of ||N(0,I)|| using Wilson-Hilferty on chi-square median
        # median_chi2 ≈ df * (1 - 2/(9*df))^3, then median_chi = sqrt(median_chi2)
        expected_median = np.sqrt(dim * (1 - 2.0 / (9.0 * dim)) ** 3)
        expected_median = max(expected_median, 1e-12)

        mean = np.random.uniform(low_b, high_b, dim)
        sigma = self.sigma * (high_b - low_b)

        C = np.eye(dim)

        p_sigma = np.zeros(dim)
        p_c = np.zeros(dim)

        best_solution = None
        best_fitness = np.inf

        history = {
            "best_fitness": [],
            "sigma": [],
            "mean": [],
            "stagnation_counter": 0,
            "restart_count": 0
        }

        for iteration in range(self.num_iterations):

            # Numerical safeguard for covariance matrix
            C = (C + C.T) / 2

            if not np.all(np.isfinite(C)):
                C = np.eye(dim)

            C = C + 1e-12 * np.eye(dim)

            eigenvalues, B = np.linalg.eigh(C)
            eigenvalues = np.maximum(eigenvalues, 1e-20)

            D = np.sqrt(eigenvalues)
            BD = B @ np.diag(D)

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 1: Sampling (unchanged from parent; Student-t injection preserved)
            # ========================================================

            # Standard Gaussian draws in isotropic coordinates
            z = np.random.randn(lambda_, dim)

            # Inject a small fraction alpha of Student-t heavy-tailed draws
            if self._alpha > 0.0:
                n_heavy = int(np.ceil(self._alpha * lambda_))
                n_heavy = min(max(n_heavy, 0), lambda_)
                if n_heavy > 0:
                    heavy_idx = np.random.choice(lambda_, size=n_heavy, replace=False)
                    # Student-t draws with nu degrees of freedom, independent per coordinate
                    z_t = np.random.standard_t(self._nu, size=(n_heavy, dim))
                    # Replace selected rows in z with Student-t samples
                    z[heavy_idx] = z_t

            # Transform into solution space using current covariance eigenbasis and scale
            population = mean + sigma * (z @ BD.T)

            # Numerical safeguard for candidate solutions
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(population)):
                population = np.nan_to_num(
                    population,
                    nan=0.0,
                    posinf=high_b,
                    neginf=low_b
                )

            population = np.clip(population, low_b, high_b)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 1
            # ========================================================

            fitness = np.array([problem(ind) for ind in population])

            sorted_indices = np.argsort(fitness)

            population = population[sorted_indices]
            z = z[sorted_indices]
            fitness = fitness[sorted_indices]

            previous_best_fitness = best_fitness

            if fitness[0] < best_fitness:
                best_fitness = fitness[0]
                best_solution = population[0].copy()
                history["stagnation_counter"] = 0
            else:
                history["stagnation_counter"] += 1

            selected_population = population[:mu]
            selected_z = z[:mu]

            old_mean = mean.copy()

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 2: Mean update (unchanged)
            # ========================================================

            mean = np.sum(
                weights[:, np.newaxis] * selected_population,
                axis=0
            )

            mean = np.clip(mean, low_b, high_b)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 2
            # ========================================================

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 3: Adaptation (modified: add median-based sigma correction)
            # ========================================================

            C_inv_sqrt = B @ np.diag(1 / D) @ B.T

            p_sigma = (
                (1 - c_sigma) * p_sigma
                + np.sqrt(c_sigma * (2 - c_sigma) * mu_eff)
                * (C_inv_sqrt @ ((mean - old_mean) / sigma))
            )

            sigma_exponent = (
                (c_sigma / d_sigma)
                * (np.linalg.norm(p_sigma) / expected_norm - 1)
            )

            # Prevent overflow in the exponential step-size update
            sigma_exponent = np.clip(
                sigma_exponent,
                -50.0,
                50.0
            )

            sigma = sigma * np.exp(sigma_exponent)

            # Recover from numerical instability if sigma becomes NaN or Inf
            if not np.isfinite(sigma):
                sigma = self.sigma * (high_b - low_b)

            sigma = np.clip(
                sigma,
                1e-12,
                1e3 * (high_b - low_b)
            )

            norm_p_sigma = np.linalg.norm(p_sigma)

            h_sigma_condition = norm_p_sigma / np.sqrt(
                1 - (1 - c_sigma) ** (2 * (iteration + 1))
            ) < (1.4 + 2 / (dim + 1)) * expected_norm

            h_sigma = 1.0 if h_sigma_condition else 0.0

            p_c = (
                (1 - c_c) * p_c
                + h_sigma
                * np.sqrt(c_c * (2 - c_c) * mu_eff)
                * ((mean - old_mean) / sigma)
            )

            y = (selected_population - old_mean) / sigma

            # --- Median-based sigma correction (single, conservative change) ---
            if mu > 0:
                # robust median of selected displacement norms
                try:
                    m_med = float(np.median(np.linalg.norm(y, axis=1)))
                    if not np.isfinite(m_med) or m_med <= 0.0:
                        m_med = expected_median
                except Exception:
                    m_med = expected_median

                eta = 0.1 * c_sigma
                # small multiplicative correction
                # guard exponent to reasonable bounds to avoid overflow
                corr_exponent = eta * (m_med / expected_median - 1.0)
                corr_exponent = np.clip(corr_exponent, -5.0, 5.0)
                sigma = sigma * np.exp(corr_exponent)

                # Ensure sigma remains finite and within previous safeguards
                if not np.isfinite(sigma):
                    sigma = self.sigma * (high_b - low_b)

                sigma = np.clip(sigma, 1e-12, 1e3 * (high_b - low_b))
            # -------------------------------------------------------------------

            rank_mu_update = np.zeros((dim, dim))

            for i in range(mu):
                rank_mu_update += weights[i] * np.outer(y[i], y[i])

            C = (
                (1 - c1 - c_mu) * C
                + c1 * np.outer(p_c, p_c)
                + c_mu * rank_mu_update
            )

            C = (C + C.T) / 2

            # Final covariance safeguard
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(C)):
                C = np.eye(dim)

            C = C + 1e-12 * np.eye(dim)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 3
            # ========================================================

            history["best_fitness"].append(best_fitness)
            history["sigma"].append(sigma)
            history["mean"].append(mean.copy())

        return best_solution, best_fitness
```

# ========================================================
# CMA-ES Variant: LLM-generated CMA-ES variant cycle 5 | Cycle 5
# ========================================================

## Metric:
Values represent error to the BBOB optimum. Lower is better.

## Algorithm Code:
```python
import numpy as np
import ioh


class LLM_CMAES:
    '''
    CMA-ES variant that injects a small fraction of heavy-tailed Student-t samples
    into the Gaussian offspring each generation. Injected Student-t directions are
    rescaled so their Euclidean norm matches the expected norm of a standard
    Gaussian draw (preserving directional heavy tails while limiting extreme radii).
    '''
    def __init__(
            self,
            pop_size=20,
            num_iterations=100,
            sigma=0.5
        ):

        self.pop_size = pop_size
        self.num_iterations = num_iterations
        self.sigma = sigma

        # Hardcoded mixture parameters per EXPLAINABILITY_REPORT
        self._alpha = 0.10  # fraction of offspring to replace with Student-t
        self._nu = 3        # degrees of freedom for Student-t

        self.variant_description = {
            "sampling": (
                "Modified: Each generation, a small fixed fraction alpha="
                "0.10 of offspring are replaced by independent multivariate "
                "Student-t(nu=3) draws in the current covariance basis. "
                "Each injected Student-t vector z_t is rescaled so that its "
                "Euclidean norm equals the expected norm of a standard Gaussian "
                "draw (expected_norm). Concretely: after drawing z_t ~ Student-t(nu), "
                "compute r = ||z_t|| and scale z_t <- z_t * (expected_norm / max(r, eps)). "
                "This preserves heavy-tailed directional variability while limiting "
                "extreme radii that can destabilize step-size and covariance adaptation. "
                "Transformed by BD and scaled by sigma: x = mean + sigma * (z_t @ BD.T). "
                "The remaining offspring are sampled as standard Gaussians. Injected "
                "offspring are clipped to bounds and evaluated alongside the rest; "
                "selection and all adaptation rules remain unchanged."
            ),
            "mean_update": "Unchanged from parent algorithm.",
            "adaptation": "Unchanged from parent algorithm."
        }

    def __call__(self, problem: ioh.problem.RealSingleObjective):

        dim = problem.meta_data.n_variables
        low_b, high_b = problem.bounds.lb[0], problem.bounds.ub[0]

        if self.pop_size is None:
            lambda_ = 4 + int(3 * np.log(dim))
        else:
            lambda_ = self.pop_size

        mu = lambda_ // 2

        weights = np.log(mu + 0.5) - np.log(np.arange(1, mu + 1))
        weights = weights / np.sum(weights)

        mu_eff = 1.0 / np.sum(weights ** 2)

        c_sigma = (mu_eff + 2) / (dim + mu_eff + 5)

        d_sigma = (
            1
            + 2 * max(0, np.sqrt((mu_eff - 1) / (dim + 1)) - 1)
            + c_sigma
        )

        c_c = (4 + mu_eff / dim) / (dim + 4 + 2 * mu_eff / dim)

        c1 = 2 / ((dim + 1.3) ** 2 + mu_eff)

        c_mu = min(
            1 - c1,
            2 * (mu_eff - 2 + 1 / mu_eff) / ((dim + 2) ** 2 + mu_eff)
        )

        expected_norm = np.sqrt(dim) * (
            1 - 1 / (4 * dim) + 1 / (21 * dim ** 2)
        )

        mean = np.random.uniform(low_b, high_b, dim)
        sigma = self.sigma * (high_b - low_b)

        C = np.eye(dim)

        p_sigma = np.zeros(dim)
        p_c = np.zeros(dim)

        best_solution = None
        best_fitness = np.inf

        history = {
            "best_fitness": [],
            "sigma": [],
            "mean": [],
            "stagnation_counter": 0,
            "restart_count": 0
        }

        for iteration in range(self.num_iterations):

            # Numerical safeguard for covariance matrix
            C = (C + C.T) / 2

            if not np.all(np.isfinite(C)):
                C = np.eye(dim)

            C = C + 1e-12 * np.eye(dim)

            eigenvalues, B = np.linalg.eigh(C)
            eigenvalues = np.maximum(eigenvalues, 1e-20)

            D = np.sqrt(eigenvalues)
            BD = B @ np.diag(D)

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 1: Sampling (modified)
            # ========================================================

            # Standard Gaussian draws in isotropic coordinates
            z = np.random.randn(lambda_, dim)

            # Inject a small fraction alpha of Student-t heavy-tailed draws
            if self._alpha > 0.0:
                n_heavy = int(np.ceil(self._alpha * lambda_))
                n_heavy = min(max(n_heavy, 0), lambda_)
                if n_heavy > 0:
                    heavy_idx = np.random.choice(lambda_, size=n_heavy, replace=False)
                    # Student-t draws with nu degrees of freedom, independent per coordinate
                    z_t = np.random.standard_t(self._nu, size=(n_heavy, dim))
                    # Normalize each injected Student-t vector's norm to expected_norm
                    eps = 1e-12
                    norms = np.linalg.norm(z_t, axis=1)
                    scales = expected_norm / np.maximum(norms, eps)
                    z_t = z_t * scales[:, np.newaxis]
                    # Replace selected rows in z with normalized Student-t samples
                    z[heavy_idx] = z_t

            # Transform into solution space using current covariance eigenbasis and scale
            population = mean + sigma * (z @ BD.T)

            # Numerical safeguard for candidate solutions
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(population)):
                population = np.nan_to_num(
                    population,
                    nan=0.0,
                    posinf=high_b,
                    neginf=low_b
                )

            population = np.clip(population, low_b, high_b)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 1
            # ========================================================

            fitness = np.array([problem(ind) for ind in population])

            sorted_indices = np.argsort(fitness)

            population = population[sorted_indices]
            z = z[sorted_indices]
            fitness = fitness[sorted_indices]

            previous_best_fitness = best_fitness

            if fitness[0] < best_fitness:
                best_fitness = fitness[0]
                best_solution = population[0].copy()
                history["stagnation_counter"] = 0
            else:
                history["stagnation_counter"] += 1

            selected_population = population[:mu]
            selected_z = z[:mu]

            old_mean = mean.copy()

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 2: Mean update
            # ========================================================

            mean = np.sum(
                weights[:, np.newaxis] * selected_population,
                axis=0
            )

            mean = np.clip(mean, low_b, high_b)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 2
            # ========================================================

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 3: Adaptation
            # ========================================================

            C_inv_sqrt = B @ np.diag(1 / D) @ B.T

            p_sigma = (
                (1 - c_sigma) * p_sigma
                + np.sqrt(c_sigma * (2 - c_sigma) * mu_eff)
                * (C_inv_sqrt @ ((mean - old_mean) / sigma))
            )

            sigma_exponent = (
                (c_sigma / d_sigma)
                * (np.linalg.norm(p_sigma) / expected_norm - 1)
            )

            # Prevent overflow in the exponential step-size update
            sigma_exponent = np.clip(
                sigma_exponent,
                -50.0,
                50.0
            )

            sigma = sigma * np.exp(sigma_exponent)

            # Recover from numerical instability if sigma becomes NaN or Inf
            if not np.isfinite(sigma):
                sigma = self.sigma * (high_b - low_b)

            sigma = np.clip(
                sigma,
                1e-12,
                1e3 * (high_b - low_b)
            )

            norm_p_sigma = np.linalg.norm(p_sigma)

            h_sigma_condition = norm_p_sigma / np.sqrt(
                1 - (1 - c_sigma) ** (2 * (iteration + 1))
            ) < (1.4 + 2 / (dim + 1)) * expected_norm

            h_sigma = 1.0 if h_sigma_condition else 0.0

            p_c = (
                (1 - c_c) * p_c
                + h_sigma
                * np.sqrt(c_c * (2 - c_c) * mu_eff)
                * ((mean - old_mean) / sigma)
            )

            y = (selected_population - old_mean) / sigma

            rank_mu_update = np.zeros((dim, dim))

            for i in range(mu):
                rank_mu_update += weights[i] * np.outer(y[i], y[i])

            C = (
                (1 - c1 - c_mu) * C
                + c1 * np.outer(p_c, p_c)
                + c_mu * rank_mu_update
            )

            C = (C + C.T) / 2

            # Final covariance safeguard
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(C)):
                C = np.eye(dim)

            C = C + 1e-12 * np.eye(dim)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 3
            # ========================================================

            history["best_fitness"].append(best_fitness)
            history["sigma"].append(sigma)
            history["mean"].append(mean.copy())

        return best_solution, best_fitness
```

# ========================================================
# CMA-ES Variant: LLM-generated CMA-ES variant cycle 6 | Cycle 6
# ========================================================

## Metric:
Values represent error to the BBOB optimum. Lower is better.

## Algorithm Code:
```python
import numpy as np
import ioh


class LLM_CMAES:
    '''
    CMA-ES variant that injects a small fraction of heavy-tailed Student-t samples
    into the Gaussian offspring each generation. Injected Student-t directions are
    rescaled so their Euclidean norm matches the expected norm of a standard
    Gaussian draw (preserving directional heavy tails while limiting extreme radii).
    Additionally implements a stagnation-triggered temporary increase in the
    heavy-tail injection fraction to promote exploration when the run is stuck.
    '''
    def __init__(
            self,
            pop_size=20,
            num_iterations=100,
            sigma=0.5
        ):

        self.pop_size = pop_size
        self.num_iterations = num_iterations
        self.sigma = sigma

        # Hardcoded mixture parameters per EXPLAINABILITY_REPORT
        # Base injection fraction
        self._alpha_base = 0.10  # fraction of offspring to replace normally with Student-t
        # Degrees of freedom for Student-t
        self._nu = 3        # degrees of freedom for Student-t

        # Stagnation-triggered adaptive injection parameters (TRY)
        self._alpha_high = 0.30           # raised fraction during boost
        self._stagnation_threshold = 5    # S: consecutive generations without improvement to trigger boost
        self._alpha_boost_duration = 3    # T: number of generations to keep alpha high

        self.variant_description = {
            "sampling": (
                "Modified: Adaptive heavy-tail injection fraction. Normally use alpha_base=0.10 "
                "of offspring replaced by multivariate Student-t(nu=3) draws (with per-vector "
                "norm rescaling to the expected Gaussian norm). If no best-so-far improvement "
                "is observed for S=5 consecutive generations, temporarily raise alpha to "
                "alpha_high=0.30 for T=3 generations to increase exploratory mass. After the "
                "boost period, revert to alpha_base. Any improvement resets the stagnation "
                "counter (and cancels an ongoing boost). All injected Student-t vectors are "
                "rescaled so their Euclidean norm equals the expected norm of a standard "
                "Gaussian draw. Injected offspring replace existing ones within the lambda budget; "
                "no extra evaluations are performed."
            ),
            "mean_update": "Unchanged from parent algorithm.",
            "adaptation": "Unchanged from parent algorithm."
        }

    def __call__(self, problem: ioh.problem.RealSingleObjective):

        dim = problem.meta_data.n_variables
        low_b, high_b = problem.bounds.lb[0], problem.bounds.ub[0]

        if self.pop_size is None:
            lambda_ = 4 + int(3 * np.log(dim))
        else:
            lambda_ = self.pop_size

        mu = lambda_ // 2

        weights = np.log(mu + 0.5) - np.log(np.arange(1, mu + 1))
        weights = weights / np.sum(weights)

        mu_eff = 1.0 / np.sum(weights ** 2)

        c_sigma = (mu_eff + 2) / (dim + mu_eff + 5)

        d_sigma = (
            1
            + 2 * max(0, np.sqrt((mu_eff - 1) / (dim + 1)) - 1)
            + c_sigma
        )

        c_c = (4 + mu_eff / dim) / (dim + 4 + 2 * mu_eff / dim)

        c1 = 2 / ((dim + 1.3) ** 2 + mu_eff)

        c_mu = min(
            1 - c1,
            2 * (mu_eff - 2 + 1 / mu_eff) / ((dim + 2) ** 2 + mu_eff)
        )

        expected_norm = np.sqrt(dim) * (
            1 - 1 / (4 * dim) + 1 / (21 * dim ** 2)
        )

        mean = np.random.uniform(low_b, high_b, dim)
        sigma = self.sigma * (high_b - low_b)

        C = np.eye(dim)

        p_sigma = np.zeros(dim)
        p_c = np.zeros(dim)

        best_solution = None
        best_fitness = np.inf

        history = {
            "best_fitness": [],
            "sigma": [],
            "mean": [],
            "stagnation_counter": 0,
            "restart_count": 0
        }

        # Runtime counter for remaining boosted generations
        boost_remaining = 0

        for iteration in range(self.num_iterations):

            # Numerical safeguard for covariance matrix
            C = (C + C.T) / 2

            if not np.all(np.isfinite(C)):
                C = np.eye(dim)

            C = C + 1e-12 * np.eye(dim)

            eigenvalues, B = np.linalg.eigh(C)
            eigenvalues = np.maximum(eigenvalues, 1e-20)

            D = np.sqrt(eigenvalues)
            BD = B @ np.diag(D)

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 1: Sampling (modified: adaptive alpha)
            # ========================================================

            # Determine current alpha: either base or high if in boost period or triggered now
            # If stagnation counter reaches threshold and no boost is active, start a boost
            if history["stagnation_counter"] >= self._stagnation_threshold and boost_remaining == 0:
                boost_remaining = self._alpha_boost_duration

            current_alpha = self._alpha_high if boost_remaining > 0 else self._alpha_base

            # Standard Gaussian draws in isotropic coordinates
            z = np.random.randn(lambda_, dim)

            # Inject a fraction current_alpha of Student-t heavy-tailed draws
            if current_alpha > 0.0:
                n_heavy = int(np.ceil(current_alpha * lambda_))
                n_heavy = min(max(n_heavy, 0), lambda_)
                if n_heavy > 0:
                    heavy_idx = np.random.choice(lambda_, size=n_heavy, replace=False)
                    # Student-t draws with nu degrees of freedom, independent per coordinate
                    z_t = np.random.standard_t(self._nu, size=(n_heavy, dim))
                    # Normalize each injected Student-t vector's norm to expected_norm
                    eps = 1e-12
                    norms = np.linalg.norm(z_t, axis=1)
                    scales = expected_norm / np.maximum(norms, eps)
                    z_t = z_t * scales[:, np.newaxis]
                    # Replace selected rows in z with normalized Student-t samples
                    z[heavy_idx] = z_t

            # After using one boosted generation, decrement remaining counter
            if boost_remaining > 0:
                boost_remaining -= 1

            # Transform into solution space using current covariance eigenbasis and scale
            population = mean + sigma * (z @ BD.T)

            # Numerical safeguard for candidate solutions
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(population)):
                population = np.nan_to_num(
                    population,
                    nan=0.0,
                    posinf=high_b,
                    neginf=low_b
                )

            population = np.clip(population, low_b, high_b)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 1
            # ========================================================

            fitness = np.array([problem(ind) for ind in population])

            sorted_indices = np.argsort(fitness)

            population = population[sorted_indices]
            z = z[sorted_indices]
            fitness = fitness[sorted_indices]

            previous_best_fitness = best_fitness

            if fitness[0] < best_fitness:
                best_fitness = fitness[0]
                best_solution = population[0].copy()
                history["stagnation_counter"] = 0
                # Cancel any ongoing boost upon improvement
                boost_remaining = 0
            else:
                history["stagnation_counter"] += 1

            selected_population = population[:mu]
            selected_z = z[:mu]

            old_mean = mean.copy()

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 2: Mean update
            # ========================================================

            mean = np.sum(
                weights[:, np.newaxis] * selected_population,
                axis=0
            )

            mean = np.clip(mean, low_b, high_b)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 2
            # ========================================================

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 3: Adaptation
            # ========================================================

            C_inv_sqrt = B @ np.diag(1 / D) @ B.T

            p_sigma = (
                (1 - c_sigma) * p_sigma
                + np.sqrt(c_sigma * (2 - c_sigma) * mu_eff)
                * (C_inv_sqrt @ ((mean - old_mean) / sigma))
            )

            sigma_exponent = (
                (c_sigma / d_sigma)
                * (np.linalg.norm(p_sigma) / expected_norm - 1)
            )

            # Prevent overflow in the exponential step-size update
            sigma_exponent = np.clip(
                sigma_exponent,
                -50.0,
                50.0
            )

            sigma = sigma * np.exp(sigma_exponent)

            # Recover from numerical instability if sigma becomes NaN or Inf
            if not np.isfinite(sigma):
                sigma = self.sigma * (high_b - low_b)

            sigma = np.clip(
                sigma,
                1e-12,
                1e3 * (high_b - low_b)
            )

            norm_p_sigma = np.linalg.norm(p_sigma)

            h_sigma_condition = norm_p_sigma / np.sqrt(
                1 - (1 - c_sigma) ** (2 * (iteration + 1))
            ) < (1.4 + 2 / (dim + 1)) * expected_norm

            h_sigma = 1.0 if h_sigma_condition else 0.0

            p_c = (
                (1 - c_c) * p_c
                + h_sigma
                * np.sqrt(c_c * (2 - c_c) * mu_eff)
                * ((mean - old_mean) / sigma)
            )

            y = (selected_population - old_mean) / sigma

            rank_mu_update = np.zeros((dim, dim))

            for i in range(mu):
                rank_mu_update += weights[i] * np.outer(y[i], y[i])

            C = (
                (1 - c1 - c_mu) * C
                + c1 * np.outer(p_c, p_c)
                + c_mu * rank_mu_update
            )

            C = (C + C.T) / 2

            # Final covariance safeguard
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(C)):
                C = np.eye(dim)

            C = C + 1e-12 * np.eye(dim)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 3
            # ========================================================

            history["best_fitness"].append(best_fitness)
            history["sigma"].append(sigma)
            history["mean"].append(mean.copy())

        return best_solution, best_fitness
```
