# ========================================================
# CMA-ES Variant: LLM-generated CMA-ES variant cycle 7 | Cycle 7
# ========================================================

## Metric:
Values represent error to the BBOB optimum. Lower is better.

## Algorithm Code:
```python
import numpy as np
import ioh


class LLM_CMAES:
    '''
    CMA-ES variant that injects a small fraction of heavy-tailed Student-t samples
    into the Gaussian offspring each generation. Injected Student-t directions are
    rescaled so their Euclidean norm matches the expected norm of a standard
    Gaussian draw (preserving directional heavy tails while limiting extreme radii).
    Additionally implements a stagnation-triggered temporary increase in the
    heavy-tail injection fraction to promote exploration when the run is stuck.
    '''
    def __init__(
            self,
            pop_size=20,
            num_iterations=100,
            sigma=0.5
        ):

        self.pop_size = pop_size
        self.num_iterations = num_iterations
        self.sigma = sigma

        # Hardcoded mixture parameters per EXPLAINABILITY_REPORT
        # Base injection fraction
        self._alpha_base = 0.10  # fraction of offspring to replace normally with Student-t
        # Degrees of freedom for Student-t
        self._nu = 3        # degrees of freedom for Student-t

        # Stagnation-triggered adaptive injection parameters (TRY)
        self._alpha_high = 0.30           # raised fraction during boost
        self._stagnation_threshold = 5    # S: consecutive generations without improvement to trigger boost
        self._alpha_boost_duration = 3    # T: number of generations to keep alpha high

        # Blending factor for steering heavy-tailed directions toward evolution path p_c
        # as specified in the EXPLAINABILITY_REPORT TRY (single conceptual modification).
        # Choose gamma in recommended range (0.4-0.6).
        self._pc_blend_gamma = 0.5
        # Small epsilon to consider p_c meaningful
        self._pc_eps = 1e-12

        self.variant_description = {
            "sampling": (
                "Modified: Adaptive heavy-tail injection fraction with directional bias. "
                "Normally use alpha_base=0.10 of offspring replaced by multivariate Student-t(nu=3) draws "
                "(with per-vector norm rescaling to the expected Gaussian norm). If no best-so-far "
                "improvement is observed for S=5 consecutive generations, temporarily raise alpha to "
                "alpha_high=0.30 for T=3 generations to increase exploratory mass. Additionally, when "
                "injecting Student-t directions, if the evolution path p_c is meaningful (||p_c||>eps), "
                "each Student-t vector z_t is blended toward the projection along the unit evolution-path "
                "direction u = p_c / ||p_c|| via z_t <- (1-gamma)*z_t + gamma*(u*(u·z_t)) with gamma=0.5, "
                "then per-vector norm rescaling to expected Gaussian norm is applied. This biases heavy tails "
                "toward promising directions without adding extra evaluations. All other sampling safeguards "
                "and boost logic remain as in the parent."
            ),
            "mean_update": "Unchanged from parent algorithm.",
            "adaptation": "Unchanged from parent algorithm."
        }

    def __call__(self, problem: ioh.problem.RealSingleObjective):

        dim = problem.meta_data.n_variables
        low_b, high_b = problem.bounds.lb[0], problem.bounds.ub[0]

        if self.pop_size is None:
            lambda_ = 4 + int(3 * np.log(dim))
        else:
            lambda_ = self.pop_size

        mu = lambda_ // 2

        weights = np.log(mu + 0.5) - np.log(np.arange(1, mu + 1))
        weights = weights / np.sum(weights)

        mu_eff = 1.0 / np.sum(weights ** 2)

        c_sigma = (mu_eff + 2) / (dim + mu_eff + 5)

        d_sigma = (
            1
            + 2 * max(0, np.sqrt((mu_eff - 1) / (dim + 1)) - 1)
            + c_sigma
        )

        c_c = (4 + mu_eff / dim) / (dim + 4 + 2 * mu_eff / dim)

        c1 = 2 / ((dim + 1.3) ** 2 + mu_eff)

        c_mu = min(
            1 - c1,
            2 * (mu_eff - 2 + 1 / mu_eff) / ((dim + 2) ** 2 + mu_eff)
        )

        expected_norm = np.sqrt(dim) * (
            1 - 1 / (4 * dim) + 1 / (21 * dim ** 2)
        )

        mean = np.random.uniform(low_b, high_b, dim)
        sigma = self.sigma * (high_b - low_b)

        C = np.eye(dim)

        p_sigma = np.zeros(dim)
        p_c = np.zeros(dim)

        best_solution = None
        best_fitness = np.inf

        history = {
            "best_fitness": [],
            "sigma": [],
            "mean": [],
            "stagnation_counter": 0,
            "restart_count": 0
        }

        # Runtime counter for remaining boosted generations
        boost_remaining = 0

        for iteration in range(self.num_iterations):

            # Numerical safeguard for covariance matrix
            C = (C + C.T) / 2

            if not np.all(np.isfinite(C)):
                C = np.eye(dim)

            C = C + 1e-12 * np.eye(dim)

            eigenvalues, B = np.linalg.eigh(C)
            eigenvalues = np.maximum(eigenvalues, 1e-20)

            D = np.sqrt(eigenvalues)
            BD = B @ np.diag(D)

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 1: Sampling (modified: adaptive alpha and directional blend)
            # ========================================================

            # Determine current alpha: either base or high if in boost period or triggered now
            # If stagnation counter reaches threshold and no boost is active, start a boost
            if history["stagnation_counter"] >= self._stagnation_threshold and boost_remaining == 0:
                boost_remaining = self._alpha_boost_duration

            current_alpha = self._alpha_high if boost_remaining > 0 else self._alpha_base

            # Standard Gaussian draws in isotropic coordinates
            z = np.random.randn(lambda_, dim)

            # Inject a fraction current_alpha of Student-t heavy-tailed draws
            if current_alpha > 0.0:
                n_heavy = int(np.ceil(current_alpha * lambda_))
                n_heavy = min(max(n_heavy, 0), lambda_)
                if n_heavy > 0:
                    heavy_idx = np.random.choice(lambda_, size=n_heavy, replace=False)
                    # Student-t draws with nu degrees of freedom, independent per coordinate
                    z_t = np.random.standard_t(self._nu, size=(n_heavy, dim))

                    # Blend Student-t directions toward evolution path p_c if meaningful
                    norm_p_c = np.linalg.norm(p_c)
                    if norm_p_c > self._pc_eps:
                        u = p_c / norm_p_c
                        # Project z_t onto u and blend: z_t <- (1-gamma)*z_t + gamma*(u*(u·z_t))
                        dot = z_t @ u  # shape (n_heavy,)
                        projection = dot[:, np.newaxis] * u[np.newaxis, :]
                        gamma = self._pc_blend_gamma
                        z_t = (1.0 - gamma) * z_t + gamma * projection

                    # Normalize each injected Student-t vector's norm to expected_norm
                    eps = 1e-12
                    norms = np.linalg.norm(z_t, axis=1)
                    scales = expected_norm / np.maximum(norms, eps)
                    z_t = z_t * scales[:, np.newaxis]
                    # Replace selected rows in z with normalized Student-t samples
                    z[heavy_idx] = z_t

            # After using one boosted generation, decrement remaining counter
            if boost_remaining > 0:
                boost_remaining -= 1

            # Transform into solution space using current covariance eigenbasis and scale
            population = mean + sigma * (z @ BD.T)

            # Numerical safeguard for candidate solutions
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(population)):
                population = np.nan_to_num(
                    population,
                    nan=0.0,
                    posinf=high_b,
                    neginf=low_b
                )

            population = np.clip(population, low_b, high_b)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 1
            # ========================================================

            fitness = np.array([problem(ind) for ind in population])

            sorted_indices = np.argsort(fitness)

            population = population[sorted_indices]
            z = z[sorted_indices]
            fitness = fitness[sorted_indices]

            previous_best_fitness = best_fitness

            if fitness[0] < best_fitness:
                best_fitness = fitness[0]
                best_solution = population[0].copy()
                history["stagnation_counter"] = 0
                # Cancel any ongoing boost upon improvement
                boost_remaining = 0
            else:
                history["stagnation_counter"] += 1

            selected_population = population[:mu]
            selected_z = z[:mu]

            old_mean = mean.copy()

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 2: Mean update
            # ========================================================

            mean = np.sum(
                weights[:, np.newaxis] * selected_population,
                axis=0
            )

            mean = np.clip(mean, low_b, high_b)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 2
            # ========================================================

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 3: Adaptation
            # ========================================================

            C_inv_sqrt = B @ np.diag(1 / D) @ B.T

            p_sigma = (
                (1 - c_sigma) * p_sigma
                + np.sqrt(c_sigma * (2 - c_sigma) * mu_eff)
                * (C_inv_sqrt @ ((mean - old_mean) / sigma))
            )

            sigma_exponent = (
                (c_sigma / d_sigma)
                * (np.linalg.norm(p_sigma) / expected_norm - 1)
            )

            # Prevent overflow in the exponential step-size update
            sigma_exponent = np.clip(
                sigma_exponent,
                -50.0,
                50.0
            )

            sigma = sigma * np.exp(sigma_exponent)

            # Recover from numerical instability if sigma becomes NaN or Inf
            if not np.isfinite(sigma):
                sigma = self.sigma * (high_b - low_b)

            sigma = np.clip(
                sigma,
                1e-12,
                1e3 * (high_b - low_b)
            )

            norm_p_sigma = np.linalg.norm(p_sigma)

            h_sigma_condition = norm_p_sigma / np.sqrt(
                1 - (1 - c_sigma) ** (2 * (iteration + 1))
            ) < (1.4 + 2 / (dim + 1)) * expected_norm

            h_sigma = 1.0 if h_sigma_condition else 0.0

            p_c = (
                (1 - c_c) * p_c
                + h_sigma
                * np.sqrt(c_c * (2 - c_c) * mu_eff)
                * ((mean - old_mean) / sigma)
            )

            y = (selected_population - old_mean) / sigma

            rank_mu_update = np.zeros((dim, dim))

            for i in range(mu):
                rank_mu_update += weights[i] * np.outer(y[i], y[i])

            C = (
                (1 - c1 - c_mu) * C
                + c1 * np.outer(p_c, p_c)
                + c_mu * rank_mu_update
            )

            C = (C + C.T) / 2

            # Final covariance safeguard
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(C)):
                C = np.eye(dim)

            C = C + 1e-12 * np.eye(dim)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 3
            # ========================================================

            history["best_fitness"].append(best_fitness)
            history["sigma"].append(sigma)
            history["mean"].append(mean.copy())

        return best_solution, best_fitness
```

# CMA-ES Variant: LLM-generated CMA-ES variant cycle 8 | Cycle 8

## Metric:
Values represent error to the BBOB optimum. Lower is better.

## Algorithm Code:
```python
import numpy as np
import ioh


class LLM_CMAES:
    '''
    CMA-ES variant that injects a small fraction of heavy-tailed Student-t samples
    into the Gaussian offspring each generation. Injected Student-t directions are
    rescaled so their Euclidean norm matches the expected norm of a standard
    Gaussian draw (preserving directional heavy tails while limiting extreme radii).
    Additionally implements a stagnation-triggered temporary increase in the
    heavy-tail injection fraction to promote exploration when the run is stuck.
    '''
    def __init__(
            self,
            pop_size=20,
            num_iterations=100,
            sigma=0.5
        ):

        self.pop_size = pop_size
        self.num_iterations = num_iterations
        self.sigma = sigma

        # Hardcoded mixture parameters per EXPLAINABILITY_REPORT
        # Base injection fraction
        self._alpha_base = 0.10  # fraction of offspring to replace normally with Student-t
        # Degrees of freedom for Student-t
        self._nu = 3        # degrees of freedom for Student-t

        # Stagnation-triggered adaptive injection parameters (TRY)
        self._alpha_high = 0.25           # raised fraction during boost (reduced from 0.30)
        self._stagnation_threshold = 5    # S: consecutive generations without improvement to trigger boost
        self._alpha_boost_duration = 3    # T: number of generations to keep alpha high

        self.variant_description = {
            "sampling": (
                "Modified: Adaptive heavy-tail injection fraction. Normally use alpha_base=0.10 "
                "of offspring replaced by multivariate Student-t(nu=3) draws (with per-vector "
                "norm rescaling to the expected Gaussian norm). If no best-so-far improvement "
                "is observed for S=5 consecutive generations, temporarily raise alpha to "
                "alpha_high=0.25 for T=3 generations to increase exploratory mass. After the "
                "boost period, revert to alpha_base. Any improvement resets the stagnation "
                "counter (and cancels an ongoing boost). All injected Student-t vectors are "
                "rescaled so their Euclidean norm equals the expected norm of a standard "
                "Gaussian draw. Injected offspring replace existing ones within the lambda budget; "
                "no extra evaluations are performed."
            ),
            "mean_update": "Unchanged from parent algorithm.",
            "adaptation": "Unchanged from parent algorithm."
        }

    def __call__(self, problem: ioh.problem.RealSingleObjective):

        dim = problem.meta_data.n_variables
        low_b, high_b = problem.bounds.lb[0], problem.bounds.ub[0]

        if self.pop_size is None:
            lambda_ = 4 + int(3 * np.log(dim))
        else:
            lambda_ = self.pop_size

        mu = lambda_ // 2

        weights = np.log(mu + 0.5) - np.log(np.arange(1, mu + 1))
        weights = weights / np.sum(weights)

        mu_eff = 1.0 / np.sum(weights ** 2)

        c_sigma = (mu_eff + 2) / (dim + mu_eff + 5)

        d_sigma = (
            1
            + 2 * max(0, np.sqrt((mu_eff - 1) / (dim + 1)) - 1)
            + c_sigma
        )

        c_c = (4 + mu_eff / dim) / (dim + 4 + 2 * mu_eff / dim)

        c1 = 2 / ((dim + 1.3) ** 2 + mu_eff)

        c_mu = min(
            1 - c1,
            2 * (mu_eff - 2 + 1 / mu_eff) / ((dim + 2) ** 2 + mu_eff)
        )

        expected_norm = np.sqrt(dim) * (
            1 - 1 / (4 * dim) + 1 / (21 * dim ** 2)
        )

        mean = np.random.uniform(low_b, high_b, dim)
        sigma = self.sigma * (high_b - low_b)

        C = np.eye(dim)

        p_sigma = np.zeros(dim)
        p_c = np.zeros(dim)

        best_solution = None
        best_fitness = np.inf

        history = {
            "best_fitness": [],
            "sigma": [],
            "mean": [],
            "stagnation_counter": 0,
            "restart_count": 0
        }

        # Runtime counter for remaining boosted generations
        boost_remaining = 0

        for iteration in range(self.num_iterations):

            # Numerical safeguard for covariance matrix
            C = (C + C.T) / 2

            if not np.all(np.isfinite(C)):
                C = np.eye(dim)

            C = C + 1e-12 * np.eye(dim)

            eigenvalues, B = np.linalg.eigh(C)
            eigenvalues = np.maximum(eigenvalues, 1e-20)

            D = np.sqrt(eigenvalues)
            BD = B @ np.diag(D)

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 1: Sampling (modified: adaptive alpha)
            # ========================================================

            # Determine current alpha: either base or high if in boost period or triggered now
            # If stagnation counter reaches threshold and no boost is active, start a boost
            if history["stagnation_counter"] >= self._stagnation_threshold and boost_remaining == 0:
                boost_remaining = self._alpha_boost_duration

            current_alpha = self._alpha_high if boost_remaining > 0 else self._alpha_base

            # Standard Gaussian draws in isotropic coordinates
            z = np.random.randn(lambda_, dim)

            # Inject a fraction current_alpha of Student-t heavy-tailed draws
            if current_alpha > 0.0:
                n_heavy = int(np.ceil(current_alpha * lambda_))
                n_heavy = min(max(n_heavy, 0), lambda_)
                if n_heavy > 0:
                    heavy_idx = np.random.choice(lambda_, size=n_heavy, replace=False)
                    # Student-t draws with nu degrees of freedom, independent per coordinate
                    z_t = np.random.standard_t(self._nu, size=(n_heavy, dim))
                    # Normalize each injected Student-t vector's norm to expected_norm
                    eps = 1e-12
                    norms = np.linalg.norm(z_t, axis=1)
                    scales = expected_norm / np.maximum(norms, eps)
                    z_t = z_t * scales[:, np.newaxis]
                    # Replace selected rows in z with normalized Student-t samples
                    z[heavy_idx] = z_t

            # After using one boosted generation, decrement remaining counter
            if boost_remaining > 0:
                boost_remaining -= 1

            # Transform into solution space using current covariance eigenbasis and scale
            population = mean + sigma * (z @ BD.T)

            # Numerical safeguard for candidate solutions
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(population)):
                population = np.nan_to_num(
                    population,
                    nan=0.0,
                    posinf=high_b,
                    neginf=low_b
                )

            population = np.clip(population, low_b, high_b)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 1
            # ========================================================

            fitness = np.array([problem(ind) for ind in population])

            sorted_indices = np.argsort(fitness)

            population = population[sorted_indices]
            z = z[sorted_indices]
            fitness = fitness[sorted_indices]

            previous_best_fitness = best_fitness

            if fitness[0] < best_fitness:
                best_fitness = fitness[0]
                best_solution = population[0].copy()
                history["stagnation_counter"] = 0
                # Cancel any ongoing boost upon improvement
                boost_remaining = 0
            else:
                history["stagnation_counter"] += 1

            selected_population = population[:mu]
            selected_z = z[:mu]

            old_mean = mean.copy()

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 2: Mean update
            # ========================================================

            mean = np.sum(
                weights[:, np.newaxis] * selected_population,
                axis=0
            )

            mean = np.clip(mean, low_b, high_b)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 2
            # ========================================================

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 3: Adaptation
            # ========================================================

            C_inv_sqrt = B @ np.diag(1 / D) @ B.T

            p_sigma = (
                (1 - c_sigma) * p_sigma
                + np.sqrt(c_sigma * (2 - c_sigma) * mu_eff)
                * (C_inv_sqrt @ ((mean - old_mean) / sigma))
            )

            sigma_exponent = (
                (c_sigma / d_sigma)
                * (np.linalg.norm(p_sigma) / expected_norm - 1)
            )

            # Prevent overflow in the exponential step-size update
            sigma_exponent = np.clip(
                sigma_exponent,
                -50.0,
                50.0
            )

            sigma = sigma * np.exp(sigma_exponent)

            # Recover from numerical instability if sigma becomes NaN or Inf
            if not np.isfinite(sigma):
                sigma = self.sigma * (high_b - low_b)

            sigma = np.clip(
                sigma,
                1e-12,
                1e3 * (high_b - low_b)
            )

            norm_p_sigma = np.linalg.norm(p_sigma)

            h_sigma_condition = norm_p_sigma / np.sqrt(
                1 - (1 - c_sigma) ** (2 * (iteration + 1))
            ) < (1.4 + 2 / (dim + 1)) * expected_norm

            h_sigma = 1.0 if h_sigma_condition else 0.0

            p_c = (
                (1 - c_c) * p_c
                + h_sigma
                * np.sqrt(c_c * (2 - c_c) * mu_eff)
                * ((mean - old_mean) / sigma)
            )

            y = (selected_population - old_mean) / sigma

            rank_mu_update = np.zeros((dim, dim))

            for i in range(mu):
                rank_mu_update += weights[i] * np.outer(y[i], y[i])

            C = (
                (1 - c1 - c_mu) * C
                + c1 * np.outer(p_c, p_c)
                + c_mu * rank_mu_update
            )

            C = (C + C.T) / 2

            # Final covariance safeguard
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(C)):
                C = np.eye(dim)

            C = C + 1e-12 * np.eye(dim)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 3
            # ========================================================

            history["best_fitness"].append(best_fitness)
            history["sigma"].append(sigma)
            history["mean"].append(mean.copy())

        return best_solution, best_fitness
```

# ========================================================
# CMA-ES Variant: LLM-generated CMA-ES variant cycle 9 | Cycle 9
# ========================================================

## Metric:
Values represent error to the BBOB optimum. Lower is better.

## Algorithm Code:
```python
import numpy as np
import ioh


class LLM_CMAES:
    '''
    CMA-ES variant that injects a small fraction of heavy-tailed Student-t samples
    into the Gaussian offspring each generation. Injected Student-t directions are
    rescaled so their Euclidean norm matches the expected norm of a standard
    Gaussian draw (preserving directional heavy tails while limiting extreme radii).
    Additionally implements a stagnation-triggered temporary increase in the
    heavy-tail injection fraction; the magnitude of this boost is adaptive to the
    stagnation length (longer stagnation -> larger boost up to a cap).
    '''
    def __init__(
            self,
            pop_size=20,
            num_iterations=100,
            sigma=0.5
        ):

        self.pop_size = pop_size
        self.num_iterations = num_iterations
        self.sigma = sigma

        # Hardcoded mixture parameters per EXPLAINABILITY_REPORT
        # Base injection fraction
        self._alpha_base = 0.10  # fraction of offspring to replace normally with Student-t
        # Degrees of freedom for Student-t
        self._nu = 3        # degrees of freedom for Student-t

        # Stagnation-triggered adaptive injection parameters
        # The boost magnitude is now adaptive to how long stagnation has persisted:
        # alpha_boost = min(alpha_max, alpha_base + alpha_k * (stagnation_counter - S + 1))
        self._alpha_max = 0.30            # maximum allowed boost fraction
        self._alpha_k = 0.05              # increment per extra stagnation generation beyond threshold
        self._stagnation_threshold = 5    # S: consecutive generations without improvement to trigger boost
        self._alpha_boost_duration = 3    # T: number of generations to keep adaptive alpha during a boost

        self.variant_description = {
            "sampling": (
                "Modified: Adaptive heavy-tail injection fraction. Normally use alpha_base=0.10 "
                "of offspring replaced by multivariate Student-t(nu=3) draws (with per-vector "
                "norm rescaling to the expected Gaussian norm). If no best-so-far improvement "
                "is observed for S=5 consecutive generations, start a boost of fixed duration "
                "T=3 generations. The boost magnitude is adaptive to how long the run has been "
                "stagnating: alpha_boost = min(alpha_max, alpha_base + alpha_k * (stagnation_counter - S + 1)). "
                "This increases exploratory mass progressively for prolonged stagnation while "
                "capping disruption. Any improvement resets the stagnation counter (and cancels "
                "an ongoing boost). All injected Student-t vectors are rescaled so their Euclidean "
                "norm equals the expected norm of a standard Gaussian draw. Injected offspring replace "
                "existing ones within the lambda budget; no extra evaluations are performed."
            ),
            "mean_update": "Unchanged from parent algorithm.",
            "adaptation": "Unchanged from parent algorithm."
        }

    def __call__(self, problem: ioh.problem.RealSingleObjective):

        dim = problem.meta_data.n_variables
        low_b, high_b = problem.bounds.lb[0], problem.bounds.ub[0]

        if self.pop_size is None:
            lambda_ = 4 + int(3 * np.log(dim))
        else:
            lambda_ = self.pop_size

        mu = lambda_ // 2

        weights = np.log(mu + 0.5) - np.log(np.arange(1, mu + 1))
        weights = weights / np.sum(weights)

        mu_eff = 1.0 / np.sum(weights ** 2)

        c_sigma = (mu_eff + 2) / (dim + mu_eff + 5)

        d_sigma = (
            1
            + 2 * max(0, np.sqrt((mu_eff - 1) / (dim + 1)) - 1)
            + c_sigma
        )

        c_c = (4 + mu_eff / dim) / (dim + 4 + 2 * mu_eff / dim)

        c1 = 2 / ((dim + 1.3) ** 2 + mu_eff)

        c_mu = min(
            1 - c1,
            2 * (mu_eff - 2 + 1 / mu_eff) / ((dim + 2) ** 2 + mu_eff)
        )

        expected_norm = np.sqrt(dim) * (
            1 - 1 / (4 * dim) + 1 / (21 * dim ** 2)
        )

        mean = np.random.uniform(low_b, high_b, dim)
        sigma = self.sigma * (high_b - low_b)

        C = np.eye(dim)

        p_sigma = np.zeros(dim)
        p_c = np.zeros(dim)

        best_solution = None
        best_fitness = np.inf

        history = {
            "best_fitness": [],
            "sigma": [],
            "mean": [],
            "stagnation_counter": 0,
            "restart_count": 0
        }

        # Runtime counter for remaining boosted generations
        boost_remaining = 0
        # Store the adaptive alpha used during an ongoing boost
        boost_alpha_current = None

        for iteration in range(self.num_iterations):

            # Numerical safeguard for covariance matrix
            C = (C + C.T) / 2

            if not np.all(np.isfinite(C)):
                C = np.eye(dim)

            C = C + 1e-12 * np.eye(dim)

            eigenvalues, B = np.linalg.eigh(C)
            eigenvalues = np.maximum(eigenvalues, 1e-20)

            D = np.sqrt(eigenvalues)
            BD = B @ np.diag(D)

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 1: Sampling (modified: adaptive alpha magnitude)
            # ========================================================

            # If stagnation counter reaches threshold and no boost is active, start a boost
            if history["stagnation_counter"] >= self._stagnation_threshold and boost_remaining == 0:
                # compute adaptive boost magnitude based on how long we've been stuck
                extra = history["stagnation_counter"] - self._stagnation_threshold + 1
                adaptive_alpha = self._alpha_base + self._alpha_k * extra
                adaptive_alpha = min(self._alpha_max, adaptive_alpha)
                boost_alpha_current = adaptive_alpha
                boost_remaining = self._alpha_boost_duration

            current_alpha = boost_alpha_current if boost_remaining > 0 and boost_alpha_current is not None else self._alpha_base

            # Standard Gaussian draws in isotropic coordinates
            z = np.random.randn(lambda_, dim)

            # Inject a fraction current_alpha of Student-t heavy-tailed draws
            if current_alpha > 0.0:
                n_heavy = int(np.ceil(current_alpha * lambda_))
                n_heavy = min(max(n_heavy, 0), lambda_)
                if n_heavy > 0:
                    heavy_idx = np.random.choice(lambda_, size=n_heavy, replace=False)
                    # Student-t draws with nu degrees of freedom, independent per coordinate
                    z_t = np.random.standard_t(self._nu, size=(n_heavy, dim))
                    # Normalize each injected Student-t vector's norm to expected_norm
                    eps = 1e-12
                    norms = np.linalg.norm(z_t, axis=1)
                    scales = expected_norm / np.maximum(norms, eps)
                    z_t = z_t * scales[:, np.newaxis]
                    # Replace selected rows in z with normalized Student-t samples
                    z[heavy_idx] = z_t

            # After using one boosted generation, decrement remaining counter
            if boost_remaining > 0:
                boost_remaining -= 1
                if boost_remaining == 0:
                    boost_alpha_current = None

            # Transform into solution space using current covariance eigenbasis and scale
            population = mean + sigma * (z @ BD.T)

            # Numerical safeguard for candidate solutions
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(population)):
                population = np.nan_to_num(
                    population,
                    nan=0.0,
                    posinf=high_b,
                    neginf=low_b
                )

            population = np.clip(population, low_b, high_b)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 1
            # ========================================================

            fitness = np.array([problem(ind) for ind in population])

            sorted_indices = np.argsort(fitness)

            population = population[sorted_indices]
            z = z[sorted_indices]
            fitness = fitness[sorted_indices]

            previous_best_fitness = best_fitness

            if fitness[0] < best_fitness:
                best_fitness = fitness[0]
                best_solution = population[0].copy()
                history["stagnation_counter"] = 0
                # Cancel any ongoing boost upon improvement
                boost_remaining = 0
                boost_alpha_current = None
            else:
                history["stagnation_counter"] += 1

            selected_population = population[:mu]
            selected_z = z[:mu]

            old_mean = mean.copy()

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 2: Mean update
            # ========================================================

            mean = np.sum(
                weights[:, np.newaxis] * selected_population,
                axis=0
            )

            mean = np.clip(mean, low_b, high_b)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 2
            # ========================================================

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 3: Adaptation
            # ========================================================

            C_inv_sqrt = B @ np.diag(1 / D) @ B.T

            p_sigma = (
                (1 - c_sigma) * p_sigma
                + np.sqrt(c_sigma * (2 - c_sigma) * mu_eff)
                * (C_inv_sqrt @ ((mean - old_mean) / sigma))
            )

            sigma_exponent = (
                (c_sigma / d_sigma)
                * (np.linalg.norm(p_sigma) / expected_norm - 1)
            )

            # Prevent overflow in the exponential step-size update
            sigma_exponent = np.clip(
                sigma_exponent,
                -50.0,
                50.0
            )

            sigma = sigma * np.exp(sigma_exponent)

            # Recover from numerical instability if sigma becomes NaN or Inf
            if not np.isfinite(sigma):
                sigma = self.sigma * (high_b - low_b)

            sigma = np.clip(
                sigma,
                1e-12,
                1e3 * (high_b - low_b)
            )

            norm_p_sigma = np.linalg.norm(p_sigma)

            h_sigma_condition = norm_p_sigma / np.sqrt(
                1 - (1 - c_sigma) ** (2 * (iteration + 1))
            ) < (1.4 + 2 / (dim + 1)) * expected_norm

            h_sigma = 1.0 if h_sigma_condition else 0.0

            p_c = (
                (1 - c_c) * p_c
                + h_sigma
                * np.sqrt(c_c * (2 - c_c) * mu_eff)
                * ((mean - old_mean) / sigma)
            )

            y = (selected_population - old_mean) / sigma

            rank_mu_update = np.zeros((dim, dim))

            for i in range(mu):
                rank_mu_update += weights[i] * np.outer(y[i], y[i])

            C = (
                (1 - c1 - c_mu) * C
                + c1 * np.outer(p_c, p_c)
                + c_mu * rank_mu_update
            )

            C = (C + C.T) / 2

            # Final covariance safeguard
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(C)):
                C = np.eye(dim)

            C = C + 1e-12 * np.eye(dim)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 3
            # ========================================================

            history["best_fitness"].append(best_fitness)
            history["sigma"].append(sigma)
            history["mean"].append(mean.copy())

        return best_solution, best_fitness
```

# ========================================================
# CMA-ES Variant: LLM-generated CMA-ES variant cycle 10 | Cycle 10
# ========================================================

## Metric:
Values represent error to the BBOB optimum. Lower is better.

## Algorithm Code:
```python
import numpy as np
import ioh


class LLM_CMAES:
    '''
    CMA-ES variant that injects a small fraction of heavy-tailed Student-t samples
    into the Gaussian offspring each generation. Injected Student-t directions are
    rescaled so their Euclidean norm matches the expected norm of a standard
    Gaussian draw (preserving directional heavy tails while limiting extreme radii).
    Additionally implements a stagnation-triggered temporary increase in the
    heavy-tail injection fraction; the magnitude of this boost is adaptive to the
    stagnation length (longer stagnation -> larger boost up to a cap).

    New in Cycle 10: Adaptive tail heaviness. The degrees-of-freedom (nu) of the
    Student-t used for injected samples is nu_base during normal progress and,
    when a stagnation-triggered boost is active, is reduced (heavier tails) by
    1 every K stagnation generations down to nu_min. This is applied only during
    the same boost window used by the adaptive alpha.
    '''
    def __init__(
            self,
            pop_size=20,
            num_iterations=100,
            sigma=0.5
        ):

        self.pop_size = pop_size
        self.num_iterations = num_iterations
        self.sigma = sigma

        # Hardcoded mixture parameters per EXPLAINABILITY_REPORT
        # Base injection fraction
        self._alpha_base = 0.10  # fraction of offspring to replace normally with Student-t

        # Adaptive tail-heaviness parameters (new: Cycle 10)
        # Base and minimum degrees of freedom for Student-t
        self._nu_base = 6      # moderate tail during normal progress
        self._nu_min = 2       # do not go below this (heavier tails)
        # Decrement nu by 1 every K extra stagnation generations during boost
        self._nu_k = 2

        # Stagnation-triggered adaptive injection parameters
        # The boost magnitude is now adaptive to how long stagnation has persisted:
        # alpha_boost = min(alpha_max, alpha_base + alpha_k * (stagnation_counter - S + 1))
        self._alpha_max = 0.30            # maximum allowed boost fraction
        self._alpha_k = 0.05              # increment per extra stagnation generation beyond threshold
        self._stagnation_threshold = 5    # S: consecutive generations without improvement to trigger boost
        self._alpha_boost_duration = 3    # T: number of generations to keep adaptive alpha during a boost

        self.variant_description = {
            "sampling": (
                "Modified: Adaptive heavy-tail injection fraction and adaptive tail heaviness. "
                "Normally use alpha_base=0.10 of offspring replaced by multivariate Student-t draws. "
                "If no best-so-far improvement is observed for S=5 consecutive generations, start "
                "a boost of fixed duration T=3 generations. The boost magnitude is adaptive to "
                "stagnation length: alpha_boost = min(alpha_max, alpha_base + alpha_k * (stagnation_counter - S + 1)). "
                "Additionally, during the same boost window, the Student-t degrees-of-freedom nu is reduced "
                "to make tails heavier: nu = max(nu_min, nu_base - floor((stagnation_counter - S + 1)/K)), "
                "with nu_base=6, nu_min=2, and K controlling the decrement pace. Injected Student-t vectors "
                "are rescaled so their Euclidean norm equals the expected norm of a standard Gaussian draw. "
                "Injected offspring replace existing ones within the lambda budget; no extra evaluations are performed."
            ),
            "mean_update": "Unchanged from parent algorithm.",
            "adaptation": "Unchanged from parent algorithm."
        }

    def __call__(self, problem: ioh.problem.RealSingleObjective):

        dim = problem.meta_data.n_variables
        low_b, high_b = problem.bounds.lb[0], problem.bounds.ub[0]

        if self.pop_size is None:
            lambda_ = 4 + int(3 * np.log(dim))
        else:
            lambda_ = self.pop_size

        mu = lambda_ // 2

        weights = np.log(mu + 0.5) - np.log(np.arange(1, mu + 1))
        weights = weights / np.sum(weights)

        mu_eff = 1.0 / np.sum(weights ** 2)

        c_sigma = (mu_eff + 2) / (dim + mu_eff + 5)

        d_sigma = (
            1
            + 2 * max(0, np.sqrt((mu_eff - 1) / (dim + 1)) - 1)
            + c_sigma
        )

        c_c = (4 + mu_eff / dim) / (dim + 4 + 2 * mu_eff / dim)

        c1 = 2 / ((dim + 1.3) ** 2 + mu_eff)

        c_mu = min(
            1 - c1,
            2 * (mu_eff - 2 + 1 / mu_eff) / ((dim + 2) ** 2 + mu_eff)
        )

        expected_norm = np.sqrt(dim) * (
            1 - 1 / (4 * dim) + 1 / (21 * dim ** 2)
        )

        mean = np.random.uniform(low_b, high_b, dim)
        sigma = self.sigma * (high_b - low_b)

        C = np.eye(dim)

        p_sigma = np.zeros(dim)
        p_c = np.zeros(dim)

        best_solution = None
        best_fitness = np.inf

        history = {
            "best_fitness": [],
            "sigma": [],
            "mean": [],
            "stagnation_counter": 0,
            "restart_count": 0
        }

        # Runtime counter for remaining boosted generations
        boost_remaining = 0
        # Store the adaptive alpha used during an ongoing boost
        boost_alpha_current = None

        for iteration in range(self.num_iterations):

            # Numerical safeguard for covariance matrix
            C = (C + C.T) / 2

            if not np.all(np.isfinite(C)):
                C = np.eye(dim)

            C = C + 1e-12 * np.eye(dim)

            eigenvalues, B = np.linalg.eigh(C)
            eigenvalues = np.maximum(eigenvalues, 1e-20)

            D = np.sqrt(eigenvalues)
            BD = B @ np.diag(D)

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 1: Sampling (modified: adaptive alpha magnitude and adaptive nu)
            # ========================================================

            # If stagnation counter reaches threshold and no boost is active, start a boost
            if history["stagnation_counter"] >= self._stagnation_threshold and boost_remaining == 0:
                # compute adaptive boost magnitude based on how long we've been stuck
                extra = history["stagnation_counter"] - self._stagnation_threshold + 1
                adaptive_alpha = self._alpha_base + self._alpha_k * extra
                adaptive_alpha = min(self._alpha_max, adaptive_alpha)
                boost_alpha_current = adaptive_alpha
                boost_remaining = self._alpha_boost_duration

            current_alpha = boost_alpha_current if boost_remaining > 0 and boost_alpha_current is not None else self._alpha_base

            # Determine current degrees of freedom for Student-t:
            # Use nu_base during normal progress; during an active boost, make tails heavier
            # by decrementing nu by 1 every _nu_k extra stagnation generations (floored), down to nu_min.
            if boost_remaining > 0 and boost_alpha_current is not None and history["stagnation_counter"] >= self._stagnation_threshold:
                extra = history["stagnation_counter"] - self._stagnation_threshold + 1
                decrements = extra // self._nu_k
                current_nu = max(self._nu_min, self._nu_base - int(decrements))
            else:
                current_nu = float(self._nu_base)

            # Standard Gaussian draws in isotropic coordinates
            z = np.random.randn(lambda_, dim)

            # Inject a fraction current_alpha of Student-t heavy-tailed draws
            if current_alpha > 0.0:
                n_heavy = int(np.ceil(current_alpha * lambda_))
                n_heavy = min(max(n_heavy, 0), lambda_)
                if n_heavy > 0:
                    heavy_idx = np.random.choice(lambda_, size=n_heavy, replace=False)
                    # Student-t draws with current_nu degrees of freedom, independent per coordinate
                    # numpy accepts float df, ensure it's positive
                    df = float(current_nu)
                    # Protect against invalid df values
                    if df <= 0:
                        df = float(self._nu_min)
                    z_t = np.random.standard_t(df, size=(n_heavy, dim))
                    # Normalize each injected Student-t vector's norm to expected_norm
                    eps = 1e-12
                    norms = np.linalg.norm(z_t, axis=1)
                    scales = expected_norm / np.maximum(norms, eps)
                    z_t = z_t * scales[:, np.newaxis]
                    # Replace selected rows in z with normalized Student-t samples
                    z[heavy_idx] = z_t

            # After using one boosted generation, decrement remaining counter
            if boost_remaining > 0:
                boost_remaining -= 1
                if boost_remaining == 0:
                    boost_alpha_current = None

            # Transform into solution space using current covariance eigenbasis and scale
            population = mean + sigma * (z @ BD.T)

            # Numerical safeguard for candidate solutions
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(population)):
                population = np.nan_to_num(
                    population,
                    nan=0.0,
                    posinf=high_b,
                    neginf=low_b
                )

            population = np.clip(population, low_b, high_b)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 1
            # ========================================================

            fitness = np.array([problem(ind) for ind in population])

            sorted_indices = np.argsort(fitness)

            population = population[sorted_indices]
            z = z[sorted_indices]
            fitness = fitness[sorted_indices]

            previous_best_fitness = best_fitness

            if fitness[0] < best_fitness:
                best_fitness = fitness[0]
                best_solution = population[0].copy()
                history["stagnation_counter"] = 0
                # Cancel any ongoing boost upon improvement
                boost_remaining = 0
                boost_alpha_current = None
            else:
                history["stagnation_counter"] += 1

            selected_population = population[:mu]
            selected_z = z[:mu]

            old_mean = mean.copy()

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 2: Mean update
            # ========================================================

            mean = np.sum(
                weights[:, np.newaxis] * selected_population,
                axis=0
            )

            mean = np.clip(mean, low_b, high_b)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 2
            # ========================================================

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 3: Adaptation
            # ========================================================

            C_inv_sqrt = B @ np.diag(1 / D) @ B.T

            p_sigma = (
                (1 - c_sigma) * p_sigma
                + np.sqrt(c_sigma * (2 - c_sigma) * mu_eff)
                * (C_inv_sqrt @ ((mean - old_mean) / sigma))
            )

            sigma_exponent = (
                (c_sigma / d_sigma)
                * (np.linalg.norm(p_sigma) / expected_norm - 1)
            )

            # Prevent overflow in the exponential step-size update
            sigma_exponent = np.clip(
                sigma_exponent,
                -50.0,
                50.0
            )

            sigma = sigma * np.exp(sigma_exponent)

            # Recover from numerical instability if sigma becomes NaN or Inf
            if not np.isfinite(sigma):
                sigma = self.sigma * (high_b - low_b)

            sigma = np.clip(
                sigma,
                1e-12,
                1e3 * (high_b - low_b)
            )

            norm_p_sigma = np.linalg.norm(p_sigma)

            h_sigma_condition = norm_p_sigma / np.sqrt(
                1 - (1 - c_sigma) ** (2 * (iteration + 1))
            ) < (1.4 + 2 / (dim + 1)) * expected_norm

            h_sigma = 1.0 if h_sigma_condition else 0.0

            p_c = (
                (1 - c_c) * p_c
                + h_sigma
                * np.sqrt(c_c * (2 - c_c) * mu_eff)
                * ((mean - old_mean) / sigma)
            )

            y = (selected_population - old_mean) / sigma

            rank_mu_update = np.zeros((dim, dim))

            for i in range(mu):
                rank_mu_update += weights[i] * np.outer(y[i], y[i])

            C = (
                (1 - c1 - c_mu) * C
                + c1 * np.outer(p_c, p_c)
                + c_mu * rank_mu_update
            )

            C = (C + C.T) / 2

            # Final covariance safeguard
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(C)):
                C = np.eye(dim)

            C = C + 1e-12 * np.eye(dim)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 3
            # ========================================================

            history["best_fitness"].append(best_fitness)
            history["sigma"].append(sigma)
            history["mean"].append(mean.copy())

        return best_solution, best_fitness
```

# ========================================================
# CMA-ES Variant: LLM-generated CMA-ES variant cycle 11 | Cycle 11
# ========================================================

## Metric:
Values represent error to the BBOB optimum. Lower is better.

## Algorithm Code:
```python
import numpy as np
import ioh


class LLM_CMAES:
    '''
    CMA-ES variant that injects a small fraction of heavy-tailed Student-t samples
    into the Gaussian offspring each generation. Injected Student-t directions are
    rescaled so their Euclidean norm matches the expected norm of a standard
    Gaussian draw (preserving directional heavy tails while limiting extreme radii).
    Additionally implements a stagnation-triggered temporary increase in the
    heavy-tail injection fraction; the magnitude of this boost is adaptive to the
    stagnation length (longer stagnation -> larger boost up to a cap).

    Cycle 11 change: Filter the influence of injected heavy-tailed offspring on
    adaptation: When computing evolution paths (p_sigma, p_c) and the rank-mu
    covariance update, injected Student-t offspring are excluded (zero contribution)
    from the adaptation statistics. They still participate in selection and
    mean recombination unchanged.
    '''
    def __init__(
            self,
            pop_size=20,
            num_iterations=100,
            sigma=0.5
        ):

        self.pop_size = pop_size
        self.num_iterations = num_iterations
        self.sigma = sigma

        # Hardcoded mixture parameters per EXPLAINABILITY_REPORT
        # Base injection fraction
        self._alpha_base = 0.10  # fraction of offspring to replace normally with Student-t

        # Adaptive tail-heaviness parameters (new: Cycle 10)
        # Base and minimum degrees of freedom for Student-t
        self._nu_base = 6      # moderate tail during normal progress
        self._nu_min = 2       # do not go below this (heavier tails)
        # Decrement nu by 1 every K extra stagnation generations during boost
        self._nu_k = 2

        # Stagnation-triggered adaptive injection parameters
        # The boost magnitude is now adaptive to how long stagnation has persisted:
        # alpha_boost = min(alpha_max, alpha_base + alpha_k * (stagnation_counter - S + 1))
        self._alpha_max = 0.30            # maximum allowed boost fraction
        self._alpha_k = 0.05              # increment per extra stagnation generation beyond threshold
        self._stagnation_threshold = 5    # S: consecutive generations without improvement to trigger boost
        self._alpha_boost_duration = 3    # T: number of generations to keep adaptive alpha during a boost

        self.variant_description = {
            "sampling": "Unchanged from parent algorithm.",
            "mean_update": "Unchanged from parent algorithm.",
            "adaptation": (
                "Modified: When computing evolution paths (p_sigma, p_c) and the rank-mu "
                "covariance update, ignore (zero-weight) any offspring that originated from "
                "injected Student-t heavy-tailed sampling. Injected samples still participate "
                "in selection and the mean recombination unchanged, but their contributions to "
                "p_sigma, p_c, and the rank-mu covariance update are filtered out to avoid "
                "skewing adaptation by rare heavy-tailed draws."
            )
        }

    def __call__(self, problem: ioh.problem.RealSingleObjective):

        dim = problem.meta_data.n_variables
        low_b, high_b = problem.bounds.lb[0], problem.bounds.ub[0]

        if self.pop_size is None:
            lambda_ = 4 + int(3 * np.log(dim))
        else:
            lambda_ = self.pop_size

        mu = lambda_ // 2

        weights = np.log(mu + 0.5) - np.log(np.arange(1, mu + 1))
        weights = weights / np.sum(weights)

        mu_eff = 1.0 / np.sum(weights ** 2)

        c_sigma = (mu_eff + 2) / (dim + mu_eff + 5)

        d_sigma = (
            1
            + 2 * max(0, np.sqrt((mu_eff - 1) / (dim + 1)) - 1)
            + c_sigma
        )

        c_c = (4 + mu_eff / dim) / (dim + 4 + 2 * mu_eff / dim)

        c1 = 2 / ((dim + 1.3) ** 2 + mu_eff)

        c_mu = min(
            1 - c1,
            2 * (mu_eff - 2 + 1 / mu_eff) / ((dim + 2) ** 2 + mu_eff)
        )

        expected_norm = np.sqrt(dim) * (
            1 - 1 / (4 * dim) + 1 / (21 * dim ** 2)
        )

        mean = np.random.uniform(low_b, high_b, dim)
        sigma = self.sigma * (high_b - low_b)

        C = np.eye(dim)

        p_sigma = np.zeros(dim)
        p_c = np.zeros(dim)

        best_solution = None
        best_fitness = np.inf

        history = {
            "best_fitness": [],
            "sigma": [],
            "mean": [],
            "stagnation_counter": 0,
            "restart_count": 0
        }

        # Runtime counter for remaining boosted generations
        boost_remaining = 0
        # Store the adaptive alpha used during an ongoing boost
        boost_alpha_current = None

        for iteration in range(self.num_iterations):

            # Numerical safeguard for covariance matrix
            C = (C + C.T) / 2

            if not np.all(np.isfinite(C)):
                C = np.eye(dim)

            C = C + 1e-12 * np.eye(dim)

            eigenvalues, B = np.linalg.eigh(C)
            eigenvalues = np.maximum(eigenvalues, 1e-20)

            D = np.sqrt(eigenvalues)
            BD = B @ np.diag(D)

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 1: Sampling (unchanged)
            # ========================================================

            # If stagnation counter reaches threshold and no boost is active, start a boost
            if history["stagnation_counter"] >= self._stagnation_threshold and boost_remaining == 0:
                # compute adaptive boost magnitude based on how long we've been stuck
                extra = history["stagnation_counter"] - self._stagnation_threshold + 1
                adaptive_alpha = self._alpha_base + self._alpha_k * extra
                adaptive_alpha = min(self._alpha_max, adaptive_alpha)
                boost_alpha_current = adaptive_alpha
                boost_remaining = self._alpha_boost_duration

            current_alpha = boost_alpha_current if boost_remaining > 0 and boost_alpha_current is not None else self._alpha_base

            # Determine current degrees of freedom for Student-t:
            if boost_remaining > 0 and boost_alpha_current is not None and history["stagnation_counter"] >= self._stagnation_threshold:
                extra = history["stagnation_counter"] - self._stagnation_threshold + 1
                decrements = extra // self._nu_k
                current_nu = max(self._nu_min, self._nu_base - int(decrements))
            else:
                current_nu = float(self._nu_base)

            # Standard Gaussian draws in isotropic coordinates
            z = np.random.randn(lambda_, dim)

            # Track which offspring are heavy-injected
            is_heavy = np.zeros(lambda_, dtype=bool)

            # Inject a fraction current_alpha of Student-t heavy-tailed draws
            if current_alpha > 0.0:
                n_heavy = int(np.ceil(current_alpha * lambda_))
                n_heavy = min(max(n_heavy, 0), lambda_)
                if n_heavy > 0:
                    heavy_idx = np.random.choice(lambda_, size=n_heavy, replace=False)
                    is_heavy[heavy_idx] = True
                    # Student-t draws with current_nu degrees of freedom, independent per coordinate
                    df = float(current_nu)
                    if df <= 0:
                        df = float(self._nu_min)
                    z_t = np.random.standard_t(df, size=(n_heavy, dim))
                    # Normalize each injected Student-t vector's norm to expected_norm
                    eps = 1e-12
                    norms = np.linalg.norm(z_t, axis=1)
                    scales = expected_norm / np.maximum(norms, eps)
                    z_t = z_t * scales[:, np.newaxis]
                    # Replace selected rows in z with normalized Student-t samples
                    z[heavy_idx] = z_t

            # After using one boosted generation, decrement remaining counter
            if boost_remaining > 0:
                boost_remaining -= 1
                if boost_remaining == 0:
                    boost_alpha_current = None

            # Transform into solution space using current covariance eigenbasis and scale
            population = mean + sigma * (z @ BD.T)

            # Numerical safeguard for candidate solutions
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(population)):
                population = np.nan_to_num(
                    population,
                    nan=0.0,
                    posinf=high_b,
                    neginf=low_b
                )

            population = np.clip(population, low_b, high_b)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 1
            # ========================================================

            fitness = np.array([problem(ind) for ind in population])

            sorted_indices = np.argsort(fitness)

            population = population[sorted_indices]
            z = z[sorted_indices]
            fitness = fitness[sorted_indices]
            is_heavy = is_heavy[sorted_indices]

            previous_best_fitness = best_fitness

            if fitness[0] < best_fitness:
                best_fitness = fitness[0]
                best_solution = population[0].copy()
                history["stagnation_counter"] = 0
                # Cancel any ongoing boost upon improvement
                boost_remaining = 0
                boost_alpha_current = None
            else:
                history["stagnation_counter"] += 1

            selected_population = population[:mu]
            selected_z = z[:mu]
            selected_is_heavy = is_heavy[:mu]

            old_mean = mean.copy()

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 2: Mean update (UNCHANGED)
            # ========================================================

            mean = np.sum(
                weights[:, np.newaxis] * selected_population,
                axis=0
            )

            mean = np.clip(mean, low_b, high_b)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 2
            # ========================================================

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 3: Adaptation (MODIFIED: filter heavy samples from adaptation)
            # ========================================================

            # Build adaptation-specific weights: zero out contributions from injected heavy samples,
            # then renormalize if any non-heavy remain. If all selected are heavy, fall back to original weights.
            adaptation_weights = weights.copy()
            if selected_is_heavy.any():
                adaptation_weights = adaptation_weights.copy()
                adaptation_weights[selected_is_heavy] = 0.0
                sum_aw = adaptation_weights.sum()
                if sum_aw > 0:
                    adaptation_weights = adaptation_weights / sum_aw
                else:
                    # fallback: no filtering if it would remove all weight
                    adaptation_weights = weights.copy()

            # Compute an adaptation-only recombination mean (used only in evolution path updates)
            mean_adapt = np.sum(
                adaptation_weights[:, np.newaxis] * selected_population,
                axis=0
            )

            C_inv_sqrt = B @ np.diag(1 / D) @ B.T

            # Use mean_adapt - old_mean to avoid heavy-sample-driven jumps in evolution paths
            delta_adapt = (mean_adapt - old_mean) / sigma

            p_sigma = (
                (1 - c_sigma) * p_sigma
                + np.sqrt(c_sigma * (2 - c_sigma) * mu_eff)
                * (C_inv_sqrt @ delta_adapt)
            )

            sigma_exponent = (
                (c_sigma / d_sigma)
                * (np.linalg.norm(p_sigma) / expected_norm - 1)
            )

            # Prevent overflow in the exponential step-size update
            sigma_exponent = np.clip(
                sigma_exponent,
                -50.0,
                50.0
            )

            sigma = sigma * np.exp(sigma_exponent)

            # Recover from numerical instability if sigma becomes NaN or Inf
            if not np.isfinite(sigma):
                sigma = self.sigma * (high_b - low_b)

            sigma = np.clip(
                sigma,
                1e-12,
                1e3 * (high_b - low_b)
            )

            norm_p_sigma = np.linalg.norm(p_sigma)

            h_sigma_condition = norm_p_sigma / np.sqrt(
                1 - (1 - c_sigma) ** (2 * (iteration + 1))
            ) < (1.4 + 2 / (dim + 1)) * expected_norm

            h_sigma = 1.0 if h_sigma_condition else 0.0

            p_c = (
                (1 - c_c) * p_c
                + h_sigma
                * np.sqrt(c_c * (2 - c_c) * mu_eff)
                * delta_adapt
            )

            # Compute rank-mu update but exclude heavy samples by using adaptation_weights
            y = (selected_population - old_mean) / sigma

            rank_mu_update = np.zeros((dim, dim))

            for i in range(mu):
                w_i = adaptation_weights[i]
                if w_i != 0.0:
                    rank_mu_update += w_i * np.outer(y[i], y[i])
                # if w_i == 0.0, injected heavy sample contributes nothing

            C = (
                (1 - c1 - c_mu) * C
                + c1 * np.outer(p_c, p_c)
                + c_mu * rank_mu_update
            )

            C = (C + C.T) / 2

            # Final covariance safeguard
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(C)):
                C = np.eye(dim)

            C = C + 1e-12 * np.eye(dim)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 3
            # ========================================================

            history["best_fitness"].append(best_fitness)
            history["sigma"].append(sigma)
            history["mean"].append(mean.copy())

        return best_solution, best_fitness
```
