# ========================================================
# CMA-ES Variant: LLM-generated CMA-ES variant cycle 12 | Cycle 12
# ========================================================

## Metric:
Values represent error to the BBOB optimum. Lower is better.

## Algorithm Code:
```python
import numpy as np
import ioh


class LLM_CMAES:
    '''
    CMA-ES variant that injects a small fraction of heavy-tailed Student-t samples
    into the Gaussian offspring each generation. Injected Student-t directions are
    rescaled so their Euclidean norm matches the expected norm of a standard
    Gaussian draw (preserving directional heavy tails while limiting extreme radii).
    Additionally implements a stagnation-triggered temporary increase in the
    heavy-tail injection fraction; the magnitude of this boost is adaptive to the
    stagnation length (longer stagnation -> larger boost up to a cap).

    New in Cycle 12: Soft attenuation of injected-offspring contributions to adaptation.
    Injected heavy-tailed offspring still fully influence selection and the mean recombination,
    but their contributions to the evolution paths and the rank-mu covariance update are
    multiplied by an attenuation factor alpha_att in (0,1) (default 0.5). After attenuation,
    adaptation weights are renormalized to sum to one; if attenuation would remove all mass,
    the original weights are used as a fallback.
    '''
    def __init__(
            self,
            pop_size=20,
            num_iterations=100,
            sigma=0.5
        ):

        self.pop_size = pop_size
        self.num_iterations = num_iterations
        self.sigma = sigma

        # Hardcoded mixture parameters per EXPLAINABILITY_REPORT
        # Base injection fraction
        self._alpha_base = 0.10  # fraction of offspring to replace normally with Student-t

        # Adaptive tail-heaviness parameters (new: Cycle 10)
        # Base and minimum degrees of freedom for Student-t
        self._nu_base = 6      # moderate tail during normal progress
        self._nu_min = 2       # do not go below this (heavier tails)
        # Decrement nu by 1 every K stagnation generations during boost
        self._nu_k = 2

        # Stagnation-triggered adaptive injection parameters
        # The boost magnitude is now adaptive to how long stagnation has persisted:
        # alpha_boost = min(alpha_max, alpha_base + alpha_k * (stagnation_counter - S + 1))
        self._alpha_max = 0.30            # maximum allowed boost fraction
        self._alpha_k = 0.05              # increment per extra stagnation generation beyond threshold
        self._stagnation_threshold = 5    # S: consecutive generations without improvement to trigger boost
        self._alpha_boost_duration = 3    # T: number of generations to keep adaptive alpha during a boost

        # New in Cycle 12: attenuation factor for adaptation contributions of heavy-tailed offspring
        # Value in (0,1); example starting value 0.5 as suggested in the report.
        self._alpha_att = 0.5

        self.variant_description = {
            "sampling": "Unchanged from parent algorithm.",
            "mean_update": "Unchanged from parent algorithm.",
            "adaptation": (
                "Modified: Soft attenuation of injected-offspring contributions to adaptation. "
                "For the recombination weights used in evolution-path updates (p_sigma, p_c) "
                "and the rank-mu covariance update, multiply the weights corresponding to "
                "selected offspring that originated from injected Student-t draws by alpha_att "
                "(default 0.5), then renormalize the attenuated weights to sum to one. "
                "Fallback to original weights if attenuation would zero the mass. "
                "Selection and mean recombination remain unchanged (heavy samples fully influence the mean)."
            )
        }

    def __call__(self, problem: ioh.problem.RealSingleObjective):

        dim = problem.meta_data.n_variables
        low_b, high_b = problem.bounds.lb[0], problem.bounds.ub[0]

        if self.pop_size is None:
            lambda_ = 4 + int(3 * np.log(dim))
        else:
            lambda_ = self.pop_size

        mu = lambda_ // 2

        weights = np.log(mu + 0.5) - np.log(np.arange(1, mu + 1))
        weights = weights / np.sum(weights)

        mu_eff = 1.0 / np.sum(weights ** 2)

        c_sigma = (mu_eff + 2) / (dim + mu_eff + 5)

        d_sigma = (
            1
            + 2 * max(0, np.sqrt((mu_eff - 1) / (dim + 1)) - 1)
            + c_sigma
        )

        c_c = (4 + mu_eff / dim) / (dim + 4 + 2 * mu_eff / dim)

        c1 = 2 / ((dim + 1.3) ** 2 + mu_eff)

        c_mu = min(
            1 - c1,
            2 * (mu_eff - 2 + 1 / mu_eff) / ((dim + 2) ** 2 + mu_eff)
        )

        expected_norm = np.sqrt(dim) * (
            1 - 1 / (4 * dim) + 1 / (21 * dim ** 2)
        )

        mean = np.random.uniform(low_b, high_b, dim)
        sigma = self.sigma * (high_b - low_b)

        C = np.eye(dim)

        p_sigma = np.zeros(dim)
        p_c = np.zeros(dim)

        best_solution = None
        best_fitness = np.inf

        history = {
            "best_fitness": [],
            "sigma": [],
            "mean": [],
            "stagnation_counter": 0,
            "restart_count": 0
        }

        # Runtime counter for remaining boosted generations
        boost_remaining = 0
        # Store the adaptive alpha used during an ongoing boost
        boost_alpha_current = None

        for iteration in range(self.num_iterations):

            # Numerical safeguard for covariance matrix
            C = (C + C.T) / 2

            if not np.all(np.isfinite(C)):
                C = np.eye(dim)

            C = C + 1e-12 * np.eye(dim)

            eigenvalues, B = np.linalg.eigh(C)
            eigenvalues = np.maximum(eigenvalues, 1e-20)

            D = np.sqrt(eigenvalues)
            BD = B @ np.diag(D)

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 1: Sampling (modified: adaptive alpha magnitude and adaptive nu)
            # ========================================================

            # If stagnation counter reaches threshold and no boost is active, start a boost
            if history["stagnation_counter"] >= self._stagnation_threshold and boost_remaining == 0:
                # compute adaptive boost magnitude based on how long we've been stuck
                extra = history["stagnation_counter"] - self._stagnation_threshold + 1
                adaptive_alpha = self._alpha_base + self._alpha_k * extra
                adaptive_alpha = min(self._alpha_max, adaptive_alpha)
                boost_alpha_current = adaptive_alpha
                boost_remaining = self._alpha_boost_duration

            current_alpha = boost_alpha_current if boost_remaining > 0 and boost_alpha_current is not None else self._alpha_base

            # Determine current degrees of freedom for Student-t:
            # Use nu_base during normal progress; during an active boost, make tails heavier
            # by decrementing nu by 1 every _nu_k extra stagnation generations (floored), down to nu_min.
            if boost_remaining > 0 and boost_alpha_current is not None and history["stagnation_counter"] >= self._stagnation_threshold:
                extra = history["stagnation_counter"] - self._stagnation_threshold + 1
                decrements = extra // self._nu_k
                current_nu = max(self._nu_min, self._nu_base - int(decrements))
            else:
                current_nu = float(self._nu_base)

            # Standard Gaussian draws in isotropic coordinates
            z = np.random.randn(lambda_, dim)

            # Track which samples were injected as heavy-tailed
            is_heavy = np.zeros(lambda_, dtype=bool)

            # Inject a fraction current_alpha of Student-t heavy-tailed draws
            if current_alpha > 0.0:
                n_heavy = int(np.ceil(current_alpha * lambda_))
                n_heavy = min(max(n_heavy, 0), lambda_)
                if n_heavy > 0:
                    heavy_idx = np.random.choice(lambda_, size=n_heavy, replace=False)
                    is_heavy[heavy_idx] = True
                    # Student-t draws with current_nu degrees of freedom, independent per coordinate
                    # numpy accepts float df, ensure it's positive
                    df = float(current_nu)
                    # Protect against invalid df values
                    if df <= 0:
                        df = float(self._nu_min)
                    z_t = np.random.standard_t(df, size=(n_heavy, dim))
                    # Normalize each injected Student-t vector's norm to expected_norm
                    eps = 1e-12
                    norms = np.linalg.norm(z_t, axis=1)
                    scales = expected_norm / np.maximum(norms, eps)
                    z_t = z_t * scales[:, np.newaxis]
                    # Replace selected rows in z with normalized Student-t samples
                    z[heavy_idx] = z_t

            # After using one boosted generation, decrement remaining counter
            if boost_remaining > 0:
                boost_remaining -= 1
                if boost_remaining == 0:
                    boost_alpha_current = None

            # Transform into solution space using current covariance eigenbasis and scale
            population = mean + sigma * (z @ BD.T)

            # Numerical safeguard for candidate solutions
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(population)):
                population = np.nan_to_num(
                    population,
                    nan=0.0,
                    posinf=high_b,
                    neginf=low_b
                )

            population = np.clip(population, low_b, high_b)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 1
            # ========================================================

            fitness = np.array([problem(ind) for ind in population])

            sorted_indices = np.argsort(fitness)

            population = population[sorted_indices]
            z = z[sorted_indices]
            is_heavy = is_heavy[sorted_indices]
            fitness = fitness[sorted_indices]

            previous_best_fitness = best_fitness

            if fitness[0] < best_fitness:
                best_fitness = fitness[0]
                best_solution = population[0].copy()
                history["stagnation_counter"] = 0
                # Cancel any ongoing boost upon improvement
                boost_remaining = 0
                boost_alpha_current = None
            else:
                history["stagnation_counter"] += 1

            selected_population = population[:mu]
            selected_z = z[:mu]
            selected_is_heavy = is_heavy[:mu]

            old_mean = mean.copy()

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 2: Mean update
            # ========================================================

            mean = np.sum(
                weights[:, np.newaxis] * selected_population,
                axis=0
            )

            mean = np.clip(mean, low_b, high_b)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 2
            # ========================================================

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 3: Adaptation
            # ========================================================
            # Soft attenuation of adaptation contributions from heavy-tailed selected offspring.
            # Create attenuated adaptation weights by scaling weights for selected heavy samples
            # by self._alpha_att, then renormalize. Fallback to original weights if attenuation
            # would zero the mass.
            atten = np.ones_like(weights)
            # For each selected index i (0..mu-1), if it is heavy, multiply atten[i] by alpha_att
            for i in range(mu):
                if selected_is_heavy[i]:
                    atten[i] = self._alpha_att
            weights_att = weights * atten
            sum_att = np.sum(weights_att)
            if sum_att <= 0:
                # fallback to original weights
                weights_att = weights.copy()
                mu_eff_att = mu_eff
            else:
                weights_att = weights_att / sum_att
                mu_eff_att = 1.0 / np.sum(weights_att ** 2)

            C_inv_sqrt = B @ np.diag(1 / D) @ B.T

            p_sigma = (
                (1 - c_sigma) * p_sigma
                + np.sqrt(c_sigma * (2 - c_sigma) * mu_eff_att)
                * (C_inv_sqrt @ ((mean - old_mean) / sigma))
            )

            sigma_exponent = (
                (c_sigma / d_sigma)
                * (np.linalg.norm(p_sigma) / expected_norm - 1)
            )

            # Prevent overflow in the exponential step-size update
            sigma_exponent = np.clip(
                sigma_exponent,
                -50.0,
                50.0
            )

            sigma = sigma * np.exp(sigma_exponent)

            # Recover from numerical instability if sigma becomes NaN or Inf
            if not np.isfinite(sigma):
                sigma = self.sigma * (high_b - low_b)

            sigma = np.clip(
                sigma,
                1e-12,
                1e3 * (high_b - low_b)
            )

            norm_p_sigma = np.linalg.norm(p_sigma)

            h_sigma_condition = norm_p_sigma / np.sqrt(
                1 - (1 - c_sigma) ** (2 * (iteration + 1))
            ) < (1.4 + 2 / (dim + 1)) * expected_norm

            h_sigma = 1.0 if h_sigma_condition else 0.0

            p_c = (
                (1 - c_c) * p_c
                + h_sigma
                * np.sqrt(c_c * (2 - c_c) * mu_eff_att)
                * ((mean - old_mean) / sigma)
            )

            y = (selected_population - old_mean) / sigma

            rank_mu_update = np.zeros((dim, dim))

            for i in range(mu):
                rank_mu_update += weights_att[i] * np.outer(y[i], y[i])

            C = (
                (1 - c1 - c_mu) * C
                + c1 * np.outer(p_c, p_c)
                + c_mu * rank_mu_update
            )

            C = (C + C.T) / 2

            # Final covariance safeguard
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(C)):
                C = np.eye(dim)

            C = C + 1e-12 * np.eye(dim)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 3
            # ========================================================

            history["best_fitness"].append(best_fitness)
            history["sigma"].append(sigma)
            history["mean"].append(mean.copy())

        return best_solution, best_fitness
```

# ========================================================
# CMA-ES Variant: LLM-generated CMA-ES variant cycle 13 | Cycle 13
# ========================================================

## Metric:
Values represent error to the BBOB optimum. Lower is better.

## Algorithm Code:
```python
import numpy as np
import ioh


class LLM_CMAES:
    '''
    CMA-ES variant that injects a small fraction of heavy-tailed Student-t samples
    into the Gaussian offspring each generation. Injected Student-t directions are
    rescaled so their Euclidean norm matches the expected norm of a standard
    Gaussian draw (preserving directional heavy tails while limiting extreme radii).
    Additionally implements a stagnation-triggered temporary increase in the
    heavy-tail injection fraction; the magnitude of this boost is adaptive to the
    stagnation length (longer stagnation -> larger boost up to a cap).

    New in Cycle 13: Robust adaptation-mean recombination. For adaptation-only
    (evolution paths and rank-mu covariance update), a coordinate-wise winsorized
    mean is computed across the mu selected offspring: values outside the 10th/90th
    percentile per-coordinate are clipped to the respective percentile boundary,
    and the weighted recombination is computed on these winsorized coordinates.
    This reduces the influence of extreme coordinate-wise outliers from heavy-tailed
    samples on the adaptation machinery while preserving the original weighted
    recombination for selection and best-solution bookkeeping.
    '''
    def __init__(
            self,
            pop_size=20,
            num_iterations=100,
            sigma=0.5
        ):

        self.pop_size = pop_size
        self.num_iterations = num_iterations
        self.sigma = sigma

        # Hardcoded mixture parameters per EXPLAINABILITY_REPORT
        # Base injection fraction
        self._alpha_base = 0.10  # fraction of offspring to replace normally with Student-t

        # Adaptive tail-heaviness parameters (new: Cycle 10)
        # Base and minimum degrees of freedom for Student-t
        self._nu_base = 6      # moderate tail during normal progress
        self._nu_min = 2       # do not go below this (heavier tails)
        # Decrement nu by 1 every K extra stagnation generations during boost
        self._nu_k = 2

        # Stagnation-triggered adaptive injection parameters
        # The boost magnitude is now adaptive to how long stagnation has persisted:
        # alpha_boost = min(alpha_max, alpha_base + alpha_k * (stagnation_counter - S + 1))
        self._alpha_max = 0.30            # maximum allowed boost fraction
        self._alpha_k = 0.05              # increment per extra stagnation generation beyond threshold
        self._stagnation_threshold = 5    # S: consecutive generations without improvement to trigger boost
        self._alpha_boost_duration = 3    # T: number of generations to keep adaptive alpha during a boost

        self.variant_description = {
            "sampling": (
                "Unchanged from parent algorithm: Adaptive heavy-tail injection fraction and adaptive tail heaviness. "
                "Normally use alpha_base=0.10 of offspring replaced by multivariate Student-t draws. "
                "If no best-so-far improvement is observed for S=5 consecutive generations, start "
                "a boost of fixed duration T=3 generations. The boost magnitude is adaptive to "
                "stagnation length: alpha_boost = min(alpha_max, alpha_base + alpha_k * (stagnation_counter - S + 1)). "
                "Additionally, during the same boost window, the Student-t degrees-of-freedom nu is reduced "
                "to make tails heavier: nu = max(nu_min, nu_base - floor((stagnation_counter - S + 1)/K)), "
                "with nu_base=6, nu_min=2, and K controlling the decrement pace. Injected Student-t vectors "
                "are rescaled so their Euclidean norm equals the expected norm of a standard Gaussian draw. "
                "Injected offspring replace existing ones within the lambda budget; no extra evaluations are performed."
            ),
            "mean_update": (
                "Modified: For adaptation only, compute a coordinate-wise winsorized mean from the mu selected offspring. "
                "Per-coordinate values outside the 10th/90th percentiles are clipped to those percentile boundaries, "
                "and the weighted recombination is evaluated on these winsorized coordinates to form the adaptation-mean. "
                "The original weighted recombination mean is retained for selection and best-solution bookkeeping. "
                "If clipping would remove all variability (degenerate percentiles), the algorithm falls back to the original weighted recombination for adaptation."
            ),
            "adaptation": "Unchanged from parent algorithm except that p_sigma, p_c, and the rank-mu update use the winsorized adaptation-mean described under mean_update."
        }

    def __call__(self, problem: ioh.problem.RealSingleObjective):

        dim = problem.meta_data.n_variables
        low_b, high_b = problem.bounds.lb[0], problem.bounds.ub[0]

        if self.pop_size is None:
            lambda_ = 4 + int(3 * np.log(dim))
        else:
            lambda_ = self.pop_size

        mu = lambda_ // 2

        weights = np.log(mu + 0.5) - np.log(np.arange(1, mu + 1))
        weights = weights / np.sum(weights)

        mu_eff = 1.0 / np.sum(weights ** 2)

        c_sigma = (mu_eff + 2) / (dim + mu_eff + 5)

        d_sigma = (
            1
            + 2 * max(0, np.sqrt((mu_eff - 1) / (dim + 1)) - 1)
            + c_sigma
        )

        c_c = (4 + mu_eff / dim) / (dim + 4 + 2 * mu_eff / dim)

        c1 = 2 / ((dim + 1.3) ** 2 + mu_eff)

        c_mu = min(
            1 - c1,
            2 * (mu_eff - 2 + 1 / mu_eff) / ((dim + 2) ** 2 + mu_eff)
        )

        expected_norm = np.sqrt(dim) * (
            1 - 1 / (4 * dim) + 1 / (21 * dim ** 2)
        )

        mean = np.random.uniform(low_b, high_b, dim)
        sigma = self.sigma * (high_b - low_b)

        C = np.eye(dim)

        p_sigma = np.zeros(dim)
        p_c = np.zeros(dim)

        best_solution = None
        best_fitness = np.inf

        history = {
            "best_fitness": [],
            "sigma": [],
            "mean": [],
            "stagnation_counter": 0,
            "restart_count": 0
        }

        # Runtime counter for remaining boosted generations
        boost_remaining = 0
        # Store the adaptive alpha used during an ongoing boost
        boost_alpha_current = None

        for iteration in range(self.num_iterations):

            # Numerical safeguard for covariance matrix
            C = (C + C.T) / 2

            if not np.all(np.isfinite(C)):
                C = np.eye(dim)

            C = C + 1e-12 * np.eye(dim)

            eigenvalues, B = np.linalg.eigh(C)
            eigenvalues = np.maximum(eigenvalues, 1e-20)

            D = np.sqrt(eigenvalues)
            BD = B @ np.diag(D)

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 1: Sampling (modified: adaptive alpha magnitude and adaptive nu)
            # ========================================================

            # If stagnation counter reaches threshold and no boost is active, start a boost
            if history["stagnation_counter"] >= self._stagnation_threshold and boost_remaining == 0:
                # compute adaptive boost magnitude based on how long we've been stuck
                extra = history["stagnation_counter"] - self._stagnation_threshold + 1
                adaptive_alpha = self._alpha_base + self._alpha_k * extra
                adaptive_alpha = min(self._alpha_max, adaptive_alpha)
                boost_alpha_current = adaptive_alpha
                boost_remaining = self._alpha_boost_duration

            current_alpha = boost_alpha_current if boost_remaining > 0 and boost_alpha_current is not None else self._alpha_base

            # Determine current degrees of freedom for Student-t:
            # Use nu_base during normal progress; during an active boost, make tails heavier
            # by decrementing nu by 1 every _nu_k extra stagnation generations (floored), down to nu_min.
            if boost_remaining > 0 and boost_alpha_current is not None and history["stagnation_counter"] >= self._stagnation_threshold:
                extra = history["stagnation_counter"] - self._stagnation_threshold + 1
                decrements = extra // self._nu_k
                current_nu = max(self._nu_min, self._nu_base - int(decrements))
            else:
                current_nu = float(self._nu_base)

            # Standard Gaussian draws in isotropic coordinates
            z = np.random.randn(lambda_, dim)

            # Inject a fraction current_alpha of Student-t heavy-tailed draws
            if current_alpha > 0.0:
                n_heavy = int(np.ceil(current_alpha * lambda_))
                n_heavy = min(max(n_heavy, 0), lambda_)
                if n_heavy > 0:
                    heavy_idx = np.random.choice(lambda_, size=n_heavy, replace=False)
                    # Student-t draws with current_nu degrees of freedom, independent per coordinate
                    # numpy accepts float df, ensure it's positive
                    df = float(current_nu)
                    # Protect against invalid df values
                    if df <= 0:
                        df = float(self._nu_min)
                    z_t = np.random.standard_t(df, size=(n_heavy, dim))
                    # Normalize each injected Student-t vector's norm to expected_norm
                    eps = 1e-12
                    norms = np.linalg.norm(z_t, axis=1)
                    scales = expected_norm / np.maximum(norms, eps)
                    z_t = z_t * scales[:, np.newaxis]
                    # Replace selected rows in z with normalized Student-t samples
                    z[heavy_idx] = z_t

            # After using one boosted generation, decrement remaining counter
            if boost_remaining > 0:
                boost_remaining -= 1
                if boost_remaining == 0:
                    boost_alpha_current = None

            # Transform into solution space using current covariance eigenbasis and scale
            population = mean + sigma * (z @ BD.T)

            # Numerical safeguard for candidate solutions
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(population)):
                population = np.nan_to_num(
                    population,
                    nan=0.0,
                    posinf=high_b,
                    neginf=low_b
                )

            population = np.clip(population, low_b, high_b)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 1
            # ========================================================

            fitness = np.array([problem(ind) for ind in population])

            sorted_indices = np.argsort(fitness)

            population = population[sorted_indices]
            z = z[sorted_indices]
            fitness = fitness[sorted_indices]

            previous_best_fitness = best_fitness

            if fitness[0] < best_fitness:
                best_fitness = fitness[0]
                best_solution = population[0].copy()
                history["stagnation_counter"] = 0
                # Cancel any ongoing boost upon improvement
                boost_remaining = 0
                boost_alpha_current = None
            else:
                history["stagnation_counter"] += 1

            selected_population = population[:mu]
            selected_z = z[:mu]

            old_mean = mean.copy()

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 2: Mean update (modified: winsorized adaptation-mean for adaptation only)
            # ========================================================

            # Original weighted recombination mean used for selection and bookkeeping
            mean = np.sum(
                weights[:, np.newaxis] * selected_population,
                axis=0
            )

            mean = np.clip(mean, low_b, high_b)

            # Compute a coordinate-wise winsorized mean across the mu selected offspring for adaptation only.
            # Clip per-coordinate values outside the 10th/90th percentiles to percentile boundaries,
            # then compute the weighted recombination mean on the winsorized samples.
            # Fallback: if clipping would remove all variability (i.e., p10 == p90 for all coords), revert to original weighted mean.
            adaptation_mean = mean.copy()
            try:
                if selected_population.shape[0] > 0:
                    p10 = np.percentile(selected_population, 10, axis=0)
                    p90 = np.percentile(selected_population, 90, axis=0)
                    # Check degenerate case: all percentiles equal across all coordinates
                    if not np.allclose(p10, p90):
                        winsorized = np.clip(selected_population, p10, p90)
                        # If winsorization removed all variability across ALL coordinates (extreme degenerate), fallback.
                        if not np.allclose(np.std(winsorized, axis=0), 0.0):
                            adaptation_mean = np.sum(weights[:, np.newaxis] * winsorized, axis=0)
                        else:
                            # If winsorized is degenerate across all coords, fallback to original mean (already set)
                            adaptation_mean = mean.copy()
                    else:
                        adaptation_mean = mean.copy()
                else:
                    adaptation_mean = mean.copy()
            except Exception:
                # In any unexpected numerical issue, fallback to original mean
                adaptation_mean = mean.copy()

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 2
            # ========================================================

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 3: Adaptation (use adaptation_mean for path and rank-mu updates)
            # ========================================================

            C_inv_sqrt = B @ np.diag(1 / D) @ B.T

            p_sigma = (
                (1 - c_sigma) * p_sigma
                + np.sqrt(c_sigma * (2 - c_sigma) * mu_eff)
                * (C_inv_sqrt @ ((adaptation_mean - old_mean) / sigma))
            )

            sigma_exponent = (
                (c_sigma / d_sigma)
                * (np.linalg.norm(p_sigma) / expected_norm - 1)
            )

            # Prevent overflow in the exponential step-size update
            sigma_exponent = np.clip(
                sigma_exponent,
                -50.0,
                50.0
            )

            sigma = sigma * np.exp(sigma_exponent)

            # Recover from numerical instability if sigma becomes NaN or Inf
            if not np.isfinite(sigma):
                sigma = self.sigma * (high_b - low_b)

            sigma = np.clip(
                sigma,
                1e-12,
                1e3 * (high_b - low_b)
            )

            norm_p_sigma = np.linalg.norm(p_sigma)

            h_sigma_condition = norm_p_sigma / np.sqrt(
                1 - (1 - c_sigma) ** (2 * (iteration + 1))
            ) < (1.4 + 2 / (dim + 1)) * expected_norm

            h_sigma = 1.0 if h_sigma_condition else 0.0

            p_c = (
                (1 - c_c) * p_c
                + h_sigma
                * np.sqrt(c_c * (2 - c_c) * mu_eff)
                * ((adaptation_mean - old_mean) / sigma)
            )

            # Use adaptation_mean in computing deviations for the rank-mu update
            y = (selected_population - adaptation_mean) / sigma

            rank_mu_update = np.zeros((dim, dim))

            for i in range(mu):
                rank_mu_update += weights[i] * np.outer(y[i], y[i])

            C = (
                (1 - c1 - c_mu) * C
                + c1 * np.outer(p_c, p_c)
                + c_mu * rank_mu_update
            )

            C = (C + C.T) / 2

            # Final covariance safeguard
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(C)):
                C = np.eye(dim)

            C = C + 1e-12 * np.eye(dim)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 3
            # ========================================================

            history["best_fitness"].append(best_fitness)
            history["sigma"].append(sigma)
            history["mean"].append(mean.copy())

        return best_solution, best_fitness
```

## BBOB Error Results:
 Function_ID      Function_Name    Avg_Error    Std_Error
           1             Sphere 0.000000e+00 0.000000e+00

# ========================================================
# CMA-ES Variant: LLM-generated CMA-ES variant cycle 14 | Cycle 14
# ========================================================

## Metric:
Values represent error to the BBOB optimum. Lower is better.

## Algorithm Code:
```python
import numpy as np
import ioh


class LLM_CMAES:
    '''
    CMA-ES variant that injects a small fraction of heavy-tailed Student-t samples
    into the Gaussian offspring each generation. Injected Student-t directions are
    rescaled so their Euclidean norm matches the expected norm of a standard
    Gaussian draw (preserving directional heavy tails while limiting extreme radii).
    Additionally implements a stagnation-triggered temporary increase in the
    heavy-tail injection fraction; the magnitude of this boost is adaptive to the
    stagnation length (longer stagnation -> larger boost up to a cap).

    New in Cycle 10: Adaptive tail heaviness. The degrees-of-freedom (nu) of the
    Student-t used for injected samples is nu_base during normal progress and,
    when a stagnation-triggered boost is active, is reduced (heavier tails) by
    1 every K stagnation generations down to nu_min. This is applied only during
    the same boost window used by the adaptive alpha.

    Cycle 14 change: Soft attenuation of adaptation contributions from injected
    heavy-tailed selected offspring. Adaptation weights corresponding to selected
    heavy samples are multiplied by alpha_att in (0,1) and then renormalized
    before computing p_sigma, p_c and the rank-mu covariance update. If the
    attenuation would zero the adaptation mass, revert to original weights.
    '''
    def __init__(
            self,
            pop_size=20,
            num_iterations=100,
            sigma=0.5
        ):

        self.pop_size = pop_size
        self.num_iterations = num_iterations
        self.sigma = sigma

        # Hardcoded mixture parameters per EXPLAINABILITY_REPORT
        # Base injection fraction
        self._alpha_base = 0.10  # fraction of offspring to replace normally with Student-t

        # Adaptive tail-heaviness parameters (new: Cycle 10)
        # Base and minimum degrees of freedom for Student-t
        self._nu_base = 6      # moderate tail during normal progress
        self._nu_min = 2       # do not go below this (heavier tails)
        # Decrement nu by 1 every K extra stagnation generations during boost
        self._nu_k = 2

        # Stagnation-triggered adaptive injection parameters
        # The boost magnitude is now adaptive to how long stagnation has persisted:
        # alpha_boost = min(alpha_max, alpha_base + alpha_k * (stagnation_counter - S + 1))
        self._alpha_max = 0.30            # maximum allowed boost fraction
        self._alpha_k = 0.05              # increment per extra stagnation generation beyond threshold
        self._stagnation_threshold = 5    # S: consecutive generations without improvement to trigger boost
        self._alpha_boost_duration = 3    # T: number of generations to keep adaptive alpha during a boost

        # New in Cycle 14: attenuation factor for adaptation contributions of heavy samples
        # Multiply adaptation weights of selected heavy samples by this factor, then renormalize.
        # Safe fallback: if sum becomes effectively zero, revert to original weights.
        self._alpha_att = 0.5

        self.variant_description = {
            "sampling": (
                "Modified: Adaptive heavy-tail injection fraction and adaptive tail heaviness. "
                "Normally use alpha_base=0.10 of offspring replaced by multivariate Student-t draws. "
                "If no best-so-far improvement is observed for S=5 consecutive generations, start "
                "a boost of fixed duration T=3 generations. The boost magnitude is adaptive to "
                "stagnation length: alpha_boost = min(alpha_max, alpha_base + alpha_k * (stagnation_counter - S + 1)). "
                "Additionally, during the same boost window, the Student-t degrees-of-freedom nu is reduced "
                "to make tails heavier: nu = max(nu_min, nu_base - floor((stagnation_counter - S + 1)/K)), "
                "with nu_base=6, nu_min=2, and K controlling the decrement pace. Injected Student-t vectors "
                "are rescaled so their Euclidean norm equals the expected norm of a standard Gaussian draw. "
                "Injected offspring replace existing ones within the lambda budget; no extra evaluations are performed."
            ),
            "mean_update": "Unchanged from parent algorithm.",
            "adaptation": (
                "Modified: Soft attenuation of adaptation contributions from injected heavy-tailed selected offspring. "
                "For adaptation (p_sigma, p_c, and rank-mu covariance update), recombination weights corresponding "
                "to selected offspring that originated from injected Student-t draws are multiplied by alpha_att=0.5 "
                "and then renormalized. If attenuation would zero total adaptation mass, revert to original weights. "
                "Selection and mean update remain unchanged (heavy samples still fully influence selection)."
            )
        }

    def __call__(self, problem: ioh.problem.RealSingleObjective):

        dim = problem.meta_data.n_variables
        low_b, high_b = problem.bounds.lb[0], problem.bounds.ub[0]

        if self.pop_size is None:
            lambda_ = 4 + int(3 * np.log(dim))
        else:
            lambda_ = self.pop_size

        mu = lambda_ // 2

        weights = np.log(mu + 0.5) - np.log(np.arange(1, mu + 1))
        weights = weights / np.sum(weights)

        mu_eff = 1.0 / np.sum(weights ** 2)

        c_sigma = (mu_eff + 2) / (dim + mu_eff + 5)

        d_sigma = (
            1
            + 2 * max(0, np.sqrt((mu_eff - 1) / (dim + 1)) - 1)
            + c_sigma
        )

        c_c = (4 + mu_eff / dim) / (dim + 4 + 2 * mu_eff / dim)

        c1 = 2 / ((dim + 1.3) ** 2 + mu_eff)

        c_mu = min(
            1 - c1,
            2 * (mu_eff - 2 + 1 / mu_eff) / ((dim + 2) ** 2 + mu_eff)
        )

        expected_norm = np.sqrt(dim) * (
            1 - 1 / (4 * dim) + 1 / (21 * dim ** 2)
        )

        mean = np.random.uniform(low_b, high_b, dim)
        sigma = self.sigma * (high_b - low_b)

        C = np.eye(dim)

        p_sigma = np.zeros(dim)
        p_c = np.zeros(dim)

        best_solution = None
        best_fitness = np.inf

        history = {
            "best_fitness": [],
            "sigma": [],
            "mean": [],
            "stagnation_counter": 0,
            "restart_count": 0
        }

        # Runtime counter for remaining boosted generations
        boost_remaining = 0
        # Store the adaptive alpha used during an ongoing boost
        boost_alpha_current = None

        for iteration in range(self.num_iterations):

            # Numerical safeguard for covariance matrix
            C = (C + C.T) / 2

            if not np.all(np.isfinite(C)):
                C = np.eye(dim)

            C = C + 1e-12 * np.eye(dim)

            eigenvalues, B = np.linalg.eigh(C)
            eigenvalues = np.maximum(eigenvalues, 1e-20)

            D = np.sqrt(eigenvalues)
            BD = B @ np.diag(D)

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 1: Sampling (modified: adaptive alpha magnitude and adaptive nu)
            # ========================================================

            # If stagnation counter reaches threshold and no boost is active, start a boost
            if history["stagnation_counter"] >= self._stagnation_threshold and boost_remaining == 0:
                # compute adaptive boost magnitude based on how long we've been stuck
                extra = history["stagnation_counter"] - self._stagnation_threshold + 1
                adaptive_alpha = self._alpha_base + self._alpha_k * extra
                adaptive_alpha = min(self._alpha_max, adaptive_alpha)
                boost_alpha_current = adaptive_alpha
                boost_remaining = self._alpha_boost_duration

            current_alpha = boost_alpha_current if boost_remaining > 0 and boost_alpha_current is not None else self._alpha_base

            # Determine current degrees of freedom for Student-t:
            # Use nu_base during normal progress; during an active boost, make tails heavier
            # by decrementing nu by 1 every _nu_k extra stagnation generations (floored), down to nu_min.
            if boost_remaining > 0 and boost_alpha_current is not None and history["stagnation_counter"] >= self._stagnation_threshold:
                extra = history["stagnation_counter"] - self._stagnation_threshold + 1
                decrements = extra // self._nu_k
                current_nu = max(self._nu_min, self._nu_base - int(decrements))
            else:
                current_nu = float(self._nu_base)

            # Standard Gaussian draws in isotropic coordinates
            z = np.random.randn(lambda_, dim)

            # Track which samples are heavy-injected
            is_heavy = np.zeros(lambda_, dtype=bool)

            # Inject a fraction current_alpha of Student-t heavy-tailed draws
            if current_alpha > 0.0:
                n_heavy = int(np.ceil(current_alpha * lambda_))
                n_heavy = min(max(n_heavy, 0), lambda_)
                if n_heavy > 0:
                    heavy_idx = np.random.choice(lambda_, size=n_heavy, replace=False)
                    is_heavy[heavy_idx] = True
                    # Student-t draws with current_nu degrees of freedom, independent per coordinate
                    df = float(current_nu)
                    if df <= 0:
                        df = float(self._nu_min)
                    z_t = np.random.standard_t(df, size=(n_heavy, dim))
                    # Normalize each injected Student-t vector's norm to expected_norm
                    eps = 1e-12
                    norms = np.linalg.norm(z_t, axis=1)
                    scales = expected_norm / np.maximum(norms, eps)
                    z_t = z_t * scales[:, np.newaxis]
                    # Replace selected rows in z with normalized Student-t samples
                    z[heavy_idx] = z_t

            # After using one boosted generation, decrement remaining counter
            if boost_remaining > 0:
                boost_remaining -= 1
                if boost_remaining == 0:
                    boost_alpha_current = None

            # Transform into solution space using current covariance eigenbasis and scale
            population = mean + sigma * (z @ BD.T)

            # Numerical safeguard for candidate solutions
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(population)):
                population = np.nan_to_num(
                    population,
                    nan=0.0,
                    posinf=high_b,
                    neginf=low_b
                )

            population = np.clip(population, low_b, high_b)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 1
            # ========================================================

            fitness = np.array([problem(ind) for ind in population])

            sorted_indices = np.argsort(fitness)

            population = population[sorted_indices]
            z = z[sorted_indices]
            fitness = fitness[sorted_indices]
            is_heavy = is_heavy[sorted_indices]

            previous_best_fitness = best_fitness

            if fitness[0] < best_fitness:
                best_fitness = fitness[0]
                best_solution = population[0].copy()
                history["stagnation_counter"] = 0
                # Cancel any ongoing boost upon improvement
                boost_remaining = 0
                boost_alpha_current = None
            else:
                history["stagnation_counter"] += 1

            selected_population = population[:mu]
            selected_z = z[:mu]
            selected_is_heavy = is_heavy[:mu]

            old_mean = mean.copy()

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 2: Mean update
            # ========================================================

            mean = np.sum(
                weights[:, np.newaxis] * selected_population,
                axis=0
            )

            mean = np.clip(mean, low_b, high_b)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 2
            # ========================================================

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 3: Adaptation
            # ========================================================

            C_inv_sqrt = B @ np.diag(1 / D) @ B.T

            # Prepare attenuated weights for adaptation only (soft attenuation of heavy-selected samples)
            weights_adapt = weights.copy()
            # Multiply weights corresponding to selected heavy samples by alpha_att
            for i in range(mu):
                if selected_is_heavy[i]:
                    weights_adapt[i] = weights_adapt[i] * self._alpha_att
            sum_weights_adapt = np.sum(weights_adapt)
            # Safe fallback: if attenuation zeros total adaptation mass, revert to original weights
            if sum_weights_adapt <= 1e-12:
                weights_adapt = weights.copy()
                sum_weights_adapt = np.sum(weights_adapt)
            # Renormalize adaptation weights to sum to 1
            weights_adapt = weights_adapt / sum_weights_adapt
            mu_eff_adapt = 1.0 / np.sum(weights_adapt ** 2)

            p_sigma = (
                (1 - c_sigma) * p_sigma
                + np.sqrt(c_sigma * (2 - c_sigma) * mu_eff_adapt)
                * (C_inv_sqrt @ ((mean - old_mean) / sigma))
            )

            sigma_exponent = (
                (c_sigma / d_sigma)
                * (np.linalg.norm(p_sigma) / expected_norm - 1)
            )

            # Prevent overflow in the exponential step-size update
            sigma_exponent = np.clip(
                sigma_exponent,
                -50.0,
                50.0
            )

            sigma = sigma * np.exp(sigma_exponent)

            # Recover from numerical instability if sigma becomes NaN or Inf
            if not np.isfinite(sigma):
                sigma = self.sigma * (high_b - low_b)

            sigma = np.clip(
                sigma,
                1e-12,
                1e3 * (high_b - low_b)
            )

            norm_p_sigma = np.linalg.norm(p_sigma)

            h_sigma_condition = norm_p_sigma / np.sqrt(
                1 - (1 - c_sigma) ** (2 * (iteration + 1))
            ) < (1.4 + 2 / (dim + 1)) * expected_norm

            h_sigma = 1.0 if h_sigma_condition else 0.0

            p_c = (
                (1 - c_c) * p_c
                + h_sigma
                * np.sqrt(c_c * (2 - c_c) * mu_eff_adapt)
                * ((mean - old_mean) / sigma)
            )

            y = (selected_population - old_mean) / sigma

            rank_mu_update = np.zeros((dim, dim))

            for i in range(mu):
                rank_mu_update += weights_adapt[i] * np.outer(y[i], y[i])

            C = (
                (1 - c1 - c_mu) * C
                + c1 * np.outer(p_c, p_c)
                + c_mu * rank_mu_update
            )

            C = (C + C.T) / 2

            # Final covariance safeguard
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(C)):
                C = np.eye(dim)

            C = C + 1e-12 * np.eye(dim)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 3
            # ========================================================

            history["best_fitness"].append(best_fitness)
            history["sigma"].append(sigma)
            history["mean"].append(mean.copy())

        return best_solution, best_fitness
```

          15   RastriginRotated       1.376358e+01       5.431911e+00        1.316660e+01        6.431054e+00     5.969793e-01               1.045340e+00              WORSE

# ========================================================
# CMA-ES Variant: LLM-generated CMA-ES variant cycle 15 | Cycle 15
# ========================================================

## Metric:
Values represent error to the BBOB optimum. Lower is better.

## Algorithm Code:
```python
import numpy as np
import ioh


class LLM_CMAES:
    '''
    CMA-ES variant that injects a small fraction of heavy-tailed Student-t samples
    into the Gaussian offspring each generation. Injected Student-t directions are
    rescaled so their Euclidean norm matches the expected norm of a standard
    Gaussian draw (preserving directional heavy tails while limiting extreme radii).
    Additionally implements a stagnation-triggered temporary increase in the
    heavy-tail injection fraction; the magnitude of this boost is adaptive to the
    stagnation length (longer stagnation -> larger boost up to a cap).

    New in Cycle 10: Adaptive tail heaviness. The degrees-of-freedom (nu) of the
    Student-t used for injected samples is nu_base during normal progress and,
    when a stagnation-triggered boost is active, is reduced (heavier tails) by
    1 every K stagnation generations down to nu_min. This is applied only during
    the same boost window used by the adaptive alpha.

    Cycle 15 addition: Transient, stagnation-triggered population-size increase
    (lambda boost) that temporarily samples more offspring for T generations
    when stagnation is detected. The total evaluation budget is preserved by
    shortening the remaining number of generations proportionally so that no
    extra objective evaluations occur overall.
    '''
    def __init__(
            self,
            pop_size=20,
            num_iterations=100,
            sigma=0.5
        ):

        self.pop_size = pop_size
        self.num_iterations = num_iterations
        self.sigma = sigma

        # Hardcoded mixture parameters per EXPLAINABILITY_REPORT
        # Base injection fraction
        self._alpha_base = 0.10  # fraction of offspring to replace normally with Student-t

        # Adaptive tail-heaviness parameters (new: Cycle 10)
        # Base and minimum degrees of freedom for Student-t
        self._nu_base = 6      # moderate tail during normal progress
        self._nu_min = 2       # do not go below this (heavier tails)
        # Decrement nu by 1 every K stagnation generations during boost
        self._nu_k = 2

        # Stagnation-triggered adaptive injection parameters
        # The boost magnitude is now adaptive to how long stagnation has persisted:
        # alpha_boost = min(alpha_max, alpha_base + alpha_k * (stagnation_counter - S + 1))
        self._alpha_max = 0.30            # maximum allowed boost fraction
        self._alpha_k = 0.05              # increment per extra stagnation generation beyond threshold
        self._stagnation_threshold = 5    # S: consecutive generations without improvement to trigger boost
        self._alpha_boost_duration = 3    # T: number of generations to keep adaptive alpha during a boost

        # Cycle 15: transient population-size increase parameters
        # Multiply lambda by this factor during a boost window (e.g., 1.5x-2x suggested)
        self._lambda_boost_factor = 1.8

        self.variant_description = {
            "sampling": (
                "Modified: Adaptive heavy-tail injection fraction and adaptive tail heaviness, "
                "plus a transient, stagnation-triggered population-size (lambda) increase. "
                "Normally use alpha_base=0.10 of offspring replaced by multivariate Student-t draws. "
                "If no best-so-far improvement is observed for S=5 consecutive generations, start "
                "a boost of fixed duration T=3 generations. The heavy-tail injection boost magnitude "
                "is adaptive to stagnation length: alpha_boost = min(alpha_max, alpha_base + alpha_k * (stagnation_counter - S + 1)). "
                "During the same boost window, the Student-t degrees-of-freedom nu is reduced to make tails heavier: "
                "nu = max(nu_min, nu_base - floor((stagnation_counter - S + 1)/K)). "
                "Additionally (Cycle 15), start a transient lambda increase during the boost window by a factor "
                "lambda_factor=_lambda_boost_factor (rounded up to integer offspring). The total evaluation budget "
                "(lambda_base * num_iterations) is preserved by shortening remaining generations proportionally so that "
                "no extra objective evaluations are performed. Injected Student-t vectors are rescaled so their Euclidean "
                "norm equals the expected norm of a standard Gaussian draw. Injected offspring replace existing ones "
                "within the per-generation budget; no extra evaluations are performed."
            ),
            "mean_update": "Unchanged from parent algorithm.",
            "adaptation": "Unchanged from parent algorithm."
        }

    def __call__(self, problem: ioh.problem.RealSingleObjective):

        dim = problem.meta_data.n_variables
        low_b, high_b = problem.bounds.lb[0], problem.bounds.ub[0]

        if self.pop_size is None:
            lambda_base = 4 + int(3 * np.log(dim))
        else:
            lambda_base = self.pop_size

        # Total evaluation budget preserved: baseline lambda_base * num_iterations
        total_budget = int(lambda_base * self.num_iterations)

        expected_norm = np.sqrt(dim) * (
            1 - 1 / (4 * dim) + 1 / (21 * dim ** 2)
        )

        mean = np.random.uniform(low_b, high_b, dim)
        sigma = self.sigma * (high_b - low_b)

        C = np.eye(dim)

        p_sigma = np.zeros(dim)
        p_c = np.zeros(dim)

        best_solution = None
        best_fitness = np.inf

        history = {
            "best_fitness": [],
            "sigma": [],
            "mean": [],
            "stagnation_counter": 0,
            "restart_count": 0
        }

        # Runtime counter for remaining boosted generations (shared for alpha and lambda boost)
        boost_remaining = 0
        # Store the adaptive alpha used during an ongoing boost
        boost_alpha_current = None
        # Store the transient lambda used during an ongoing boost
        lambda_boost_current = None

        # Evaluation-driven loop to preserve total evaluation budget while allowing
        # transient per-generation lambda increases.
        evals_done = 0
        iteration = 0

        # Main loop: continue while we have budget and have not exceeded nominal iterations.
        while evals_done < total_budget and iteration < self.num_iterations:

            # Numerical safeguard for covariance matrix
            C = (C + C.T) / 2

            if not np.all(np.isfinite(C)):
                C = np.eye(dim)

            C = C + 1e-12 * np.eye(dim)

            eigenvalues, B = np.linalg.eigh(C)
            eigenvalues = np.maximum(eigenvalues, 1e-20)

            D = np.sqrt(eigenvalues)
            BD = B @ np.diag(D)

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 1: Sampling (modified: adaptive alpha magnitude, adaptive nu, transient lambda increase)
            # ========================================================

            # If stagnation counter reaches threshold and no boost is active, start a boost
            if history["stagnation_counter"] >= self._stagnation_threshold and boost_remaining == 0:
                # compute adaptive boost magnitude based on how long we've been stuck
                extra = history["stagnation_counter"] - self._stagnation_threshold + 1
                adaptive_alpha = self._alpha_base + self._alpha_k * extra
                adaptive_alpha = min(self._alpha_max, adaptive_alpha)
                boost_alpha_current = adaptive_alpha
                # Start transient lambda increase as well
                lambda_boost_current = int(np.ceil(lambda_base * self._lambda_boost_factor))
                # Cap lambda_boost_current to remaining budget (at least 1)
                lambda_boost_current = max(1, min(lambda_boost_current, total_budget - evals_done))
                boost_remaining = self._alpha_boost_duration

            # Current heavy-tail injection fraction
            current_alpha = boost_alpha_current if boost_remaining > 0 and boost_alpha_current is not None else self._alpha_base

            # Determine current degrees of freedom for Student-t:
            if boost_remaining > 0 and boost_alpha_current is not None and history["stagnation_counter"] >= self._stagnation_threshold:
                extra = history["stagnation_counter"] - self._stagnation_threshold + 1
                decrements = extra // self._nu_k
                current_nu = max(self._nu_min, self._nu_base - int(decrements))
            else:
                current_nu = float(self._nu_base)

            # Determine number of offspring this generation while preserving total budget
            if boost_remaining > 0 and lambda_boost_current is not None:
                lambda_gen = lambda_boost_current
            else:
                lambda_gen = lambda_base

            # Ensure we do not exceed remaining budget
            remaining_budget = total_budget - evals_done
            if lambda_gen > remaining_budget:
                lambda_gen = max(1, int(remaining_budget))

            # Compute mu and recompute combination weights for current lambda_gen
            mu = max(1, lambda_gen // 2)
            weights = np.log(mu + 0.5) - np.log(np.arange(1, mu + 1))
            weights = weights / np.sum(weights)
            mu_eff = 1.0 / np.sum(weights ** 2)

            # Recompute strategy parameters depending on mu_eff and dim
            c_sigma = (mu_eff + 2) / (dim + mu_eff + 5)

            d_sigma = (
                1
                + 2 * max(0, np.sqrt((mu_eff - 1) / (dim + 1)) - 1)
                + c_sigma
            )

            c_c = (4 + mu_eff / dim) / (dim + 4 + 2 * mu_eff / dim)

            c1 = 2 / ((dim + 1.3) ** 2 + mu_eff)

            c_mu = min(
                1 - c1,
                2 * (mu_eff - 2 + 1 / mu_eff) / ((dim + 2) ** 2 + mu_eff)
            )

            # Standard Gaussian draws in isotropic coordinates
            z = np.random.randn(lambda_gen, dim)

            # Inject a fraction current_alpha of Student-t heavy-tailed draws
            if current_alpha > 0.0:
                n_heavy = int(np.ceil(current_alpha * lambda_gen))
                n_heavy = min(max(n_heavy, 0), lambda_gen)
                if n_heavy > 0:
                    heavy_idx = np.random.choice(lambda_gen, size=n_heavy, replace=False)
                    df = float(current_nu)
                    if df <= 0:
                        df = float(self._nu_min)
                    z_t = np.random.standard_t(df, size=(n_heavy, dim))
                    eps = 1e-12
                    norms = np.linalg.norm(z_t, axis=1)
                    scales = expected_norm / np.maximum(norms, eps)
                    z_t = z_t * scales[:, np.newaxis]
                    z[heavy_idx] = z_t

            # After using one boosted generation, decrement remaining counter
            if boost_remaining > 0:
                boost_remaining -= 1
                if boost_remaining == 0:
                    boost_alpha_current = None
                    lambda_boost_current = None

            # Transform into solution space using current covariance eigenbasis and scale
            population = mean + sigma * (z @ BD.T)

            # Numerical safeguard for candidate solutions
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(population)):
                population = np.nan_to_num(
                    population,
                    nan=0.0,
                    posinf=high_b,
                    neginf=low_b
                )

            population = np.clip(population, low_b, high_b)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 1
            # ========================================================

            # Evaluate population (must not exceed remaining budget)
            fitness = np.array([problem(ind) for ind in population])
            evals_done += lambda_gen

            sorted_indices = np.argsort(fitness)

            population = population[sorted_indices]
            z = z[sorted_indices]
            fitness = fitness[sorted_indices]

            previous_best_fitness = best_fitness

            if fitness[0] < best_fitness:
                best_fitness = fitness[0]
                best_solution = population[0].copy()
                history["stagnation_counter"] = 0
                # Cancel any ongoing boost upon improvement
                boost_remaining = 0
                boost_alpha_current = None
                lambda_boost_current = None
            else:
                history["stagnation_counter"] += 1

            selected_population = population[:mu]
            selected_z = z[:mu]

            old_mean = mean.copy()

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 2: Mean update
            # ========================================================

            mean = np.sum(
                weights[:, np.newaxis] * selected_population,
                axis=0
            )

            mean = np.clip(mean, low_b, high_b)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 2
            # ========================================================

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 3: Adaptation
            # ========================================================

            C_inv_sqrt = B @ np.diag(1 / D) @ B.T

            p_sigma = (
                (1 - c_sigma) * p_sigma
                + np.sqrt(c_sigma * (2 - c_sigma) * mu_eff)
                * (C_inv_sqrt @ ((mean - old_mean) / sigma))
            )

            sigma_exponent = (
                (c_sigma / d_sigma)
                * (np.linalg.norm(p_sigma) / expected_norm - 1)
            )

            sigma_exponent = np.clip(
                sigma_exponent,
                -50.0,
                50.0
            )

            sigma = sigma * np.exp(sigma_exponent)

            if not np.isfinite(sigma):
                sigma = self.sigma * (high_b - low_b)

            sigma = np.clip(
                sigma,
                1e-12,
                1e3 * (high_b - low_b)
            )

            norm_p_sigma = np.linalg.norm(p_sigma)

            h_sigma_condition = norm_p_sigma / np.sqrt(
                1 - (1 - c_sigma) ** (2 * (iteration + 1))
            ) < (1.4 + 2 / (dim + 1)) * expected_norm

            h_sigma = 1.0 if h_sigma_condition else 0.0

            p_c = (
                (1 - c_c) * p_c
                + h_sigma
                * np.sqrt(c_c * (2 - c_c) * mu_eff)
                * ((mean - old_mean) / sigma)
            )

            y = (selected_population - old_mean) / sigma

            rank_mu_update = np.zeros((dim, dim))

            for i in range(mu):
                rank_mu_update += weights[i] * np.outer(y[i], y[i])

            C = (
                (1 - c1 - c_mu) * C
                + c1 * np.outer(p_c, p_c)
                + c_mu * rank_mu_update
            )

            C = (C + C.T) / 2

            # Final covariance safeguard
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(C)):
                C = np.eye(dim)

            C = C + 1e-12 * np.eye(dim)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 3
            # ========================================================

            history["best_fitness"].append(best_fitness)
            history["sigma"].append(sigma)
            history["mean"].append(mean.copy())

            iteration += 1

        return best_solution, best_fitness
```

# CMA-ES Variant: LLM-generated CMA-ES variant cycle 16 | Cycle 16
