## Metric:
Values represent error to the BBOB optimum. Lower is better.

## Algorithm Code:
```python
import numpy as np
import ioh


class LLM_CMAES:
    '''
    CMA-ES variant that injects a small fraction of heavy-tailed Student-t samples
    into the Gaussian offspring each generation. Injected Student-t directions are
    rescaled so their Euclidean norm matches the expected norm of a standard
    Gaussian draw (preserving directional heavy tails while limiting extreme radii).
    Additionally implements a stagnation-triggered temporary increase in the
    heavy-tail injection fraction; the magnitude of this boost is adaptive to the
    stagnation length (longer stagnation -> larger boost up to a cap).

    New in Cycle 10: Adaptive tail heaviness. The degrees-of-freedom (nu) of the
    Student-t used for injected samples is nu_base during normal progress and,
    when a stagnation-triggered boost is active, is reduced (heavier tails) by
    1 every K stagnation generations down to nu_min. This is applied only during
    the same boost window used by the adaptive alpha.

    Cycle 15 addition: Transient, stagnation-triggered population-size increase
    (lambda boost) that temporarily samples more offspring for T generations
    when stagnation is detected. The total evaluation budget is preserved by
    shortening the remaining number of generations proportionally so that no
    extra objective evaluations occur overall.
    '''
    def __init__(
            self,
            pop_size=20,
            num_iterations=100,
            sigma=0.5
        ):

        self.pop_size = pop_size
        self.num_iterations = num_iterations
        self.sigma = sigma

        # Hardcoded mixture parameters per EXPLAINABILITY_REPORT
        # Base injection fraction
        self._alpha_base = 0.10  # fraction of offspring to replace normally with Student-t

        # Adaptive tail-heaviness parameters (new: Cycle 10)
        # Base and minimum degrees of freedom for Student-t
        self._nu_base = 6      # moderate tail during normal progress
        self._nu_min = 2       # do not go below this (heavier tails)
        # Decrement nu by 1 every K stagnation generations during boost
        self._nu_k = 2

        # Stagnation-triggered adaptive injection parameters
        # The boost magnitude is now adaptive to how long stagnation has persisted:
        # alpha_boost = min(alpha_max, alpha_base + alpha_k * (stagnation_counter - S + 1))
        self._alpha_max = 0.30            # maximum allowed boost fraction
        self._alpha_k = 0.05              # increment per extra stagnation generation beyond threshold
        self._stagnation_threshold = 5    # S: consecutive generations without improvement to trigger boost
        self._alpha_boost_duration = 3    # T: number of generations to keep adaptive alpha during a boost

        # Cycle 15: transient population-size increase parameters
        # Multiply lambda by this factor during a boost window (e.g., 1.5x-2x suggested)
        # TRY modification from EXPLAINABILITY_REPORT: reduce boost factor from 1.8 to 1.5
        self._lambda_boost_factor = 1.5

        self.variant_description = {
            "sampling": (
                "Modified: Adaptive heavy-tail injection fraction and adaptive tail heaviness, "
                "plus a transient, stagnation-triggered population-size (lambda) increase. "
                "Normally use alpha_base=0.10 of offspring replaced by multivariate Student-t draws. "
                "If no best-so-far improvement is observed for S=5 consecutive generations, start "
                "a boost of fixed duration T=3 generations. The heavy-tail injection boost magnitude "
                "is adaptive to stagnation length: alpha_boost = min(alpha_max, alpha_base + alpha_k * (stagnation_counter - S + 1)). "
                "During the same boost window, the Student-t degrees-of-freedom nu is reduced to make tails heavier: "
                "nu = max(nu_min, nu_base - floor((stagnation_counter - S + 1)/K)). "
                "Additionally (Cycle 15), start a transient lambda increase during the boost window by a factor "
                "lambda_factor=_lambda_boost_factor (rounded up to integer offspring). The total evaluation budget "
                "(lambda_base * num_iterations) is preserved by shortening remaining generations proportionally so that "
                "no extra objective evaluations are performed. Injected Student-t vectors are rescaled so their Euclidean "
                "norm equals the expected norm of a standard Gaussian draw. Injected offspring replace existing ones "
                "within the per-generation budget; no extra evaluations are performed."
            ),
            "mean_update": "Unchanged from parent algorithm.",
            "adaptation": "Unchanged from parent algorithm."
        }

    def __call__(self, problem: ioh.problem.RealSingleObjective):

        dim = problem.meta_data.n_variables
        low_b, high_b = problem.bounds.lb[0], problem.bounds.ub[0]

        if self.pop_size is None:
            lambda_base = 4 + int(3 * np.log(dim))
        else:
            lambda_base = self.pop_size

        # Total evaluation budget preserved: baseline lambda_base * num_iterations
        total_budget = int(lambda_base * self.num_iterations)

        expected_norm = np.sqrt(dim) * (
            1 - 1 / (4 * dim) + 1 / (21 * dim ** 2)
        )

        mean = np.random.uniform(low_b, high_b, dim)
        sigma = self.sigma * (high_b - low_b)

        C = np.eye(dim)

        p_sigma = np.zeros(dim)
        p_c = np.zeros(dim)

        best_solution = None
        best_fitness = np.inf

        history = {
            "best_fitness": [],
            "sigma": [],
            "mean": [],
            "stagnation_counter": 0,
            "restart_count": 0
        }

        # Runtime counter for remaining boosted generations (shared for alpha and lambda boost)
        boost_remaining = 0
        # Store the adaptive alpha used during an ongoing boost
        boost_alpha_current = None
        # Store the transient lambda used during an ongoing boost
        lambda_boost_current = None

        # Evaluation-driven loop to preserve total evaluation budget while allowing
        # transient per-generation lambda increases.
        evals_done = 0
        iteration = 0

        # Main loop: continue while we have budget and have not exceeded nominal iterations.
        while evals_done < total_budget and iteration < self.num_iterations:

            # Numerical safeguard for covariance matrix
            C = (C + C.T) / 2

            if not np.all(np.isfinite(C)):
                C = np.eye(dim)

            C = C + 1e-12 * np.eye(dim)

            eigenvalues, B = np.linalg.eigh(C)
            eigenvalues = np.maximum(eigenvalues, 1e-20)

            D = np.sqrt(eigenvalues)
            BD = B @ np.diag(D)

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 1: Sampling (modified: adaptive alpha magnitude, adaptive nu, transient lambda increase)
            # ========================================================

            # If stagnation counter reaches threshold and no boost is active, start a boost
            if history["stagnation_counter"] >= self._stagnation_threshold and boost_remaining == 0:
                # compute adaptive boost magnitude based on how long we've been stuck
                extra = history["stagnation_counter"] - self._stagnation_threshold + 1
                adaptive_alpha = self._alpha_base + self._alpha_k * extra
                adaptive_alpha = min(self._alpha_max, adaptive_alpha)
                boost_alpha_current = adaptive_alpha
                # Start transient lambda increase as well
                lambda_boost_current = int(np.ceil(lambda_base * self._lambda_boost_factor))
                # Cap lambda_boost_current to remaining budget (at least 1)
                lambda_boost_current = max(1, min(lambda_boost_current, total_budget - evals_done))
                boost_remaining = self._alpha_boost_duration

            # Current heavy-tail injection fraction
            current_alpha = boost_alpha_current if boost_remaining > 0 and boost_alpha_current is not None else self._alpha_base

            # Determine current degrees of freedom for Student-t:
            if boost_remaining > 0 and boost_alpha_current is not None and history["stagnation_counter"] >= self._stagnation_threshold:
                extra = history["stagnation_counter"] - self._stagnation_threshold + 1
                decrements = extra // self._nu_k
                current_nu = max(self._nu_min, self._nu_base - int(decrements))
            else:
                current_nu = float(self._nu_base)

            # Determine number of offspring this generation while preserving total budget
            if boost_remaining > 0 and lambda_boost_current is not None:
                lambda_gen = lambda_boost_current
            else:
                lambda_gen = lambda_base

            # Ensure we do not exceed remaining budget
            remaining_budget = total_budget - evals_done
            if lambda_gen > remaining_budget:
                lambda_gen = max(1, int(remaining_budget))

            # Compute mu and recompute combination weights for current lambda_gen
            mu = max(1, lambda_gen // 2)
            weights = np.log(mu + 0.5) - np.log(np.arange(1, mu + 1))
            weights = weights / np.sum(weights)
            mu_eff = 1.0 / np.sum(weights ** 2)

            # Recompute strategy parameters depending on mu_eff and dim
            c_sigma = (mu_eff + 2) / (dim + mu_eff + 5)

            d_sigma = (
                1
                + 2 * max(0, np.sqrt((mu_eff - 1) / (dim + 1)) - 1)
                + c_sigma
            )

            c_c = (4 + mu_eff / dim) / (dim + 4 + 2 * mu_eff / dim)

            c1 = 2 / ((dim + 1.3) ** 2 + mu_eff)

            c_mu = min(
                1 - c1,
                2 * (mu_eff - 2 + 1 / mu_eff) / ((dim + 2) ** 2 + mu_eff)
            )

            # Standard Gaussian draws in isotropic coordinates
            z = np.random.randn(lambda_gen, dim)

            # Inject a fraction current_alpha of Student-t heavy-tailed draws
            if current_alpha > 0.0:
                n_heavy = int(np.ceil(current_alpha * lambda_gen))
                n_heavy = min(max(n_heavy, 0), lambda_gen)
                if n_heavy > 0:
                    heavy_idx = np.random.choice(lambda_gen, size=n_heavy, replace=False)
                    df = float(current_nu)
                    if df <= 0:
                        df = float(self._nu_min)
                    z_t = np.random.standard_t(df, size=(n_heavy, dim))
                    eps = 1e-12
                    norms = np.linalg.norm(z_t, axis=1)
                    scales = expected_norm / np.maximum(norms, eps)
                    z_t = z_t * scales[:, np.newaxis]
                    z[heavy_idx] = z_t

            # After using one boosted generation, decrement remaining counter
            if boost_remaining > 0:
                boost_remaining -= 1
                if boost_remaining == 0:
                    boost_alpha_current = None
                    lambda_boost_current = None

            # Transform into solution space using current covariance eigenbasis and scale
            population = mean + sigma * (z @ BD.T)

            # Numerical safeguard for candidate solutions
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(population)):
                population = np.nan_to_num(
                    population,
                    nan=0.0,
                    posinf=high_b,
                    neginf=low_b
                )

            population = np.clip(population, low_b, high_b)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 1
            # ========================================================

            # Evaluate population (must not exceed remaining budget)
            fitness = np.array([problem(ind) for ind in population])
            evals_done += lambda_gen

            sorted_indices = np.argsort(fitness)

            population = population[sorted_indices]
            z = z[sorted_indices]
            fitness = fitness[sorted_indices]

            previous_best_fitness = best_fitness

            if fitness[0] < best_fitness:
                best_fitness = fitness[0]
                best_solution = population[0].copy()
                history["stagnation_counter"] = 0
                # Cancel any ongoing boost upon improvement
                boost_remaining = 0
                boost_alpha_current = None
                lambda_boost_current = None
            else:
                history["stagnation_counter"] += 1

            selected_population = population[:mu]
            selected_z = z[:mu]

            old_mean = mean.copy()

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 2: Mean update
            # ========================================================

            mean = np.sum(
                weights[:, np.newaxis] * selected_population,
                axis=0
            )

            mean = np.clip(mean, low_b, high_b)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 2
            # ========================================================

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 3: Adaptation
            # ========================================================

            C_inv_sqrt = B @ np.diag(1 / D) @ B.T

            p_sigma = (
                (1 - c_sigma) * p_sigma
                + np.sqrt(c_sigma * (2 - c_sigma) * mu_eff)
                * (C_inv_sqrt @ ((mean - old_mean) / sigma))
            )

            sigma_exponent = (
                (c_sigma / d_sigma)
                * (np.linalg.norm(p_sigma) / expected_norm - 1)
            )

            sigma_exponent = np.clip(
                sigma_exponent,
                -50.0,
                50.0
            )

            sigma = sigma * np.exp(sigma_exponent)

            if not np.isfinite(sigma):
                sigma = self.sigma * (high_b - low_b)

            sigma = np.clip(
                sigma,
                1e-12,
                1e3 * (high_b - low_b)
            )

            norm_p_sigma = np.linalg.norm(p_sigma)

            h_sigma_condition = norm_p_sigma / np.sqrt(
                1 - (1 - c_sigma) ** (2 * (iteration + 1))
            ) < (1.4 + 2 / (dim + 1)) * expected_norm

            h_sigma = 1.0 if h_sigma_condition else 0.0

            p_c = (
                (1 - c_c) * p_c
                + h_sigma
                * np.sqrt(c_c * (2 - c_c) * mu_eff)
                * ((mean - old_mean) / sigma)
            )

            y = (selected_population - old_mean) / sigma

            rank_mu_update = np.zeros((dim, dim))

            for i in range(mu):
                rank_mu_update += weights[i] * np.outer(y[i], y[i])

            C = (
                (1 - c1 - c_mu) * C
                + c1 * np.outer(p_c, p_c)
                + c_mu * rank_mu_update
            )

            C = (C + C.T) / 2

            # Final covariance safeguard
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(C)):
                C = np.eye(dim)

            C = C + 1e-12 * np.eye(dim)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 3
            # ========================================================

            history["best_fitness"].append(best_fitness)
            history["sigma"].append(sigma)
            history["mean"].append(mean.copy())

            iteration += 1

        return best_solution, best_fitness
```

# ========================================================
# CMA-ES Variant: LLM-generated CMA-ES variant cycle 17 | Cycle 17
# ========================================================

## Metric:
Values represent error to the BBOB optimum. Lower is better.

## Algorithm Code:
```python
import numpy as np
import ioh


class LLM_CMAES:
    '''
    CMA-ES variant that injects a small fraction of heavy-tailed Student-t samples
    into the Gaussian offspring each generation. Injected Student-t directions are
    rescaled so their Euclidean norm matches the expected norm of a standard
    Gaussian draw (preserving directional heavy tails while limiting extreme radii).
    Additionally implements a stagnation-triggered temporary increase in the
    heavy-tail injection fraction; the magnitude of this boost is adaptive to the
    stagnation length (longer stagnation -> larger boost up to a cap).

    New in Cycle 10: Adaptive tail heaviness. The degrees-of-freedom (nu) of the
    Student-t used for injected samples is nu_base during normal progress and,
    when a stagnation-triggered boost is active, is reduced (heavier tails) by
    1 every K stagnation generations down to nu_min. This is applied only during
    the same boost window used by the adaptive alpha.

    Cycle 15 addition: Transient, stagnation-triggered population-size increase
    (lambda boost) that temporarily samples more offspring for T generations
    when stagnation is detected. The total evaluation budget is preserved by
    shortening the remaining number of generations proportionally so that no
    extra objective evaluations occur overall.

    Cycle 17 modification: During active boost windows, attenuate the
    contribution of injected Student-t offspring to the adaptation machinery.
    The recombination weights used for p_sigma, p_c and the rank-mu covariance
    update are multiplied by alpha_att for selected offspring that originated
    from injected Student-t draws, then renormalized (fallback to original
    weights if renormalization would zero mass). This preserves exploration
    while reducing deleterious impact of heavy-tailed samples on adaptation.
    '''
    def __init__(
            self,
            pop_size=20,
            num_iterations=100,
            sigma=0.5
        ):

        self.pop_size = pop_size
        self.num_iterations = num_iterations
        self.sigma = sigma

        # Hardcoded mixture parameters per EXPLAINABILITY_REPORT
        # Base injection fraction
        self._alpha_base = 0.10  # fraction of offspring to replace normally with Student-t

        # Adaptive tail-heaviness parameters (new: Cycle 10)
        # Base and minimum degrees of freedom for Student-t
        self._nu_base = 6      # moderate tail during normal progress
        self._nu_min = 2       # do not go below this (heavier tails)
        # Decrement nu by 1 every K stagnation generations during boost
        self._nu_k = 2

        # Stagnation-triggered adaptive injection parameters
        # The boost magnitude is now adaptive to how long stagnation has persisted:
        # alpha_boost = min(alpha_max, alpha_base + alpha_k * (stagnation_counter - S + 1))
        self._alpha_max = 0.30            # maximum allowed boost fraction
        self._alpha_k = 0.05              # increment per extra stagnation generation beyond threshold
        self._stagnation_threshold = 5    # S: consecutive generations without improvement to trigger boost
        self._alpha_boost_duration = 3    # T: number of generations to keep adaptive alpha during a boost

        # Cycle 15: transient population-size increase parameters
        # Multiply lambda by this factor during a boost window (e.g., 1.5x-2x suggested)
        # Reverted to Cycle 15 value per EXPLAINABILITY_REPORT
        self._lambda_boost_factor = 1.8

        # Cycle 17: attenuation factor for adaptation contributions from injected samples
        # During boost windows, attenuate recombination weights of injected offspring by alpha_att
        self._alpha_att = 0.5

        self.variant_description = {
            "sampling": (
                "Unchanged from parent Cycle 15: Adaptive heavy-tail injection fraction and adaptive tail heaviness, "
                "plus a transient, stagnation-triggered population-size (lambda) increase. Normally use alpha_base=0.10 "
                "of offspring replaced by multivariate Student-t draws. If no best-so-far improvement is observed for "
                "S=5 consecutive generations, start a boost of fixed duration T=3 generations. The heavy-tail injection "
                "boost magnitude is adaptive to stagnation length: alpha_boost = min(alpha_max, alpha_base + alpha_k * (stagnation_counter - S + 1)). "
                "During the same boost window, the Student-t degrees-of-freedom nu is reduced to make tails heavier: "
                "nu = max(nu_min, nu_base - floor((stagnation_counter - S + 1)/K)). Transient lambda increase during the boost "
                "is applied by factor _lambda_boost_factor (rounded up). Total evaluation budget is preserved. Injected Student-t "
                "vectors are rescaled so their Euclidean norm equals the expected norm of a standard Gaussian draw."
            ),
            "mean_update": "Unchanged from parent algorithm.",
            "adaptation": (
                "Modified: During active boost windows, attenuate the contribution of injected Student-t offspring to the "
                "adaptation machinery. Specifically, multiply the recombination weights used for p_sigma, p_c and the rank-mu "
                "covariance update by alpha_att=0.5 for selected offspring that originated from injected Student-t draws, "
                "then renormalize adaptation weights (fallback to original weights if renormalization would produce zero mass). "
                "Outside boost windows the standard (unattenuated) adaptation is used."
            )
        }

    def __call__(self, problem: ioh.problem.RealSingleObjective):

        dim = problem.meta_data.n_variables
        low_b, high_b = problem.bounds.lb[0], problem.bounds.ub[0]

        if self.pop_size is None:
            lambda_base = 4 + int(3 * np.log(dim))
        else:
            lambda_base = self.pop_size

        # Total evaluation budget preserved: baseline lambda_base * num_iterations
        total_budget = int(lambda_base * self.num_iterations)

        expected_norm = np.sqrt(dim) * (
            1 - 1 / (4 * dim) + 1 / (21 * dim ** 2)
        )

        mean = np.random.uniform(low_b, high_b, dim)
        sigma = self.sigma * (high_b - low_b)

        C = np.eye(dim)

        p_sigma = np.zeros(dim)
        p_c = np.zeros(dim)

        best_solution = None
        best_fitness = np.inf

        history = {
            "best_fitness": [],
            "sigma": [],
            "mean": [],
            "stagnation_counter": 0,
            "restart_count": 0
        }

        # Runtime counter for remaining boosted generations (shared for alpha and lambda boost)
        boost_remaining = 0
        # Store the adaptive alpha used during an ongoing boost
        boost_alpha_current = None
        # Store the transient lambda used during an ongoing boost
        lambda_boost_current = None

        # Evaluation-driven loop to preserve total evaluation budget while allowing
        # transient per-generation lambda increases.
        evals_done = 0
        iteration = 0

        # Main loop: continue while we have budget and have not exceeded nominal iterations.
        while evals_done < total_budget and iteration < self.num_iterations:

            # Numerical safeguard for covariance matrix
            C = (C + C.T) / 2

            if not np.all(np.isfinite(C)):
                C = np.eye(dim)

            C = C + 1e-12 * np.eye(dim)

            eigenvalues, B = np.linalg.eigh(C)
            eigenvalues = np.maximum(eigenvalues, 1e-20)

            D = np.sqrt(eigenvalues)
            BD = B @ np.diag(D)

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 1: Sampling (modified: adaptive alpha magnitude, adaptive nu, transient lambda increase)
            # ========================================================

            # If stagnation counter reaches threshold and no boost is active, start a boost
            if history["stagnation_counter"] >= self._stagnation_threshold and boost_remaining == 0:
                # compute adaptive boost magnitude based on how long we've been stuck
                extra = history["stagnation_counter"] - self._stagnation_threshold + 1
                adaptive_alpha = self._alpha_base + self._alpha_k * extra
                adaptive_alpha = min(self._alpha_max, adaptive_alpha)
                boost_alpha_current = adaptive_alpha
                # Start transient lambda increase as well (Cycle 15 factor preserved)
                lambda_boost_current = int(np.ceil(lambda_base * self._lambda_boost_factor))
                # Cap lambda_boost_current to remaining budget (at least 1)
                lambda_boost_current = max(1, min(lambda_boost_current, total_budget - evals_done))
                boost_remaining = self._alpha_boost_duration

            # Current heavy-tail injection fraction
            current_alpha = boost_alpha_current if boost_remaining > 0 and boost_alpha_current is not None else self._alpha_base

            # Determine current degrees of freedom for Student-t:
            if boost_remaining > 0 and boost_alpha_current is not None and history["stagnation_counter"] >= self._stagnation_threshold:
                extra = history["stagnation_counter"] - self._stagnation_threshold + 1
                decrements = extra // self._nu_k
                current_nu = max(self._nu_min, self._nu_base - int(decrements))
            else:
                current_nu = float(self._nu_base)

            # Determine number of offspring this generation while preserving total budget
            if boost_remaining > 0 and lambda_boost_current is not None:
                lambda_gen = lambda_boost_current
            else:
                lambda_gen = lambda_base

            # Ensure we do not exceed remaining budget
            remaining_budget = total_budget - evals_done
            if lambda_gen > remaining_budget:
                lambda_gen = max(1, int(remaining_budget))

            # Compute mu and recompute combination weights for current lambda_gen
            mu = max(1, lambda_gen // 2)
            weights = np.log(mu + 0.5) - np.log(np.arange(1, mu + 1))
            weights = weights / np.sum(weights)
            mu_eff = 1.0 / np.sum(weights ** 2)

            # Recompute strategy parameters depending on mu_eff and dim
            c_sigma = (mu_eff + 2) / (dim + mu_eff + 5)

            d_sigma = (
                1
                + 2 * max(0, np.sqrt((mu_eff - 1) / (dim + 1)) - 1)
                + c_sigma
            )

            c_c = (4 + mu_eff / dim) / (dim + 4 + 2 * mu_eff / dim)

            c1 = 2 / ((dim + 1.3) ** 2 + mu_eff)

            c_mu = min(
                1 - c1,
                2 * (mu_eff - 2 + 1 / mu_eff) / ((dim + 2) ** 2 + mu_eff)
            )

            # Standard Gaussian draws in isotropic coordinates
            z = np.random.randn(lambda_gen, dim)

            # Track which samples are heavy-tailed
            is_heavy = np.zeros(lambda_gen, dtype=bool)

            # Inject a fraction current_alpha of Student-t heavy-tailed draws
            if current_alpha > 0.0:
                n_heavy = int(np.ceil(current_alpha * lambda_gen))
                n_heavy = min(max(n_heavy, 0), lambda_gen)
                if n_heavy > 0:
                    heavy_idx = np.random.choice(lambda_gen, size=n_heavy, replace=False)
                    is_heavy[heavy_idx] = True
                    df = float(current_nu)
                    if df <= 0:
                        df = float(self._nu_min)
                    z_t = np.random.standard_t(df, size=(n_heavy, dim))
                    eps = 1e-12
                    norms = np.linalg.norm(z_t, axis=1)
                    scales = expected_norm / np.maximum(norms, eps)
                    z_t = z_t * scales[:, np.newaxis]
                    z[heavy_idx] = z_t

            # Mark whether this generation had an active boost (before decrement)
            boost_active_generation = (boost_alpha_current is not None and boost_remaining > 0)

            # After using one boosted generation, decrement remaining counter
            if boost_remaining > 0:
                boost_remaining -= 1
                if boost_remaining == 0:
                    boost_alpha_current = None
                    lambda_boost_current = None

            # Transform into solution space using current covariance eigenbasis and scale
            population = mean + sigma * (z @ BD.T)

            # Numerical safeguard for candidate solutions
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(population)):
                population = np.nan_to_num(
                    population,
                    nan=0.0,
                    posinf=high_b,
                    neginf=low_b
                )

            population = np.clip(population, low_b, high_b)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 1
            # ========================================================

            # Evaluate population (must not exceed remaining budget)
            fitness = np.array([problem(ind) for ind in population])
            evals_done += lambda_gen

            sorted_indices = np.argsort(fitness)

            population = population[sorted_indices]
            z = z[sorted_indices]
            fitness = fitness[sorted_indices]
            is_heavy = is_heavy[sorted_indices]

            previous_best_fitness = best_fitness

            if fitness[0] < best_fitness:
                best_fitness = fitness[0]
                best_solution = population[0].copy()
                history["stagnation_counter"] = 0
                # Cancel any ongoing boost upon improvement
                boost_remaining = 0
                boost_alpha_current = None
                lambda_boost_current = None
            else:
                history["stagnation_counter"] += 1

            selected_population = population[:mu]
            selected_z = z[:mu]
            selected_is_heavy = is_heavy[:mu]

            old_mean = mean.copy()

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 2: Mean update
            # ========================================================

            mean = np.sum(
                weights[:, np.newaxis] * selected_population,
                axis=0
            )

            mean = np.clip(mean, low_b, high_b)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 2
            # ========================================================

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 3: Adaptation (modified: attenuate heavy-tail contributions during boost)
            # ========================================================

            C_inv_sqrt = B @ np.diag(1 / D) @ B.T

            # Prepare adaptation weights: attenuate contributions from injected samples during active boost
            if boost_active_generation:
                adapted_weights = weights.copy()
                # Multiply weights of selected injected offspring by alpha_att
                if np.any(selected_is_heavy):
                    adapted_weights[selected_is_heavy] *= self._alpha_att
                    sum_adapt = np.sum(adapted_weights)
                    if sum_adapt <= 0 or not np.isfinite(sum_adapt):
                        # Fallback to original weights
                        adapted_weights = weights.copy()
                    else:
                        adapted_weights = adapted_weights / sum_adapt
                else:
                    # No heavy samples among selected, keep original weights
                    adapted_weights = weights.copy()
            else:
                adapted_weights = weights.copy()

            # Effective mu for adaptation scaling (use adapted weights to reduce influence)
            mu_eff_adapt = 1.0 / np.sum(adapted_weights ** 2)

            # Update evolution path for sigma using adapted mu_eff (attenuated during boost)
            p_sigma = (
                (1 - c_sigma) * p_sigma
                + np.sqrt(c_sigma * (2 - c_sigma) * mu_eff_adapt)
                * (C_inv_sqrt @ ((mean - old_mean) / sigma))
            )

            sigma_exponent = (
                (c_sigma / d_sigma)
                * (np.linalg.norm(p_sigma) / expected_norm - 1)
            )

            sigma_exponent = np.clip(
                sigma_exponent,
                -50.0,
                50.0
            )

            sigma = sigma * np.exp(sigma_exponent)

            if not np.isfinite(sigma):
                sigma = self.sigma * (high_b - low_b)

            sigma = np.clip(
                sigma,
                1e-12,
                1e3 * (high_b - low_b)
            )

            norm_p_sigma = np.linalg.norm(p_sigma)

            h_sigma_condition = norm_p_sigma / np.sqrt(
                1 - (1 - c_sigma) ** (2 * (iteration + 1))
            ) < (1.4 + 2 / (dim + 1)) * expected_norm

            h_sigma = 1.0 if h_sigma_condition else 0.0

            # Update evolution path for covariance using adapted mu_eff
            p_c = (
                (1 - c_c) * p_c
                + h_sigma
                * np.sqrt(c_c * (2 - c_c) * mu_eff_adapt)
                * ((mean - old_mean) / sigma)
            )

            y = (selected_population - old_mean) / sigma

            rank_mu_update = np.zeros((dim, dim))

            # Use adapted_weights for rank-mu update (attenuate injected samples if in boost)
            for i in range(mu):
                rank_mu_update += adapted_weights[i] * np.outer(y[i], y[i])

            C = (
                (1 - c1 - c_mu) * C
                + c1 * np.outer(p_c, p_c)
                + c_mu * rank_mu_update
            )

            C = (C + C.T) / 2

            # Final covariance safeguard
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(C)):
                C = np.eye(dim)

            C = C + 1e-12 * np.eye(dim)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 3
            # ========================================================

            history["best_fitness"].append(best_fitness)
            history["sigma"].append(sigma)
            history["mean"].append(mean.copy())

            iteration += 1

        return best_solution, best_fitness
```

# ========================================================
# CMA-ES Variant: LLM-generated CMA-ES variant cycle 18 | Cycle 18
# ========================================================

## Metric:
Values represent error to the BBOB optimum. Lower is better.

## Algorithm Code:
```python
import numpy as np
import ioh


class LLM_CMAES:
    '''
    CMA-ES variant that injects a small fraction of heavy-tailed Student-t samples
    into the Gaussian offspring each generation. Injected Student-t directions are
    rescaled so their Euclidean norm matches the expected norm of a standard
    Gaussian draw (preserving directional heavy tails while limiting extreme radii).
    Additionally implements a stagnation-triggered temporary increase in the
    heavy-tail injection fraction; the magnitude of this boost is adaptive to the
    stagnation length (longer stagnation -> larger boost up to a cap).

    New in Cycle 10: Adaptive tail heaviness. The degrees-of-freedom (nu) of the
    Student-t used for injected samples is nu_base during normal progress and,
    when a stagnation-triggered boost is active, is reduced (heavier tails) by
    1 every K stagnation generations down to nu_min. This is applied only during
    the same boost window used by the adaptive alpha.

    Cycle 15 addition: Transient, stagnation-triggered population-size increase
    (lambda boost) that temporarily samples more offspring for T generations
    when stagnation is detected. The total evaluation budget is preserved by
    shortening the remaining number of generations proportionally so that no
    extra objective evaluations occur overall.

    Cycle 18 modification: Adaptive covariance shrinkage toward isotropy applied
    conditionally when selected offspring indicate disruption (fraction of selected
    offspring that were heavy-tailed). This shrinkage is lightweight and adaptive:
    beta = min(beta_max, beta_base + beta_k * f) where f is the fraction of the
    selected mu offspring that originated from heavy-tailed draws. The covariance
    update is then shrunk: C <- (1 - beta) * C + beta * (trace(C)/dim) * I.
    '''
    def __init__(
            self,
            pop_size=20,
            num_iterations=100,
            sigma=0.5
        ):

        self.pop_size = pop_size
        self.num_iterations = num_iterations
        self.sigma = sigma

        # Hardcoded mixture parameters per EXPLAINABILITY_REPORT
        # Base injection fraction
        self._alpha_base = 0.10  # fraction of offspring to replace normally with Student-t

        # Adaptive tail-heaviness parameters (new: Cycle 10)
        # Base and minimum degrees of freedom for Student-t
        self._nu_base = 6      # moderate tail during normal progress
        self._nu_min = 2       # do not go below this (heavier tails)
        # Decrement nu by 1 every K stagnation generations during boost
        self._nu_k = 2

        # Stagnation-triggered adaptive injection parameters
        # The boost magnitude is now adaptive to how long stagnation has persisted:
        # alpha_boost = min(alpha_max, alpha_base + alpha_k * (stagnation_counter - S + 1))
        self._alpha_max = 0.30            # maximum allowed boost fraction
        self._alpha_k = 0.05              # increment per extra stagnation generation beyond threshold
        self._stagnation_threshold = 5    # S: consecutive generations without improvement to trigger boost
        self._alpha_boost_duration = 3    # T: number of generations to keep adaptive alpha during a boost

        # Cycle 15: transient population-size increase parameters
        # Multiply lambda by this factor during a boost window (e.g., 1.5x-2x suggested)
        self._lambda_boost_factor = 1.8

        # Cycle 18: Adaptive covariance shrinkage parameters (single added mechanism)
        # beta = min(beta_max, beta_base + beta_k * f) where f is fraction of selected mu that were heavy-tailed
        self._beta_base = 0.01
        self._beta_max = 0.10
        # choose beta_k so that when f=1 we reach beta_max approximately: beta_k ~ beta_max - beta_base
        self._beta_k = 0.09

        self.variant_description = {
            "sampling": (
                "Modified: Adaptive heavy-tail injection fraction and adaptive tail heaviness, "
                "plus a transient, stagnation-triggered population-size (lambda) increase. "
                "Normally use alpha_base=0.10 of offspring replaced by multivariate Student-t draws. "
                "If no best-so-far improvement is observed for S=5 consecutive generations, start "
                "a boost of fixed duration T=3 generations. The heavy-tail injection boost magnitude "
                "is adaptive to stagnation length: alpha_boost = min(alpha_max, alpha_base + alpha_k * (stagnation_counter - S + 1)). "
                "During the same boost window, the Student-t degrees-of-freedom nu is reduced to make tails heavier: "
                "nu = max(nu_min, nu_base - floor((stagnation_counter - S + 1)/K)). "
                "Additionally (Cycle 15), start a transient lambda increase during the boost window by a factor "
                "lambda_factor=_lambda_boost_factor (rounded up to integer offspring). The total evaluation budget "
                "(lambda_base * num_iterations) is preserved by shortening remaining generations proportionally so that "
                "no extra objective evaluations are performed. Injected Student-t vectors are rescaled so their Euclidean "
                "norm equals the expected norm of a standard Gaussian draw. Injected offspring replace existing ones "
                "within the per-generation budget; no extra evaluations are performed."
            ),
            "mean_update": "Unchanged from parent algorithm.",
            "adaptation": (
                "Modified: Adaptive, conditional covariance shrinkage toward isotropy. "
                "Compute the fraction f of the selected mu offspring that originated from heavy-tailed draws, "
                "then set beta = min(beta_max, beta_base + beta_k * f). After the usual covariance update, "
                "apply C <- (1 - beta) * C + beta * (trace(C)/dim) * I to shrink toward isotropy when disruptions "
                "from heavy-tailed samples are indicated. Other adaptation mechanisms (p_sigma, sigma update, p_c) "
                "remain unchanged."
            )
        }

    def __call__(self, problem: ioh.problem.RealSingleObjective):

        dim = problem.meta_data.n_variables
        low_b, high_b = problem.bounds.lb[0], problem.bounds.ub[0]

        if self.pop_size is None:
            lambda_base = 4 + int(3 * np.log(dim))
        else:
            lambda_base = self.pop_size

        # Total evaluation budget preserved: baseline lambda_base * num_iterations
        total_budget = int(lambda_base * self.num_iterations)

        expected_norm = np.sqrt(dim) * (
            1 - 1 / (4 * dim) + 1 / (21 * dim ** 2)
        )

        mean = np.random.uniform(low_b, high_b, dim)
        sigma = self.sigma * (high_b - low_b)

        C = np.eye(dim)

        p_sigma = np.zeros(dim)
        p_c = np.zeros(dim)

        best_solution = None
        best_fitness = np.inf

        history = {
            "best_fitness": [],
            "sigma": [],
            "mean": [],
            "stagnation_counter": 0,
            "restart_count": 0
        }

        # Runtime counter for remaining boosted generations (shared for alpha and lambda boost)
        boost_remaining = 0
        # Store the adaptive alpha used during an ongoing boost
        boost_alpha_current = None
        # Store the transient lambda used during an ongoing boost
        lambda_boost_current = None

        # Evaluation-driven loop to preserve total evaluation budget while allowing
        # transient per-generation lambda increases.
        evals_done = 0
        iteration = 0

        # Main loop: continue while we have budget and have not exceeded nominal iterations.
        while evals_done < total_budget and iteration < self.num_iterations:

            # Numerical safeguard for covariance matrix
            C = (C + C.T) / 2

            if not np.all(np.isfinite(C)):
                C = np.eye(dim)

            C = C + 1e-12 * np.eye(dim)

            eigenvalues, B = np.linalg.eigh(C)
            eigenvalues = np.maximum(eigenvalues, 1e-20)

            D = np.sqrt(eigenvalues)
            BD = B @ np.diag(D)

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 1: Sampling (modified: adaptive alpha magnitude, adaptive nu, transient lambda increase)
            # ========================================================

            # If stagnation counter reaches threshold and no boost is active, start a boost
            if history["stagnation_counter"] >= self._stagnation_threshold and boost_remaining == 0:
                # compute adaptive boost magnitude based on how long we've been stuck
                extra = history["stagnation_counter"] - self._stagnation_threshold + 1
                adaptive_alpha = self._alpha_base + self._alpha_k * extra
                adaptive_alpha = min(self._alpha_max, adaptive_alpha)
                boost_alpha_current = adaptive_alpha
                # Start transient lambda increase as well
                lambda_boost_current = int(np.ceil(lambda_base * self._lambda_boost_factor))
                # Cap lambda_boost_current to remaining budget (at least 1)
                lambda_boost_current = max(1, min(lambda_boost_current, total_budget - evals_done))
                boost_remaining = self._alpha_boost_duration

            # Current heavy-tail injection fraction
            current_alpha = boost_alpha_current if boost_remaining > 0 and boost_alpha_current is not None else self._alpha_base

            # Determine current degrees of freedom for Student-t:
            if boost_remaining > 0 and boost_alpha_current is not None and history["stagnation_counter"] >= self._stagnation_threshold:
                extra = history["stagnation_counter"] - self._stagnation_threshold + 1
                decrements = extra // self._nu_k
                current_nu = max(self._nu_min, self._nu_base - int(decrements))
            else:
                current_nu = float(self._nu_base)

            # Determine number of offspring this generation while preserving total budget
            if boost_remaining > 0 and lambda_boost_current is not None:
                lambda_gen = lambda_boost_current
            else:
                lambda_gen = lambda_base

            # Ensure we do not exceed remaining budget
            remaining_budget = total_budget - evals_done
            if lambda_gen > remaining_budget:
                lambda_gen = max(1, int(remaining_budget))

            # Compute mu and recompute combination weights for current lambda_gen
            mu = max(1, lambda_gen // 2)
            weights = np.log(mu + 0.5) - np.log(np.arange(1, mu + 1))
            weights = weights / np.sum(weights)
            mu_eff = 1.0 / np.sum(weights ** 2)

            # Recompute strategy parameters depending on mu_eff and dim
            c_sigma = (mu_eff + 2) / (dim + mu_eff + 5)

            d_sigma = (
                1
                + 2 * max(0, np.sqrt((mu_eff - 1) / (dim + 1)) - 1)
                + c_sigma
            )

            c_c = (4 + mu_eff / dim) / (dim + 4 + 2 * mu_eff / dim)

            c1 = 2 / ((dim + 1.3) ** 2 + mu_eff)

            c_mu = min(
                1 - c1,
                2 * (mu_eff - 2 + 1 / mu_eff) / ((dim + 2) ** 2 + mu_eff)
            )

            # Standard Gaussian draws in isotropic coordinates
            z = np.random.randn(lambda_gen, dim)

            # Track which samples are heavy-tailed for later disruption detection
            heavy_mask = np.zeros(lambda_gen, dtype=bool)

            # Inject a fraction current_alpha of Student-t heavy-tailed draws
            if current_alpha > 0.0:
                n_heavy = int(np.ceil(current_alpha * lambda_gen))
                n_heavy = min(max(n_heavy, 0), lambda_gen)
                if n_heavy > 0:
                    heavy_idx = np.random.choice(lambda_gen, size=n_heavy, replace=False)
                    df = float(current_nu)
                    if df <= 0:
                        df = float(self._nu_min)
                    z_t = np.random.standard_t(df, size=(n_heavy, dim))
                    eps = 1e-12
                    norms = np.linalg.norm(z_t, axis=1)
                    scales = expected_norm / np.maximum(norms, eps)
                    z_t = z_t * scales[:, np.newaxis]
                    z[heavy_idx] = z_t
                    heavy_mask[heavy_idx] = True

            # After using one boosted generation, decrement remaining counter
            if boost_remaining > 0:
                boost_remaining -= 1
                if boost_remaining == 0:
                    boost_alpha_current = None
                    lambda_boost_current = None

            # Transform into solution space using current covariance eigenbasis and scale
            population = mean + sigma * (z @ BD.T)

            # Numerical safeguard for candidate solutions
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(population)):
                population = np.nan_to_num(
                    population,
                    nan=0.0,
                    posinf=high_b,
                    neginf=low_b
                )

            population = np.clip(population, low_b, high_b)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 1
            # ========================================================

            # Evaluate population (must not exceed remaining budget)
            fitness = np.array([problem(ind) for ind in population])
            evals_done += lambda_gen

            sorted_indices = np.argsort(fitness)

            population = population[sorted_indices]
            z = z[sorted_indices]
            fitness = fitness[sorted_indices]
            heavy_mask = heavy_mask[sorted_indices]

            previous_best_fitness = best_fitness

            if fitness[0] < best_fitness:
                best_fitness = fitness[0]
                best_solution = population[0].copy()
                history["stagnation_counter"] = 0
                # Cancel any ongoing boost upon improvement
                boost_remaining = 0
                boost_alpha_current = None
                lambda_boost_current = None
            else:
                history["stagnation_counter"] += 1

            selected_population = population[:mu]
            selected_z = z[:mu]
            selected_heavy_mask = heavy_mask[:mu]

            # Compute disruption indicator f: fraction of selected mu that were heavy-tailed
            f_disruption = float(np.sum(selected_heavy_mask)) / float(mu)

            old_mean = mean.copy()

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 2: Mean update
            # ========================================================

            mean = np.sum(
                weights[:, np.newaxis] * selected_population,
                axis=0
            )

            mean = np.clip(mean, low_b, high_b)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 2
            # ========================================================

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 3: Adaptation
            # ========================================================

            C_inv_sqrt = B @ np.diag(1 / D) @ B.T

            p_sigma = (
                (1 - c_sigma) * p_sigma
                + np.sqrt(c_sigma * (2 - c_sigma) * mu_eff)
                * (C_inv_sqrt @ ((mean - old_mean) / sigma))
            )

            sigma_exponent = (
                (c_sigma / d_sigma)
                * (np.linalg.norm(p_sigma) / expected_norm - 1)
            )

            sigma_exponent = np.clip(
                sigma_exponent,
                -50.0,
                50.0
            )

            sigma = sigma * np.exp(sigma_exponent)

            if not np.isfinite(sigma):
                sigma = self.sigma * (high_b - low_b)

            sigma = np.clip(
                sigma,
                1e-12,
                1e3 * (high_b - low_b)
            )

            norm_p_sigma = np.linalg.norm(p_sigma)

            h_sigma_condition = norm_p_sigma / np.sqrt(
                1 - (1 - c_sigma) ** (2 * (iteration + 1))
            ) < (1.4 + 2 / (dim + 1)) * expected_norm

            h_sigma = 1.0 if h_sigma_condition else 0.0

            p_c = (
                (1 - c_c) * p_c
                + h_sigma
                * np.sqrt(c_c * (2 - c_c) * mu_eff)
                * ((mean - old_mean) / sigma)
            )

            y = (selected_population - old_mean) / sigma

            rank_mu_update = np.zeros((dim, dim))

            for i in range(mu):
                rank_mu_update += weights[i] * np.outer(y[i], y[i])

            C = (
                (1 - c1 - c_mu) * C
                + c1 * np.outer(p_c, p_c)
                + c_mu * rank_mu_update
            )

            C = (C + C.T) / 2

            # Adaptive shrinkage toward isotropy based on disruption indicator f_disruption
            beta = float(self._beta_base + self._beta_k * f_disruption)
            if beta < 0.0:
                beta = 0.0
            if beta > self._beta_max:
                beta = float(self._beta_max)

            trace_C = np.trace(C)
            if np.isfinite(trace_C):
                isotropic = (trace_C / float(dim)) * np.eye(dim)
                C = (1.0 - beta) * C + beta * isotropic

            C = (C + C.T) / 2

            # Final covariance safeguard
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(C)):
                C = np.eye(dim)

            C = C + 1e-12 * np.eye(dim)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 3
            # ========================================================

            history["best_fitness"].append(best_fitness)
            history["sigma"].append(sigma)
            history["mean"].append(mean.copy())

            iteration += 1

        return best_solution, best_fitness
```

# ========================================================
# CMA-ES Variant: LLM-generated CMA-ES variant cycle 19 | Cycle 19
# ========================================================

## Metric:
Values represent error to the BBOB optimum. Lower is better.

## Algorithm Code:
```python
import numpy as np
import ioh


class LLM_CMAES:
    '''
    CMA-ES variant that injects a small fraction of heavy-tailed Student-t samples
    into the Gaussian offspring each generation. Injected Student-t directions are
    rescaled so their Euclidean norm matches the expected norm of a standard
    Gaussian draw (preserving directional heavy tails while limiting extreme radii).
    Additionally implements a stagnation-triggered temporary increase in the
    heavy-tail injection fraction; the magnitude of this boost is adaptive to the
    stagnation length (longer stagnation -> larger boost up to a cap).

    New in Cycle 10: Adaptive tail heaviness. The degrees-of-freedom (nu) of the
    Student-t used for injected samples is nu_base during normal progress and,
    when a stagnation-triggered boost is active, is reduced (heavier tails) by
    1 every K stagnation generations down to nu_min. This is applied only during
    the same boost window used by the adaptive alpha.

    Cycle 15 addition: Transient, stagnation-triggered population-size increase
    (lambda boost) that temporarily samples more offspring for T generations
    when stagnation is detected. The total evaluation budget is preserved by
    shortening the remaining number of generations proportionally so that no
    extra objective evaluations occur overall.

    Cycle 19 change: During stagnation-triggered boost windows, the isotropic
    Student-t injection is replaced by heavy-tailed sampling confined to a
    low-dimensional promising subspace spanned by the top-k eigenvectors of C
    (k = min(3, dim)). The sampled k-dimensional Student-t vectors are embedded
    into the full isotropic coordinate space (zero in orthogonal complement)
    and rescaled so their Euclidean norm equals the expected norm of a standard
    Gaussian draw. This concentrates heavy-tailed exploration in principal
    directions, aiming to preserve exploratory power while reducing disruptive
    isotropic perturbations to covariance estimation.
    '''
    def __init__(
            self,
            pop_size=20,
            num_iterations=100,
            sigma=0.5
        ):

        self.pop_size = pop_size
        self.num_iterations = num_iterations
        self.sigma = sigma

        # Hardcoded mixture parameters per EXPLAINABILITY_REPORT
        # Base injection fraction
        self._alpha_base = 0.10  # fraction of offspring to replace normally with Student-t

        # Adaptive tail-heaviness parameters (new: Cycle 10)
        # Base and minimum degrees of freedom for Student-t
        self._nu_base = 6      # moderate tail during normal progress
        self._nu_min = 2       # do not go below this (heavier tails)
        # Decrement nu by 1 every K stagnation generations during boost
        self._nu_k = 2

        # Stagnation-triggered adaptive injection parameters
        # The boost magnitude is now adaptive to how long stagnation has persisted:
        # alpha_boost = min(alpha_max, alpha_base + alpha_k * (stagnation_counter - S + 1))
        self._alpha_max = 0.30            # maximum allowed boost fraction
        self._alpha_k = 0.05              # increment per extra stagnation generation beyond threshold
        self._stagnation_threshold = 5    # S: consecutive generations without improvement to trigger boost
        self._alpha_boost_duration = 3    # T: number of generations to keep adaptive alpha during a boost

        # Cycle 15: transient population-size increase parameters
        # Multiply lambda by this factor during a boost window (e.g., 1.5x-2x suggested)
        self._lambda_boost_factor = 1.8

        self.variant_description = {
            "sampling": (
                "Modified: During stagnation-triggered boost windows, replace isotropic "
                "Student-t injection by heavy-tailed sampling restricted to a low-dimensional "
                "promising subspace. The subspace is formed by the top-k eigenvectors of the "
                "current covariance matrix C (k = min(3, dim)). Sample k-dimensional Student-t "
                "vectors, embed them into the full isotropic coordinate space (zeros in orthogonal "
                "complement), then rescale each injected vector so its Euclidean norm equals the "
                "expected norm of a standard Gaussian draw. Outside boost windows, use isotropic "
                "Student-t injection as before. All other mechanisms (adaptive alpha/nu, transient "
                "lambda boost, norm-rescaling) remain unchanged."
            ),
            "mean_update": "Unchanged from parent algorithm.",
            "adaptation": "Unchanged from parent algorithm."
        }

    def __call__(self, problem: ioh.problem.RealSingleObjective):

        dim = problem.meta_data.n_variables
        low_b, high_b = problem.bounds.lb[0], problem.bounds.ub[0]

        if self.pop_size is None:
            lambda_base = 4 + int(3 * np.log(dim))
        else:
            lambda_base = self.pop_size

        # Total evaluation budget preserved: baseline lambda_base * num_iterations
        total_budget = int(lambda_base * self.num_iterations)

        expected_norm = np.sqrt(dim) * (
            1 - 1 / (4 * dim) + 1 / (21 * dim ** 2)
        )

        mean = np.random.uniform(low_b, high_b, dim)
        sigma = self.sigma * (high_b - low_b)

        C = np.eye(dim)

        p_sigma = np.zeros(dim)
        p_c = np.zeros(dim)

        best_solution = None
        best_fitness = np.inf

        history = {
            "best_fitness": [],
            "sigma": [],
            "mean": [],
            "stagnation_counter": 0,
            "restart_count": 0
        }

        # Runtime counter for remaining boosted generations (shared for alpha and lambda boost)
        boost_remaining = 0
        # Store the adaptive alpha used during an ongoing boost
        boost_alpha_current = None
        # Store the transient lambda used during an ongoing boost
        lambda_boost_current = None

        # Evaluation-driven loop to preserve total evaluation budget while allowing
        # transient per-generation lambda increases.
        evals_done = 0
        iteration = 0

        # Main loop: continue while we have budget and have not exceeded nominal iterations.
        while evals_done < total_budget and iteration < self.num_iterations:

            # Numerical safeguard for covariance matrix
            C = (C + C.T) / 2

            if not np.all(np.isfinite(C)):
                C = np.eye(dim)

            C = C + 1e-12 * np.eye(dim)

            eigenvalues, B = np.linalg.eigh(C)
            eigenvalues = np.maximum(eigenvalues, 1e-20)

            D = np.sqrt(eigenvalues)
            BD = B @ np.diag(D)

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 1: Sampling (modified: adaptive alpha magnitude, adaptive nu, transient lambda increase)
            # ========================================================

            # If stagnation counter reaches threshold and no boost is active, start a boost
            if history["stagnation_counter"] >= self._stagnation_threshold and boost_remaining == 0:
                # compute adaptive boost magnitude based on how long we've been stuck
                extra = history["stagnation_counter"] - self._stagnation_threshold + 1
                adaptive_alpha = self._alpha_base + self._alpha_k * extra
                adaptive_alpha = min(self._alpha_max, adaptive_alpha)
                boost_alpha_current = adaptive_alpha
                # Start transient lambda increase as well
                lambda_boost_current = int(np.ceil(lambda_base * self._lambda_boost_factor))
                # Cap lambda_boost_current to remaining budget (at least 1)
                lambda_boost_current = max(1, min(lambda_boost_current, total_budget - evals_done))
                boost_remaining = self._alpha_boost_duration

            # Current heavy-tail injection fraction
            current_alpha = boost_alpha_current if boost_remaining > 0 and boost_alpha_current is not None else self._alpha_base

            # Determine current degrees of freedom for Student-t:
            if boost_remaining > 0 and boost_alpha_current is not None and history["stagnation_counter"] >= self._stagnation_threshold:
                extra = history["stagnation_counter"] - self._stagnation_threshold + 1
                decrements = extra // self._nu_k
                current_nu = max(self._nu_min, self._nu_base - int(decrements))
            else:
                current_nu = float(self._nu_base)

            # Determine number of offspring this generation while preserving total budget
            if boost_remaining > 0 and lambda_boost_current is not None:
                lambda_gen = lambda_boost_current
            else:
                lambda_gen = lambda_base

            # Ensure we do not exceed remaining budget
            remaining_budget = total_budget - evals_done
            if lambda_gen > remaining_budget:
                lambda_gen = max(1, int(remaining_budget))

            # Compute mu and recompute combination weights for current lambda_gen
            mu = max(1, lambda_gen // 2)
            weights = np.log(mu + 0.5) - np.log(np.arange(1, mu + 1))
            weights = weights / np.sum(weights)
            mu_eff = 1.0 / np.sum(weights ** 2)

            # Recompute strategy parameters depending on mu_eff and dim
            c_sigma = (mu_eff + 2) / (dim + mu_eff + 5)

            d_sigma = (
                1
                + 2 * max(0, np.sqrt((mu_eff - 1) / (dim + 1)) - 1)
                + c_sigma
            )

            c_c = (4 + mu_eff / dim) / (dim + 4 + 2 * mu_eff / dim)

            c1 = 2 / ((dim + 1.3) ** 2 + mu_eff)

            c_mu = min(
                1 - c1,
                2 * (mu_eff - 2 + 1 / mu_eff) / ((dim + 2) ** 2 + mu_eff)
            )

            # Standard Gaussian draws in isotropic coordinates
            z = np.random.randn(lambda_gen, dim)

            # Inject a fraction current_alpha of Student-t heavy-tailed draws
            if current_alpha > 0.0:
                n_heavy = int(np.ceil(current_alpha * lambda_gen))
                n_heavy = min(max(n_heavy, 0), lambda_gen)
                if n_heavy > 0:
                    heavy_idx = np.random.choice(lambda_gen, size=n_heavy, replace=False)
                    df = float(current_nu)
                    if df <= 0:
                        df = float(self._nu_min)

                    # If we are in a boost window, sample Student-t vectors confined to a low-dimensional
                    # promising subspace spanned by the top-k eigenvectors of C (Cycle 19 modification).
                    if boost_remaining > 0 and boost_alpha_current is not None and history["stagnation_counter"] >= self._stagnation_threshold:
                        k = min(3, dim)
                        # Identify indices of top-k eigenvectors (eigenvalues ascending -> take last k columns)
                        if k <= 0:
                            # fallback to isotropic sampling if degenerate
                            z_t = np.random.standard_t(df, size=(n_heavy, dim))
                            eps = 1e-12
                            norms = np.linalg.norm(z_t, axis=1)
                            scales = expected_norm / np.maximum(norms, eps)
                            z_t = z_t * scales[:, np.newaxis]
                            z[heavy_idx] = z_t
                        else:
                            # Top-k basis in isotropic z-coordinates corresponds to the last k columns of B
                            basis = B[:, -k:]  # dim x k
                            # We'll construct z_t_full with non-zero entries only in these k coordinates
                            # in the isotropic coordinate system (z-space). That corresponds to setting
                            # the components along those eigenvector coordinates.
                            # Find the indices in z-space corresponding to those eigenvectors: they are the
                            # last k coordinate axes in the eigenbasis ordering; to inject into z (which is in
                            # standard coordinates), we create full-dim vectors with non-zero entries only
                            # in the coordinates that align with these eigenvectors. Practically, we set
                            # z components in the eigenvector coordinate system: i.e., we map u_k (k-dim)
                            # into full-dim z by placing u_k into the corresponding eigen-coordinates.
                            # To do this, we create z_t_full = zeros(dim), then z_t_full = basis @ u_k
                            # expressed in the original coordinate system. However, z is defined in the
                            # isotropic coordinate system prior to multiplication by BD.T, so to keep
                            # consistent with original rescaling in z-space, we will set the components
                            # of z in the eigenbasis coordinates directly: that is, we compute embeddings
                            # by placing u_k into the positions corresponding to these eigen-coordinates.
                            # To achieve that, we compute the projection matrix from eigen-coordinates to
                            # the standard coordinate axes in z-space: it's simply the basis matrix.
                            # We therefore form full-dim z vectors via z_full = (basis) @ u_k,
                            # where u_k is k-dim Student-t. This yields z_full in standard coordinates.
                            z_t_full = np.zeros((n_heavy, dim))
                            for i in range(n_heavy):
                                u_k = np.random.standard_t(df, size=(k,))
                                # embed into full coordinates: basis @ u_k
                                z_full = basis @ u_k
                                z_t_full[i, :] = z_full
                            eps = 1e-12
                            norms = np.linalg.norm(z_t_full, axis=1)
                            scales = expected_norm / np.maximum(norms, eps)
                            z_t_full = z_t_full * scales[:, np.newaxis]
                            z[heavy_idx] = z_t_full
                    else:
                        # Standard isotropic Student-t injection (outside boost windows)
                        z_t = np.random.standard_t(df, size=(n_heavy, dim))
                        eps = 1e-12
                        norms = np.linalg.norm(z_t, axis=1)
                        scales = expected_norm / np.maximum(norms, eps)
                        z_t = z_t * scales[:, np.newaxis]
                        z[heavy_idx] = z_t

            # After using one boosted generation, decrement remaining counter
            if boost_remaining > 0:
                boost_remaining -= 1
                if boost_remaining == 0:
                    boost_alpha_current = None
                    lambda_boost_current = None

            # Transform into solution space using current covariance eigenbasis and scale
            population = mean + sigma * (z @ BD.T)

            # Numerical safeguard for candidate solutions
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(population)):
                population = np.nan_to_num(
                    population,
                    nan=0.0,
                    posinf=high_b,
                    neginf=low_b
                )

            population = np.clip(population, low_b, high_b)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 1
            # ========================================================

            # Evaluate population (must not exceed remaining budget)
            fitness = np.array([problem(ind) for ind in population])
            evals_done += lambda_gen

            sorted_indices = np.argsort(fitness)

            population = population[sorted_indices]
            z = z[sorted_indices]
            fitness = fitness[sorted_indices]

            previous_best_fitness = best_fitness

            if fitness[0] < best_fitness:
                best_fitness = fitness[0]
                best_solution = population[0].copy()
                history["stagnation_counter"] = 0
                # Cancel any ongoing boost upon improvement
                boost_remaining = 0
                boost_alpha_current = None
                lambda_boost_current = None
            else:
                history["stagnation_counter"] += 1

            selected_population = population[:mu]
            selected_z = z[:mu]

            old_mean = mean.copy()

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 2: Mean update
            # ========================================================

            mean = np.sum(
                weights[:, np.newaxis] * selected_population,
                axis=0
            )

            mean = np.clip(mean, low_b, high_b)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 2
            # ========================================================

            # ========================================================
            # LLM SECTION 3: Adaptation
            # ========================================================

            C_inv_sqrt = B @ np.diag(1 / D) @ B.T

            p_sigma = (
                (1 - c_sigma) * p_sigma
                + np.sqrt(c_sigma * (2 - c_sigma) * mu_eff)
                * (C_inv_sqrt @ ((mean - old_mean) / sigma))
            )

            sigma_exponent = (
                (c_sigma / d_sigma)
                * (np.linalg.norm(p_sigma) / expected_norm - 1)
            )

            sigma_exponent = np.clip(
                sigma_exponent,
                -50.0,
                50.0
            )

            sigma = sigma * np.exp(sigma_exponent)

            if not np.isfinite(sigma):
                sigma = self.sigma * (high_b - low_b)

            sigma = np.clip(
                sigma,
                1e-12,
                1e3 * (high_b - low_b)
            )

            norm_p_sigma = np.linalg.norm(p_sigma)

            h_sigma_condition = norm_p_sigma / np.sqrt(
                1 - (1 - c_sigma) ** (2 * (iteration + 1))
            ) < (1.4 + 2 / (dim + 1)) * expected_norm

            h_sigma = 1.0 if h_sigma_condition else 0.0

            p_c = (
                (1 - c_c) * p_c
                + h_sigma
                * np.sqrt(c_c * (2 - c_c) * mu_eff)
                * ((mean - old_mean) / sigma)
            )

            y = (selected_population - old_mean) / sigma

            rank_mu_update = np.zeros((dim, dim))

            for i in range(mu):
                rank_mu_update += weights[i] * np.outer(y[i], y[i])

            C = (
                (1 - c1 - c_mu) * C
                + c1 * np.outer(p_c, p_c)
                + c_mu * rank_mu_update
            )

            C = (C + C.T) / 2

            # Final covariance safeguard
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(C)):
                C = np.eye(dim)

            C = C + 1e-12 * np.eye(dim)

            # ========================================================
            # END LLM SECTION 3
            # ========================================================

            history["best_fitness"].append(best_fitness)
            history["sigma"].append(sigma)
            history["mean"].append(mean.copy())

            iteration += 1

        return best_solution, best_fitness
```
